  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理时变两模玻色系统的量子信息诊断时，需要针对每个具体模型重建并对角化约化密度矩阵，过程繁琐且缺乏通用性。特别是在宇宙学涨落和光学参量放大等非平稳过程中，需要一种标准化、高效的诊断工具来统一刻画系统的量子关联与混合度演化。

Method: 1. 将一般时变二次两模哈密顿量的状态动力学简化为关于λ_k的单一复Riccati方程；2. 证明约化密度矩阵谱完全由q_k=|λ_k|^2决定；3. 基于此构建纯度、线性熵、Rényi-2熵和von Neumann熵的显式模型无关公式；4. 在宇宙学扰动和啁啾光学参量放大器两个系统中进行验证。

Result: 在宇宙学背景下，该框架阐明了背景诱导的相位旋转和频率软化如何调控挤压增长与状态混合度；在光学领域，它捕捉了有限泵浦持续时间和频率啁啾导致的延迟起始、挤压积累抑制以及晚时熵饱和现象。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将模型依赖的驱动协议与通用信息论度量干净地分离，为一大类参数驱动二次玻色系统提供了一种高效、标准化的诊断工具。

Abstract: We present a compact and unified framework for quantum-information diagnostics of time-dependent two-mode bosonic systems based on the Bogoliubov ratio $λ_k(η) \equiv β_k(η)/α_k(η)$. For a general time-dependent quadratic two-mode Hamiltonian, the state dynamics is exactly reduced to a single complex Riccati equation for $λ_k$. Upon tracing out one partner mode, the spectrum of the one-mode reduced density matrix is determined entirely by the squared magnitude $q_k(η) = \vert{}λ_k(η)\vert{}^2$. Consequently, we could construct the explicit, model-independent formula for the for the reduced-state purity, linear entropy, Rényi-2 entropy, and von Neumann entropy without reconstructing and diagonalizing the reduced density matrix on a model-by-model basis using coupled squeezing parameters ($r_k, φ_k$). We demonstrate the utility of this framework in two distinct non-stationary setups: primordial cosmological perturbations and a chirped-pulse nondegenerate optical parametric amplifier. In the cosmological context, our formulation clarifies how background-induced phase rotation and frequency softening regulate squeezing growth and state mixedness; in the optical domain, it captures the delayed onset, suppression of squeezing accumulation, and late-time entropy saturation induced by finite pump duration and frequency chirp. By cleanly factorizing model-dependent driving protocols from universal information-theoretic metrics, this framework offers an efficient, standardized diagnostic tool for a broad class of parametrically driven quadratic bosonic systems.

</details>


### [97] [Neural Network Learning of One-Bit Protocols for Qubit Measurement Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23645)
*Josep Escrig,Mani Zartab,Giulio Gasbarri,Estel Ferrer,Ramon Muñoz-Tapia,Gael Sentís*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子比特制备-测量场景中，用1比特经典通信模拟量子测量统计的精度。通过神经网络发现，对称测量（如正多面体）可用1比特高精度近似，并推导出解析协议，在连续各向同性测量下达到精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典通信复杂度框架下，已知模拟任意量子比特态和测量需要2比特。但实际应用中测量族可能是受限的，是否可以用1比特实现高精度近似？本文旨在探索这一可能性。

Method: 使用神经网络程序学习1比特通信协议，分析不同测量族的模拟精度；通过分析网络学习到的模式，推导出适用于对称信息完备测量族的解析协议。

Result: 1比特可在特定测量族（尤其是对称均匀加权测量，如正多面体）下达到高平均精度；推导的解析协议在有限对称信息完备配置下极其精确，在连续各向同性测量极限下变为精确。

Conclusion: 受限测量族允许1比特经典通信的高精度近似，对称结构是关键因素；神经网络不仅可用于发现协议，还可用于推导可解释的解析解。

Abstract: Communication complexity provides a natural framework for quantifying the classical resources required to reproduce quantum statistics. In the qubit prepare-and-measure scenario, two classical bits have been shown to be necessary and sufficient to simulate arbitrary qubit states and arbi- trary quantum measurements exactly. However, this result does not exclude the possibility that restricted families of measurements may admit accurate 1-bit classical approximations. We use a neural network procedure to demonstrate that a single bit can achieve high average accuracy for specific measurement families. A performance analysis of our neural network reveals that symmet- ric measurements with uniformly weighted elements, such as those forming regular polyhedra, are particularly amenable to this restricted communication. By analyzing the patterns learned by the neural network, we derive an analytical protocol that is extremely accurate for finite information- ally complete symmetric configurations and becomes exact in the limit of a continuous isotropic measurement.

</details>


### [98] [Mitigation of Measurement-Induced State Transitions via a Fast-Load and Fast-Clear Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23681)
*Wei-En Lin,Li-Chieh Hsiao,Chen-Hsun Ma,Erh-Hsiang Yeh,Wei-Lun Peng,Hsi-Sheng Goan,Cen-Shawn Wu,Yueh-Nan Chen,Yung-Fu Chen,Chung-Ting Ke,Chii-Dong Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文系统研究了电荷敏感transmon量子比特中由偏移电荷$n_g$决定的测量诱导态跃迁（MIST）动力学，并提出了无需复杂波形或实时反馈的快速加载与清除脉冲方案来抑制MIST。通过主动栅极电压控制与快速反馈，实验精确映射了MIST概率与$n_g$及稳态谐振子光子数的关系，验证了三步脉冲方案能最小化整体读取误差，在大规模超导量子处理器中实现了读取信噪比与QND保持之间的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真度、快速量子比特读取是实现超导量子处理器的关键，但测量诱导态跃迁（MIST）从根本上限制了读取速度和保真度。对于transmon量子比特，MIST对其高能级的电荷色散极为敏感，因此亟需系统研究并开发实用的MIST抑制策略。

Method: 利用电荷敏感transmon架构，通过工程化快速加载与清除脉冲来抑制谐振子光子过冲；结合主动栅极电压控制和快速反馈，精确映射MIST概率随$n_g$和稳态光子数的变化；采用数值Floquet分支分析与三步脉冲方案进行验证和优化。

Result: 实验与数值分析结果高度吻合，证明三步脉冲方案能一致地最小化整体读取错误；$n_g$平均的总错误概率在读取和读取后阶段均得到有效降低。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用、无额外硬件开销的方法，在大规模超导量子处理器中实现了读取信噪比与QND保持之间的更优权衡。

Abstract: High-fidelity and rapid qubit readout is essential for superconducting quantum processors, typically realized through the quantum non-demolition (QND) dispersive interaction within a qubit-resonator architecture. However, the achievable readout speed and fidelity are fundamentally limited by measurement-induced state transitions (MIST). For a transmon qubit, MIST is highly sensitive to the offset charge $n_g$ due to the charge dispersion of its higher-lying energy levels. In this work, we systematically investigate $n_g$-dependent MIST dynamics governed by the diabaticity and symmetry of pulse shaping within a charge-sensitive transmon architecture. We engineer fast-load and fast-clear pulses that effectively suppress resonator photon overshoots, thereby demonstrating a highly practical strategy to mitigate MIST without requiring complex waveforms or real-time feedback. Utilizing active gate-voltage control and rapid feedback, the measurement-induced transition probability is precisely mapped against $n_g$ and the steady-state resonator photon number, exhibiting strong agreement with numerical Floquet branch analysis. Ultimately, we evaluate the $n_g$-averaged total error probabilities for both readout and post-readout stages, verifying that a straightforward three-step pulse scheme consistently minimizes overall readout errors. Within the framework of large-scale superconducting quantum processors, this practical, hardware-free approach inherently offers a better trade-off between the readout signal-to-noise ratio and QND preservation.

</details>


### [99] [Qutrit-Based Neural Quantum Kernels for Classification Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23683)
*Camila Cristiano-Romero,Pablo Rodriguez-Grasa,Mikel Sanz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将神经量子核（NQKs）从量子比特扩展至qutrit（三维量子系统）设置，系统研究了设计选择（特征数、qutrit数、核构建方式、SU(3)参数化）对分类性能的影响，发现qutrit NQKs在多数设置下优于对应QNN基线，性能提升依赖于数据集和参数化选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将NQK框架从qubit扩展到qudit（特别是qutrit），利用高维量子系统增加的局部自由度为多分类任务提供更丰富的数据嵌入，探索qudit量子模型在量子机器学习中的潜力。

Method: 将NQK扩展到qutrit（d=3）设置，系统研究关键设计选择：编码特征数量、qutrit数量、核构建方式（1-to-n与n-to-n）、以及SU(3)酉变换的参数化。在四个基准数据集上进行二分类和三分类任务实验。

Result: qutrit NQKs在几乎所有设置下优于对应QNN基线；增加特征预算和系统规模可提升性能，但增益可能饱和且依赖于数据集和参数化选择；不同SU(3)参数化显著影响优化行为和分类器性能。

Conclusion: qudit基量子模型不仅是qubit架构的简单推广，而且有望更好地利用复杂数据结构，在量子机器学习中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Neural quantum kernels (NQKs) construct quantum kernels by pretraining a quantum neural network (QNN) and subsequently reusing the trained circuit as a task-adapted embedding. Extending this framework to qudits, with local unitaries in $\mathrm{SU}(d)$, provides a natural route to richer data embeddings through the increased local degrees of freedom and a direct interface for multiclass classification via intrinsically multi-level quantum systems. In this work, focusing on qutrits ($d=3$), we extend NQKs to the qudit setting and perform a systematic study of key design choices, including the number of encoded features, the number of qutrits, the kernel construction (1-to-$n$ and $n$-to-$n$), and the parameterization of $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ unitaries. Across binary and three-class tasks on four benchmark datasets, qutrit NQKs improve over the corresponding QNN baselines in nearly all settings considered and can benefit from scaling both the feature budget and the system size, although the magnitude of these gains may saturate, is dataset-dependent, and depends on the chosen parameterization. In particular, an ablation over $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ parameterizations shows that the unitary representation can substantially impact both optimization behaviour and classifier performance. These findings highlight the potential of qudit-based quantum models not only as a straightforward generalization of qubit-based architectures, but also as a promising means to better exploit complex data structures in quantum machine learning.

</details>


### [100] [Can PCE solve the factorisation problem via optimisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23727)
*Fernando Alonso,Colomán Samprón,Jacobo Veiga,Andrés Gómez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE)算法应用于整数分解问题的可能性，利用其压缩能力大幅减少所需量子比特数，但仅作为初步可行性研究，未声称具有计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子分解方法（如Shor算法）需要大量量子比特，在当前量子硬件上难以实现。因此，研究者希望探索一种能减少量子比特需求的替代方法。

Method: 将整数分解问题转化为二元优化问题，并采用Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE)算法进行编码和求解。PCE算法的压缩能力可以显著降低所需的量子比特数量。

Result: 初步观察表明，该方法可能为量子计算中的分解问题提供一种替代视角，但未报告具体的性能优势或实验验证结果。

Conclusion: 该研究仅为探索性贡献，旨在研究量子算法和计算数论。PCE算法在分解问题上的可行性和局限性仍需进一步研究，目前不声称具有计算优势。

Abstract: The ongoing progress in quantum technologies has fueled a sustained exploration of their potential applications across various domains, particularly in computational problems that are considered intractable for classical systems. Among these problems, integer factorisation remains of special interest due to its relevance to widely used cryptographic schemes such as RSA. Among the different possibilities, one approach to factorisation is to convert the problem into a binary optimisation problem. However, current proposals usually need a large number of qubits that make them unfeasible within the current hardware. In this work, we investigate a possible adaptation of the Pauli Correlation Encoding (PCE) algorithm to the factorisation problem. Due to its compression capability, it can drastically reduce the number of needed qubits. The proposed approach explores how the structure and dynamics of the PCE framework may be employed to encode and analyze candidate factor relations within a quantum computational setting. Rather than presenting a replacement for established quantum factorisation methods, this study aims to provide a preliminary examination of the feasibility and limitations of the proposed adaptation. We discuss the algorithmic design, its conceptual relationship with existing quantum approaches, and the practical constraints associated with implementation on current or near-term quantum hardware. Initial observations suggest that the method may offer an alternative perspective for studying factorisation within the broader context of quantum computation, although no claim is made regarding computational advantage. These results are intended primarily as an exploratory contribution to ongoing research in quantum algorithms and computational number theory.

</details>


### [101] [The Physics of Unresolved Uncertainty: Quantum Mechanics as a Theory of Potentiality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23745)
*Lionel Martellini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将量子力学重新表述为一种未解决的不确定性理论，采用复值测度论作为前概率框架，在势能层面保持线性形式，而量子干涉等非经典特征源于非线性Born映射。该表述在经验上等价于标准量子力学，但明确揭示了通常隐含在希尔伯特空间形式中的前概率物理实在描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子力学的希尔伯特空间形式中隐含了前概率层面的物理实在描述，但缺乏显式表达。作者希望构建一个更清晰的复值测度论框架，将量子力学中的不确定性、干涉、测量等核心概念统一在势能（potentiality）层面进行解释。

Method: 使用复值测度论作为语言，将量子力学重新表述为一种前概率理论。在势能层面保持可加性、条件作用、独立性、混合态、转移核和时间可分割性的线性形式；通过非线性Born映射产生干涉等非经典特征。测量被描述为势能对实际化信息的贝叶斯型条件作用，非选择性测量则被视为相干势能被统计混合条件分支所替代。

Result: 成功构建了一个在经验上等价于标准量子力学的复值测度论框架，能够统一解释混合态、退相干、复合系统、纠缠和贝尔型关联等量子现象，并在势能层面赋予它们统一的解释。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持与标准量子力学经验等价的前提下，显式揭示了前概率层面的物理实在描述，为理解量子力学的基础问题提供了一个新的视角。

Abstract: We propose a reformulation of quantum mechanics as a theory of unresolved uncertainty. The framework is formulated in the language of complex-valued measure theory, regarded as a pre-probabilistic counterpart of ordinary probability theory. In this formulation, additivity, conditioning, independence, mixtures, transition kernels, and temporal divisibility retain natural linear forms at the potentiality level, while non-classical probability-level features such as interference arise from the nonlinear Born map. Measurement is described as Bayesian-type conditioning of potentialities on actualized information, and non-selective measurement as the replacement of coherent potentiality by statistical mixtures of conditional branches. Mixed states, decoherence, composite systems, entanglement, and Bell-type correlations are also given a unified potentiality-level interpretation. The resulting formulation is empirically equivalent to standard quantum mechanics, but it makes explicit a pre-probabilistic description of physical reality that is usually implicit in the Hilbert-space formalism.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum Separability in Polynomial Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23773)
*Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机多项式时间算法，用于解决量子可分离性问题，该算法对于任何固定常数间隙η>0（在欧几里得范数下度量距离）均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子可分离性问题是量子信息理论中的核心问题，判断一个二分密度矩阵是否可分离或与所有可分离态相距至少η，对于量子纠缠的检测与量化至关重要。

Method: 采用随机化多项式时间算法，在欧几里得范数下度量距离，针对固定常数间隙η>0进行求解。

Result: 给出了一个随机多项式时间算法，能够有效解决量子可分离性问题。

Conclusion: 该算法为量子可分离性问题提供了一种高效的计算方法，在固定精度下能在多项式时间内完成判定。

Abstract: The quantum separability problem asks whether a bipartite density matrix is separable or is $η$-far from every separable state. We give a randomized polynomial-time algorithm for this problem for every fixed constant gap $η>0$, when distance is measured in the Euclidean norm.

</details>


### [103] [Environment-assisted squeezing in a coherently driven non-Hermitian degenerate parametric oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23816)
*Muhdin Abdo Wodedo,Berihu Teklu,Konstantin G. Zloshchastiev,Jorge P. Zubelli,Tesfay Gebremariam Tesfahannes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种退化的参量振荡器，其中相干驱动的腔通过单端口镜耦合到宽带压缩库，并包含非厄米（增益-损耗不平衡）项。利用输入-输出理论推导了稳态正交方差、输出压缩谱和功率谱的闭合表达式，发现非厄米贡献能在特定参数窗口内将腔内正交噪声抑制到标准量子极限以下，并增强或锐化输出端的谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种环境辅助的非经典光产生途径，在存在损耗的非理想平台上实现强压缩，通过结合压缩库工程、相干驱动和非厄米增益-损耗不平衡来克服传统方法的局限性。

Method: 采用输入-输出理论，建立退化的参量振荡器模型——相干驱动腔通过单端口镜耦合到宽带压缩库，腔内包含非厄米（增益-损耗不平衡）动力学项。推导了稳态正交方差、输出压缩谱和功率谱的闭合表达式，将非厄米系数视为消除辅助自由度后的低阶有效漂移参数，分析限制在漂移矩阵稳定且压缩库扩散矩阵物理的稳定高斯区域内。

Result: 非厄米贡献在特定参数窗口内显著抑制了腔内正交噪声（低于标准量子极限），并在输出端锐化或放大谱压缩和功率谱特征。获得了压缩因子、相干驱动振幅和参量增益对性能影响的参数映射图。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一种环境辅助的方法——压缩库工程与相干驱动协同增强压缩。产生的参数映射图识别了可在实验上测试的参数窗口，为在腔QED和非线性光子学环境中结合压缩库、相干驱动和受控增益/损耗不平衡提供了指导，而非单一器件方案。

Abstract: Environment-assisted approaches to nonclassical light offer a practical path to strong squeezing in imperfect, lossy platforms. In this paper, we study a degenerate parametric oscillator in which a coherently driven cavity is coupled to a broadband squeezed reservoir via a single-port mirror. At the same time, the intracavity dynamics include non-Hermitian (gain-loss-imbalanced) terms. Within input--output theory, we obtain closed-form expressions for the steady-state quadrature variances, the output squeezing spectrum, and the power spectrum, and map their dependence on the reservoir squeeze factor, the coherent drive amplitude, and the parametric gain. We find that the non-Hermitian contributions open operating windows in which the intracavity quadrature noise is markedly suppressed below the standard quantum limit and, depending on the parameter set, either sharpen or amplify spectral squeezing and power-spectral features at the output. The non-Hermitian coefficients are treated as effective, low-order drift parameters that describe calibrated imbalance between engineered source and sink channels after auxiliary degrees of freedom have been eliminated. The analysis is restricted to the stable Gaussian regime in which the drift matrix is stable, and the squeezed-reservoir diffusion matrix remains physical. The results demonstrate an environment-assisted approach in which reservoir engineering and coherent driving work together to enhance squeezing. The resulting parameter maps identify experimentally testable windows, rather than a unique device prescription, for combining reservoir squeezing, coherent driving, and controlled gain/loss imbalance in cavity-QED and nonlinear photonic settings.

</details>


### [104] [Unified Framework for Bidirectional and Cyclic Teleportation under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23820)
*Soubhik De,Vedhanayagi R,Basherrudin Mahmud Ahmed A,Alok Sharan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一个12量子比特纠缠信道，能够同时支持任意三量子比特态的双向量子隐形传态和任意两量子比特态的循环量子隐形传态，并通过噪声分析验证了其在实际量子网络中的可扩展性和资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子隐形传态方案大多依赖特定于协议的纠缠资源，缺乏能够同时支持多种通信任务的通用量子信道，因此需要开发统一的、可扩展的通用纠缠信道框架。

Method: 利用一个12量子比特纠缠信道，通过局域贝尔态测量和单量子比特操作，实现了双向和循环量子隐形传态协议，并在振幅阻尼、相位阻尼、比特翻转、相位翻转和退极化噪声信道下分析了传态保真度。

Result: 双向协议在比特翻转噪声下对所有输入态和噪声强度保持完全保真；循环协议对环境扰动更敏感；协议内在效率为25%。

Conclusion: 该框架为实际噪声量子网络中的统一量子通信提供了一种可扩展且资源高效的方案。

Abstract: Quantum teleportation has evolved from single-qubit, unidirectional communication to multi-qubit and multidirectional protocols. However, most existing schemes rely on protocol-specific entangled resources, motivating the development of universal quantum channels capable of supporting multiple communication tasks simultaneously. In this work, we demonstrate that a single twelve-qubit entangled channel exhibits such versatility by enabling the bidirectional teleportation of arbitrary three-qubit states and the cyclic teleportation of arbitrary two-qubit states through local Bell-state measurements and single-qubit operations. Both protocols are further generalized to multi-qubit and multi-party configurations, establishing the scalability of the proposed framework. To assess its practical applicability, the protocols are analyzed under amplitude-damping, phase-damping, bit-flip, phase-flip, and depolarizing noise channels by plotting the teleportation fidelity as a function of both the input-state parameters and noise strength. The analysis reveals distinct noise sensitivities, with the bidirectional protocol remaining perfectly faithful under bit-flip noise for all input states and noise strengths, while the cyclic protocol is consistently more vulnerable to environmental disturbances. The proposed schemes achieve an intrinsic efficiency of $25\%$, which is compared with several existing protocols. The framework therefore provides a scalable and resource-efficient approach to unified quantum communication in realistic noisy quantum networks.

</details>


### [105] [Entanglement transitions and multifractality in monitored free-fermions with random long-range hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23871)
*Bo Fan,Antonio M. García-García*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维随机幂律跳跃非相互作用复费米子链在监测下的纠缠动力学。发现跳跃指数α决定纠缠熵定标行为：α≲1/2时趋于体积律；1<α≲3/2时存在测量诱导的相变(MIPT)，临界点EE对数定标；α>3/2时为面积律。临界点密度-密度关联函数呈现多重分形特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究长程跳跃（幂律跳跃）如何影响监测量子系统的纠缠动力学，区分超扩散经典跳跃与真正量子非局域性对纠缠的贡献。

Method: 理论分析一维随机幂律跳跃非相互作用复费米子链在连续监测下的稳态纠缠熵，通过改变跳跃指数α和监测强度，研究纠缠熵随系统大小的定标行为及密度-密度关联函数的多重分形特征。

Result: α≲1/2时纠缠熵趋于体积律（线性定标）；1<α≲3/2时存在依赖α的MIPT，临界点EE对数定标；α>3/2时为面积律。临界点密度-密度关联函数呈现多重分形特征。

Conclusion: 超扩散经典跳跃（小α）可导致体积律纠缠，与传统量子非局域性产生纠缠的机制不同，MIPT的临界性质依赖于跳跃指数α。

Abstract: We study the entanglement dynamics of a one-dimensional chain of monitored non-interacting complex fermions with random power-law hopping characterized by a decay exponent $α$. For $α\lesssim 1$, in stark contrast with the case of hopping to nearest neighbors, the scaling of the entanglement entropy (EE) of the steady state with system size $L$ is faster than logarithmic for any monitoring or disorder strength and it tends towards a linear (volume-law) scaling for sufficiently small $α\lesssim 1/2$. For $α> 3/2$, the EE is in the area-law phase, namely, no scaling with $L$, for any monitoring strength. For $1 < α\lesssim 3/2$, we identify an $α$-dependent measurement-induced phase transition (MIPT) at a critical value of the monitoring strength separating the mentioned area-law and sub-volume-law phases. At this critical point, the EE scales logarithmically with system size, and the density-density correlation function, closely related to the EE, exhibits multifractal features. These results highlight the importance of superdiffusive classical hopping in the entanglement dynamic of quantum many-body systems and also help differentiate its role with respect to conventional sources of entanglement such as genuine quantum non-locality.

</details>


### [106] [Strategies for quantum-enabled Bitcoin miners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23952)
*Zach Manson,Barry C. Sanders*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了配备专用量子计算机的矿工在竞争性比特币挖矿中如何影响51%攻击阈值，发现最优量子挖矿策略对该阈值影响微乎其微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估量子计算对基于工作量证明的加密货币（如比特币）的51%攻击阈值的实际威胁程度。

Method: 扩展了现有的比特币挖矿博弈论框架，计算收益矩阵，并确定两个非共谋、激进的量子矿工在经典比特币网络中的最优量子挖矿策略，允许矿工在测量未产生有效区块时重新启动搜索。

Result: 最优量子挖矿策略对51%攻击阈值的影响可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该结论对于评估基于工作量证明的加密货币（如比特币）所面临的量子挖矿威胁具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the impact that two miners equipped with quantum computers purpose-built for quantum Bitcoin mining will have on the 51% attack threshold of the Bitcoin network, given that the miners are playing a competitive game against each other to be the first to mine a block. We extend an existing game-theoretic framework for Bitcoin mining and compute the resultant payoff matrices. From these payoff matrices, we determine optimal quantum mining strategies for two non-colluding and aggressive quantum miners with multiple opportunities at finding a valid block in an otherwise classical Bitcoin network. We show that these optimal quantum mining strategies have a negligible effect on the 51% attack threshold. The novelty of our work is the inclusion of the Aggressive Quantum Mining Strategy and the realistic approach of allowing the quantum miners to restart their search if their measurements do not yield a valid block when determining the optimal quantum mining strategies. Our result is important for evaluating quantum-mining threats on cryptocurrencies based on Proof-of-Work, e.g. Bitcoin

</details>


### [107] [Scalable Photon-Mediated Two-Qubit Gates with Spectrally Noisy Quantum Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23959)
*Shreekanth S. Yuvarajan,W. A. Coish,David Hucul,Herbert F. Fotso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化腔辅助门协议（POCEG），通过脉冲序列和周期性调制耦合，克服量子比特间的光谱差异和噪声，在低阻尼和高阻尼腔条件下均实现了超过99.9%的量子态转移保真度，为固态系统两量子比特门达到容错量子计算阈值提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腔耦合两量子比特门在近共振或色散 regimes 中，当量子比特存在光谱噪声或频率失谐时，保真度会急剧下降，阻碍可扩展量子计算的实现。本文旨在克服这些光谱差异，实现高保真两量子比特门。

Method: 提出POCEG协议：对于低阻尼腔，在腔频率处对量子比特施加脉冲序列，同时周期性地调制量子比特与腔的耦合强度；对于高阻尼腔，在色散 regime 中工作，施加远离腔频率的脉冲。通过数值和解析方法验证协议的有效性。

Result: 在适中的脉冲间延迟下，原本因光谱失配而被强烈抑制的保真度可提升至超过99.9%。

Conclusion: POCEG协议能够使固态系统中的两量子比特门跨越容错量子计算所需的保真度阈值，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: When two quantum bits are coupled through a cavity, a two-qubit gate can be realized between them either in the near-resonant regime over a timescale established by the coupling strength of the qubits with the cavity, or in the dispersive regime, over a longer timescale established by the combination of the coupling strength and the frequency detuning between the qubits and the cavity. When the qubits are spectrally noisy or differently detuned from the cavity, the fidelity for the operation can be drastically reduced in either case, precluding scalable realizations. We introduce the protocol for optimal cavity-enabled gates (POCEG) that is shown, through reliable numerical and analytical solutions, to overcome spectral differences between quantum bits and to achieve high fidelity between disparate/noisy quantum emitters. Namely, for a cavity with low damping rate, we apply a sequence of pulses to the qubits at the frequency of the cavity while periodically modulating the coupling of the qubits to the cavity. Alternatively, in the case of a large damping rate, we operate in the dispersive regime and overcome spectral disparities by applying the pulses at a frequency far-detuned from the cavity. In both instances, we find for the quantum state transfer between the two qubits that, with a modest inter-pulse delay, the fidelity that would otherwise be strongly suppressed by the spectral mismatch of the qubits can be increased beyond 99.9%. These protocols have the capacity to bring two-qubit gates between solid state systems across the threshold required for fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [108] [Non-Hermitian-enhanced quantum sensing in an optical interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23978)
*X. J. Huang,Lei Xiao,Bingzi Huo,X. X. Yi,Peng Xue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在实验上利用非厄米可观测量，在线性光学平台上实现了量子参数估计，观察到相比最优厄米可观测量更低的误差传播方差，并通过经典Fisher信息分析表明该优势与标准量子计量学极限一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子参数估计的精度受限于基于厄米测量框架的量子Cramér-Rao界，而近期非厄米系统的研究为增强参数估计灵敏度提供了新可能，本文旨在实验验证非厄米可观测量在量子参数估计中的可行性及其优势。

Method: 在Sagnac干涉仪上，将参数编码于单光子探针态，通过重构干涉条纹中非厄米可观测量复期望值进行读出；利用振幅阻尼噪声测试鲁棒性，并通过正定算符值测度（POVM）和经典Fisher信息（CFI）分析其物理意义。

Result: 实验观察到非厄米可观测量相比最优厄米可观测量具有更低的误差传播方差，且该优势在振幅阻尼噪声下仍然保持；CFI分析表明，当包含所有输出端口时，该优势与标准量子计量学极限一致。

Conclusion: 实验实现了非厄米可观测量的量子参数估计读出，阐明了其在量子传感中的操作意义，为非厄米系统在量子计量学中的应用提供了实验路径。

Abstract: The precision of quantum parameter estimation is traditionally constrained by the quantum Cramér-Rao bound, which is based on the Hermitian measurement framework. Recent studies of non-Hermitian systems have suggested new possibilities for enhancing parameter-estimation sensitivity. Here, we experimentally realize quantum parameter estimation using a non-Hermitian observable on a linear optical platform. The parameter is encoded in single-photon probe states and read out with a Sagnac interferometer, which allows us to reconstruct the complex expectation value of the implemented non-Hermitian observable from interference fringes. We observe a reduced error-propagation variance compared with the optimal Hermitian observable for the same probe-state model. This advantage remains visible under amplitude-damping noise. We further analyze the complete optical measurement as a physical positive-operator-valued measure (POVM) and show, through the corresponding classical Fisher information (CFI), that the observed non-Hermitian advantage is consistent with the standard quantum metrological limit when all output ports are included. Our results provide an experimental route to non-Hermitian observable readout and clarify its operational meaning in quantum sensing.

</details>


### [109] [Scaling a CUDA-Q GQE + QSCI pipeline to 40 qubits for EUV photoresist chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23988)
*Karim Elgammal,Marc Maußner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并扩展了CUDA-Q原生管道，将生成式量子特征求解器(GQE)与量子选择组态相互作用(QSCI)耦合，应用于14至44量子比特活性空间中的单烷基锡氧羟化物EUV光刻胶化学。使用GPT-2策略发射UCCSD算符序列，采样比特串作为行列式进行经典对角化，并通过跨电路广义特征值精炼使每个GQE+QSCI能量成为变分上界。在14-40量子比特每级均设有精确CASCI/FCI参考（最大1.66亿行列式）。管道在30量子比特甲基锡三羟化物和32量子比特SnO上达到化学精度（<1.6 mHa）。电路深度而非训练长度是关键扩展杠杆；精炼子空间随算符数近线性增长，但仅占行列式空间的极小比例（32量子比特SnO级为0.017%）。在54量子比特IQM Emerald处理器上运行，14量子比特SnO达+0.330 mHa，22量子比特工业正丁基锡配体达+3.92 mHa（活性空间相关性81%）。甲基抗蚀剂中，电离使经典UCCSD(T) Sn-C键解离能从72.6骤降至21.2 kcal/mol，即曝光翻转溶解性的开关。40量子比特结果受支持限制为22.8 mHa；经典子空间扩展可达32-40量子比特空间而无需量子采样器，因此该边界已被绘制但未被攻克。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展量子计算在化学模拟中的可及活性空间尺寸，解决EUV光刻胶中单烷基锡氧羟化物这类实际工业化学体系的准确能量计算问题，验证混合量子-经典管道在大规模量子比特（14-44量子比特）上的化学精度与可扩展性。

Method: 采用CUDA-Q原生管道，将生成式量子特征求解器(GQE)与量子选择组态相互作用(QSCI)耦合。使用GPT-2策略模型生成UCCSD算符序列，对采样比特串进行经典行列式对角化，引入跨电路广义特征值精炼确保变分上界性质。在54量子比特IQM Emerald处理器上运行，结合深度截断训练电路与每电路读出自校准，以及经典组态恢复技术。

Result: 在30量子比特甲基锡三羟化物和32量子比特SnO上达到化学精度（<1.6 mHa）。精炼子空间随算符数近线性增长，在32量子比特SnO级仅占行列式空间的0.017%。硬件实验结果：14量子比特SnO达+0.330 mHa，22量子比特正丁基锡配体达+3.92 mHa（活性空间相关性81%）。甲基抗蚀剂的UCCSD(T) Sn-C键解离能从72.6降至21.2 kcal/mol。40量子比特结果为22.8 mHa。

Conclusion: GQE+QSCI管道在最高30-32量子比特可达化学精度，电路深度是扩展的关键因素而非训练长度。硬件运行受限于当前量子器件保真度，40量子比特的全训练ansatz有待硬件改进。经典子空间扩展可达32-40量子比特空间而无需量子采样器，该边界已被绘制但尚未被量子计算超越。

Abstract: We scale a CUDA-Q-native pipeline coupling a generative quantum eigensolver (GQE) to quantum-selected configuration interaction (QSCI) across active spaces of 14 to 44 qubits, applied to the extreme-ultraviolet (EUV) photoresist chemistry of monoalkyltin oxo-hydroxides. A GPT-2 policy emits UCCSD operator sequences; sampled bitstrings become determinants, diagonalised classically, and a cross-circuit generalised-eigenvalue refinement makes every reported GQE+QSCI energy a variational upper bound. Every rung from 14 to 40 qubits carries an exact CASCI or FCI reference, up to 166 million determinants for SnO at 32 qubits. The pipeline is chemically accurate, below 1.6 mHa, through 30 qubits on methyltin trihydroxide and through 32 on SnO, on the best seed at the top rungs. Circuit depth rather than training length is the scaling lever; the refined subspace grows near-linearly with the operator count while staying a vanishing fraction of the determinant space, 0.017% at the 32-qubit SnO rung. It also runs on the 54-qubit IQM Emerald processor, at the shallow depths its routed two-qubit gates allow, reaching +0.330 mHa for SnO at 14 qubits from a CCSD-amplitude-ordered pool prefix and +3.92 mHa for the industrial n-butyltin ligand at 22 qubits from depth-truncated trained circuits under per-circuit readout self-calibration, 81% of the active-space correlation; classical configuration recovery on those counts tightens the 22-qubit result to +0.18 to 0.21 mHa. For the methyl resist, ionisation collapses the classical UCCSD(T) Sn-C bond dissociation energy from 72.6 to 21.2 kcal/mol, the switch that flips solubility on exposure. Against that, the 40-qubit result is support-limited at 22.8 mHa, the full trained ansatz on hardware awaits better fidelities, and classical subspace expansion reaches the 32 to 40-qubit spaces with no quantum sampler, so that boundary is mapped, not beaten.

</details>


### [110] [DeComp2: Description Complexity aware Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23990)
*Aritra Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子编译器应同时优化电路复杂度（𝒞_circ）和描述复杂度（𝒞_Kol），将目标从执行成本扩展到生命周期复杂度，通过最小化总成本 𝒞_tot = α𝒞_circ + β𝒞_Kol 来保留层次化结构，并在 SU(2) 上通过 HT 枚举验证了该方法能发现纯电路优化器忽略的编译选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子编译器仅优化执行代理指标（门数、深度、保真度），将编译电路视为成本单位，混淆了运行时执行成本与描述程序所需的信息成本。展开循环程序虽不改变运行时成本，但会抹除层次化结构，阻碍下游优化和复用。

Method: 提出将编译器目标从执行复杂度提升为生命周期复杂度，引入 Kolmogorov 描述复杂度 𝒞_Kol 与电路复杂度 𝒞_circ 配对，最小化加权总成本 𝒞_tot = α𝒞_circ + β𝒞_Kol。在 SU(2) 上通过穷举 HT 枚举，使用基于压缩的代理来上界 𝒞_Kol，并在 Haar 代表性目标网格上验证。

Result: 两个代理与 𝒞_circ 正相关，但在非平凡比例的目标点上打破其秩序，排除了严格的函数依赖关系。在校准权重下，联合成本为少量操作上有意义的目标选择了既非最短也非最可压缩的 ε-球内 HT 字符串，展示了纯电路优化器忽略的编译选择。

Conclusion: 𝒞_Kol 可作为层次化中间表示编译器的主动优化信号，该框架类似于量子复杂度第二定律中加法位置熵与动能熵的镜像，可理解为最小描述长度正则化器。

Abstract: Quantum compilers optimize execution-only proxies such as gate count, depth, and fidelity, treating the compiled circuit as the unit of cost. This conflates two distinct resources, how much the substrate has to do at run time, and how much has to be said to describe what to do. Unrolling a looped program leaves run-time cost unchanged while erasing the hierarchical structure on which downstream optimization and reuse rely.
  We propose lifting the compiler objective from execution to life cycle complexity by pairing the circuit complexity $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{circ}}$ with a Kolmogorov-style description complexity $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Kol}}$ of the emitted circuit, and minimizing the additive total $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{tot}} = α\,\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{circ}} + β\,\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Kol}}$. The mirrors the additive positional and kinetic entropy of the second law of quantum complexity, and reads as a minimum-description-length regularizer over the otherwise degenerate set of $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{circ}}$-minimizers.
  We instantiate the objective on $SU(2)$ through exhaustive HT-enumeration with compression-based surrogates upper-bounding $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Kol}}$. Across a Haar-representative target grid the two surrogates correlate positively with $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{circ}}$ yet break its rank order on a non-trivial fraction of points, ruling out a tight functional dependence. With calibrated weights the joint cost selects, for a small but operationally meaningful fraction of targets, a candidate that is neither the shortest nor the most compressible HT-string in the $\varepsilon$-ball, exhibiting the compilation choices a $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{circ}}$-only optimizer discards and motivating $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Kol}}$ as an active optimization signal for hierarchical intermediate representation for compiler.

</details>


### [111] [Recoverable Quantum Computation: An Information-Centric Paradigm for Quantum Computing with Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23996)
*Shengwang Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出可恢复量子计算（RQC）范式，强调从噪声量子输出中提取计算信息而非完整保护量子态，以在错误存在下仍保持量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模容错量子计算机因量子纠错巨大开销而进展缓慢，促使研究者思考：在完全容错硬件可用之前，量子计算是否就无法发挥实用价值？本文旨在填补噪声量子处理器与容错量子计算机之间的中间态研究空白。

Method: 提出RQC框架，核心概念为可恢复性（recoverability），即能从噪声输出中提取目标计算信息且开销足以维持量子优势。引入恢复开销（recovery overhead）和可恢复性效率（recoverability efficiency）作为实用度量。通过IBM量子硬件上的量子傅里叶变换周期估计和量子机器学习概念示例进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，尽管存在显著物理错误，有用的计算信息仍可保持可恢复性。初步建立了量子应用按预期可恢复性的分类体系，并提出了通往可恢复性预测理论的研究路线图。

Conclusion: RQC并非容错量子计算的替代方案，而是一种互补范式，用于理解和评估从当今噪声量子处理器到未来容错量子计算机之间广泛中间状态下的有用量子计算。

Abstract: Quantum computing promises transformative advances in computation, communication, sensing, and machine learning. Yet the realization of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computers remains hindered by the enormous overhead required for quantum error correction. This challenge raises a fundamental question: Must useful quantum computing wait until fully fault-tolerant quantum hardware becomes available? In this Perspective, we propose Recoverable Quantum Computation (RQC), an information-centric paradigm for quantum computing with errors. Rather than requiring faithful preservation of the complete quantum state, RQC focuses on preserving the computational information required to accomplish a given task. A quantum computation is considered recoverable if the desired computational information can be extracted from noisy quantum outputs with an overhead that preserves quantum advantage relative to the best known classical method. We introduce recoverability as an operational principle for evaluating noisy quantum computations and propose practical metrics based on recovery overhead and recoverability efficiency. We illustrate the framework using quantum Fourier transform period estimation on IBM quantum hardware and a conceptual example from quantum machine learning, demonstrating that useful computational information may remain recoverable despite significant physical errors. Building on these examples, we propose a preliminary classification of quantum applications according to their expected recoverability and outline a research roadmap toward a predictive theory of recoverability. RQC is intended not as an alternative to fault-tolerant quantum computing, but as a complementary paradigm for understanding and evaluating useful quantum computation in the broad intermediate regime between today's noisy quantum processors and tomorrow's fault-tolerant quantum computers.

</details>


### [112] [Scalable Quantum Machine Learning: Trainability, Expressivity and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24014)
*Iordanis Kerenidis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于k粒子费米子架构的幺正砖墙（unitary brick-wall）参数化量子电路，通过可调粒子数k在经典模拟难度与训练成本之间取得平衡，实现了可训练性、表达性和效率的三重优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展参数化量子电路面临三大障碍：梯度消失（贫瘠高原）、缺乏经典难度的可证明保证、以及每个梯度步所需电路评估次数过高。现有方法难以同时在可训练性、表达性和效率上取得突破。

Method: 提出幺正砖墙架构：基于k粒子费米子体系，采用可重构分束器门与单量子位相位门交错排列，并结合非高斯魔法态编码。粒子数k作为可调参数，用于在经典模拟难度与训练成本之间进行权衡。还提出了幺正蝴蝶变体以支持全连接硬件拓扑。

Result: 可训练性：动力学李代数为𝔲(n)，直接参数化U(n)群，可实现Haar初始化；双体关联子读出梯度的方差为Θ(k²/n⁵)。表达性：经典硬度由k控制，采样和双体期望值的最优经典算法运行时间为2^{Θ(k)}poly(n)，最坏情况#P-hard在k=n^ε时成立，平均情况硬度在k=Θ(n)时成立。效率：多层并行参数移位规则仅需k(8n+4)次电路评估即可计算所有O(n²)个梯度，相较标准参数移位规则的3n²次评估降低了3n/(8k)倍。

Conclusion: 幺正砖墙架构通过粒子数k这一可调参数，系统性地解决了可扩展参数化量子电路的三大障碍，在可训练性、表达性和效率之间实现了可量化的权衡，为设计实用的大规模量子机器学习电路提供了新范式。

Abstract: Designing scalable parameterized quantum circuits for machine learning faces three fundamental obstacles: barren plateaus that prevent gradient-based training, the absence of provable guarantees that the learned function class is classically hard, and prohibitive circuit evaluations per gradient step. We propose the unitary brick-wall: a $k$-particle fermionic architecture for nearest-neighbor hardware, combining Reconfigurable Beam Splitter gates with interleaved single-qubit phase gates and a non-Gaussian magic-state encoding, where the particle number $k$ is a tunable dial trading classical simulation hardness against training cost.
  Trainable. The brick-wall has dynamical Lie algebra $\mathfrak{u}(n)$ and directly parametrizes $U(n)$ via Givens rotations, enabling Haar initialization. Two-body correlator readouts achieve gradient variance $Θ(k^2/n^5)$.
  Expressive. Classical hardness is controlled by the particle number $k$: best-known classical algorithms for sampling and for two-body expectation values run in time $2^{Θ(k)}\mathrm{poly}(n)$, worst-case #P-hardness holds at $k = n^ε$, and average-case hardness applies at $k = Θ(n)$.
  Efficient. A multi-layer parallel parameter-shift rule computes all $O(n^2)$ gradients from $k(8n+4)$ circuit evaluations per gradient step, a factor $3n/(8k)$ reduction over the $3n^2$ evaluations required by the standard parameter-shift rule.
  The unitary butterfly variant targets all-to-all hardware, with depth $2\log n$ and $n\log n$ parameters. It achieves similar hardness guarantees at $8k\log n$ evaluations per gradient step, the same factor $3n/(8k)$ reduction. Its trainability is established at two levels: the absence of exponential barren plateaus is unconditional, whereas the sharp $Θ(k^2/n^5)$ rate holds under a two-particle approximate-2-design conjecture.

</details>


### [113] [Pound-Drever-Hall Feedforward for Trapped-Ion Optical Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24050)
*Jia-Yang Gao,Jianwei Lee,Morteza Ahmadi,Manas Mukherjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光纤电光调制器(EOM)和电前馈伺服系统的主动相位噪声稳定方案，用于抑制1762 nm钡离子光学量子比特激光器的伺服凸起相位噪声。通过PDH前馈方法，在凸起峰值频率处实现了15 dB的噪声抑制，有效提升了量子比特的相干时间，且该方法可轻松适配其他光学量子比特系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光相位噪声是囚禁离子量子系统中限制门保真度和相干时间的关键因素之一。当离子量子比特的拉比频率靠近伺服凸起相位噪声频率时，驱动激光会成为保真度和相干时间的瓶颈。以往通过选择拉比频率避开伺服凸起区域来缓解该问题，但这限制了门速度的可用范围并可能制约可实现的保真度。因此，需要一种既能抑制伺服凸起相位噪声又不限制门速度范围的方案。

Method: 开发了一套针对1762 nm钡离子光学量子比特激光器的主动相位噪声稳定系统，采用光纤电光调制器(EOM)结合电前馈伺服。基于PDH前馈方法，利用延迟自外差干涉法(DSHI)分析激光相位噪声，并通过在光学量子比特上测试稳定化激光来验证效果。

Result: 该PDH前馈方法在锁定系统的伺服凸起峰值频率附近将相位噪声抑制了15 dB。在光学量子比特上的测试表明，即使拉比频率位于伺服凸起带宽内，稳定化激光器的相干时间也获得了明显改善，通过拉比振荡的振幅衰减测量得到了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动相位噪声稳定技术能够有效抑制伺服凸起相位噪声，提升量子比特相干时间，且无需牺牲门速度的可用范围。该方法可经最小修改即可适配其他光学量子比特系统，具有较强的通用性。

Abstract: Laser phase noise is one of the limiting factors that dictate gate fidelities and coherence times in trapped-ion quantum systems. Previous studies have reported that when the Rabi frequency of an ion qubit is close to the servo-bump phase-noise frequency, the driving laser limits the fidelity and coherence time. This issue has typically been mitigated by choosing a Rabi frequency outside the servo-bump region. However, this constrains the usable range of gate speeds and can limit the achievable fidelity. To address this issue, we developed an active phase-noise stabilization system for a barium-ion optical-qubit laser at 1762 nm, employing a fiber electro-optic modulator (EOM) with an electrical feedforward servo. Our results demonstrate that this setup, based on the Pound-Drever-Hall (PDH) feedforward method, can suppress servo-bump phase noise by 15 dB near the bump peak frequency in our locking system. The laser phase noise is analyzed using delayed self-heterodyne interferometry (DSHI). We further tested the stabilized laser on an optical qubit and observed a clear improvement in coherence time based on the measured amplitude decay of Rabi oscillations, even when the Rabi frequency lies within the servo-bump bandwidth. This technique can be readily adapted to other optical qubits with minimal modifications.

</details>


### [114] [Variational Quantum Conditional Boltzmann Machines for Time-Series Forecasting: Architectures, Symmetric Hyperparameter Evaluation, and a Nonlinear Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24065)
*Gerhard Hellstern,Danyal Maheshwari,Martin Zaefferer,Martin Braun,Tanja Döhler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种条件能量基预测架构（经典CRBM、混合QCRBM、全量子QQRBM和特征量子QFeatureQRBM），在对称超参数优化下进行13项结构化实验，发现无系统性量子优势证据：全量子模型显著更差，混合模型与经典基线无统计差异，且统计功效分析表明当前样本量(n=12)仅能检测中到大效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对经典与量子条件能量基模型在对称超参数优化下的公平比较，且未系统评估量子优势是否存在。

Method: 开发四种架构并进行完整条件分布、对比散度梯度和混合训练推导；采用对称超参数网格搜索；在GP金融数据和NARMA-10基准上进行13项结构化实验；进行统计功效分析和等参数预算比较。

Result: 无系统性量子优势证据；全量子QQRBM和QFeatureQRBM显著更差；混合QCRBM与经典CRBM无统计显著差异；等预算比较中经典CRBM在四个预算中的三个最低，且CRBM与QCRBM间无显著差异。

Conclusion: 在当前样本量下未发现量子优势，但小效应量优势无法排除；需更大样本量的研究以检测潜在的小量子优势。

Abstract: In this study, we developed and evaluated four conditional energy-based forecasting architectures: a classical Gaussian-Bernoulli CRBM, a hybrid quantum-classical QCRBM, a full-register QQRBM, and a lag-feature QFeatureQRBM with complete derivations of their conditional distributions, Contrastive-Divergence gradients, and hybrid training, bridging the energy-based formulation and the implementation-level quantum computation. Unlike prior comparisons, our evaluation enforces symmetric hyperparameter optimisation: classical and quantum-specific hyperparameters receive an equally thorough grid search across thirteen structured experiments. We test on two data classes, a Gaussian-process dataset (GP) generated with real financial data and the input-driven NARMA-10 nonlinear benchmark. Across both regimes we find no systematic evidence of a quantum advantage at the available sample size: no quantum architecture improves on the best classical baseline. The fully quantum QQRBM and QFeatureQRBM are significantly worse, whereas the hybrid QCRBM is statistically indistinguishable from the strongest classical CRBM on both datasets. A power analysis bounds this null result: at n = 12 only medium-to-large effects are detectable, so small advantages cannot be excluded. An iso-parameter (matched-budget) comparison reaches the same conclusion: the classical CRBM is lowest at three of the four budgets and no CRBM-vs-QCRBM difference is significant at any budget.

</details>


### [115] [Simultaneous estimation of relative phase and coherence in astronomical interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24105)
*Yawen Tang,Wei Ye,Lu Qin,Jinxin Li,Xinxin Wang,Zunlue Zhu,Shoukang Chang,Shao-Ming Fei,Xingdong Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了天文干涉测量中复相干度（模量和相对相位）的联合估计问题，比较了直接干涉测量方案和连续变量量子隐形传态方案的性能。结果表明：直接干涉测量在联合估计中具有更低的量子Cramér-Rao下界；零差检测在大平均光子数区域近似最优；短基线时直接干涉测量精度更优，长基线时连续变量量子隐形传态方案更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文干涉测量中复相干度的模量和相对相位需要联合估计，而非独立单参数估计，因此需要研究这两种参数的同时估计问题，并比较不同方案的估计性能。

Method: 基于量子Cramér-Rao下界和经典Cramér-Rao下界，分析直接干涉测量方案与连续变量量子隐形传态方案在复相干度联合估计中的极限精度，并考虑高斯测量（如零差检测）的最优性以及传输损耗的影响。

Result: (1) 直接干涉测量方案的量子Cramér-Rao下界更低；(2) 零差检测在大平均光子数区域能饱和量子Cramér-Rao下界，是近最优测量方案；(3) 短基线时直接干涉测量精度更优，长基线时连续变量量子隐形传态方案精度更优。

Conclusion: 直接干涉测量在短基线联合估计中更优，而连续变量量子隐形传态在长基线场景中更具潜力，两者各有适用场景。

Abstract: Astronomical interferometry is a cornerstone technique for high-resolution stellar imaging and observational astrophysics, extracting spatial information from the coherence of light collected by separated telescopes. Since the degree of coherence is complex, a genuine imaging task requires the joint recovery of the modulus and the relative phase, instead of independent singleparameter estimations. We investigate the simultaneous estimation of both parameters based on direct interferometry scheme and continuou-svariable quantum teleportation scheme. We find that in simultaneous estimation the direct interferometry scheme consistently yields a lower quantum Cramér-Rao bound, demonstrating its superiority over the continuous-variable quantum teleportation scheme. Furthermore, we establish the conditions under which the classical Cramér-Rao bound for Gaussian measurements saturates the quantum Cramér-Rao bound, identifying heterodyne detection as a near-optimal measurement scheme in the large mean photon number regime. An analysis of transmission loss reveals that the direct interferometry scheme yields superior precision in the short-baseline regime, whereas the continuous-variable quantum teleportation scheme outperforms it at longer baselines.

</details>


### [116] [High-order quantum correlations in nonlinear waveguide quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24106)
*I Gusti Ngurah Yudi Handayana,Ya-Tang Yu,C. -Y. Lee,K. -T. Lin,G. -D. Lin,H. H. Jen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了耦合到非线性一维波导的量子发射器阵列，通过参量驱动产生量子关联。结果表明，通过调节粒子间距和压缩参数，可以空间上控制二阶和三阶连通量子关联，在稳态下二阶关联表现出符号变化和非单调行为，而三阶关联则被显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用非线性波导量子电动力学（QED）系统中参量增益累积与波导介导相互作用的结合，实现量子态的工程化构建，特别是探究量子关联的空间结构及其可调控性。

Method: 采用有效的非线性波导QED主方程，分析二阶和三阶连通关联作为压缩参数和粒子间距的函数；研究了双发射器和多发射器阵列的瞬态与稳态行为。

Result: 双发射器下，压缩积累驱动激发增长并产生非零二阶连通关联；多发射器中，调谐粒子间距可使主导的二阶关联在体区域和边界区域之间切换，并能空间控制三阶连通关联；稳态下二阶关联呈现符号变化和非单调行为，三阶关联随压缩参数增强而显著增加。

Conclusion: 非线性波导QED可作为可调谐平台用于空间控制连通量子关联，为开放量子光学阵列中的关联工程提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Quantum emitters coupled to nonlinear one-dimensional waveguides provide a route for quantum-state engineering by utilizing parametric gain accumulation along with modified waveguide-mediated interactions. Since the accumulated squeezing depends on propagation distance, different emitter separations can experience different effective gain, making the spatial structure of connected quantum correlations a central feature of the dynamics. Here we investigate the transient and steady-state connected correlations of emitter arrays coupled to a parametrically driven waveguide. Using an effective master equation for nonlinear waveguide QED, we analyze second- and third-order connected correlations as functions of the squeezing parameter and interparticle distance. In the two-emitter limit, accumulated squeezing drives excitation buildup and generates a nonzero connected second-order correlation. For many-emitter arrays, tuning the interparticle distance switches the dominant local second-order correlation between the bulk and boundary regions, and enables an analogous spatial control of genuine third-order connected correlations. In the steady state, the averaged second-order correlation exhibits sign-changing and nonmonotonic behavior in the squeezing--interparticle distance parameter space, whereas the third-order connected correlation is strongly enhanced by increasing the squeezing parameter. These results identify nonlinear waveguide QED as a tunable platform for spatially controlling connected quantum correlations and provide insights into correlation engineering in open quantum optical arrays. We further show that this local contrast remains visible in both odd and even number of atomic arrays.

</details>


### [117] [Quantum key distribution over a 2 km free-space channel with a high secure key rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24118)
*Kyungdeuk Park,Dongkyu Kim,Dong-Gil Im,Yonggi Jo,Jisu Kim,Jonguk Choi,Yong Sup Ihn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了在2公里户外信道上、100 MHz重复频率下工作的自由空间诱骗态BB84 QKD系统，通过基于快速转向镜和位置敏感探测器的主动光束漂移校正，在约3.3%的量子误码率下实现了164.8 kbps的安全密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间QKD具有移动性和部署灵活性等优势，但面临多光子漏洞、空间模式失配和大气湍流引起的光束波动等实际挑战，需要开发能够在真实湍流环境中实现高速、长距离量子通信的实用方案。

Method: 采用诱骗态BB84协议，以100 MHz重复频率和2.5 ns脉冲宽度在2公里户外信道进行实验；使用基于快速转向镜和位置敏感探测器的主动光束漂移校正系统来补偿大气湍流影响。

Result: 在量子误码率约3.3%的条件下，实现了164.8 kbps的安全密钥率。

Conclusion: 该演示为在真实湍流环境中部署高速、长距离自由空间量子通信提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Free-space quantum key distribution (QKD) provides crucial advantages, including mobility and deployment flexibility, for securing next-generation communication networks. However, practical free-space implementations face major challenges, such as muilti-photon vulnerabilities, spatial mode mismatch, and atmospheric turbulence-induced beam fluctuations. In this work, we experimentally demonstrate a free-space decoy-state BB84 QKD system operating at a 100 MHz repetition rate with a 2.5 ns pulse width over a 2 km outdoor channel. By employing an active beam-wander correction based on fast-steering mirrors (FSMs) and position sensitive detectors (PSDs) con?figuration, our system achieves a secure key rate of 164.8 kbps under a quantum bit error rate (QBER) of approximately 3.3 %. This demonstration provides a practical framework for deploying high-rate, long-distance free-space quantum communication in realistic turbulence environments.

</details>


### [118] [Tree Tensor Network Reservoir Computing: Hierarchical Ensemble with Invariant Phase Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24127)
*Daiki Sasaki,Chih-Chieh Chen,Tomah Sogabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出树张量网络储层计算（TTN-RC）框架，利用树张量网络的层次结构作为随机储层进行时间序列预测。通过引入层次化集成方法控制输出指数的浓缩或发散，在NARMA基准测试中取得优于传统回声状态网络的性能，并从理论上推导了收缩率与稳定性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有储层计算方法（如回声状态网络）在处理高阶非线性和长程依赖任务时性能有限，而量子启发式张量网络方法存在输出指数级浓缩或发散的控制难题。

Method: 提出Tree Tensor Network Reservoir Computing (TTN-RC)框架，将树张量网络的层次结构作为随机储层；引入层次化集成方法将固定大小的储层划分为多个独立子储层以控制输出变化；基于储层雅可比矩阵推导预期收缩率，并建立储层状态统计的均场描述。

Result: 在NARMA基准测试中，TTN-RC取得了与回声状态网络竞争或更优的性能，尤其在高阶非线性处理和长程上下文依赖任务上表现突出。理论分析确定了在大树规模极限下的渐近稳定性边界为σ_T=√2，多个理论指标在该点收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于张量网络的储层计算提供了设计原则，阐明了层次化储层拓扑如何控制稳定性和非线性信息处理能力。

Abstract: We propose Tree Tensor Network Reservoir Computing (TTN-RC), a quantum-inspired reservoir computing framework for time-series prediction that uses the hierarchical structure of Tree Tensor Networks as a random reservoir. To control the exponential concentration or divergence of TTN outputs, we introduce a hierarchical ensemble method that partitions a fixed-size reservoir into multiple independent sub-reservoirs. In the tested NARMA benchmarks, TTN-RC achieves competitive or improved performance compared with conventional Echo State Networks, especially for tasks requiring higher-order nonlinear processing and longer contextual dependence. We also derive an expected contraction rate based on the reservoir Jacobian and develop a mean-field description of the reservoir-state statistics. These analyses identify an asymptotic stability boundary at $σ_{T}=\sqrt{2}$ in the large per-tree-size limit, where several theoretical indicators converge. Our results provide a design principle for tensor-network-based reservoir computing and clarify how hierarchical reservoir topology controls stability and nonlinear information processing.

</details>


### [119] [Local Universality and Structural Certificates for Minimal Fixed-Depth Two-Qutrit Gate Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24129)
*Yurui Liu,Ruoting Dou,Peng Xu,Xinsheng Tan,Shengjun Wu,Yang Yu,Zeng-Bing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种维度饱和的固定核拟设，使用四个不可调的双qutrit核心与五个可调局域层交错排列，在参数计数最小深度上实现了局部普适性。通过构造Clifford词核心证明了恒等点微分是精确等距，并分类了2304种满足相同分裂准则的辛作用。同时证明了复对称核心在恒等点存在结构秩障碍（最多78）。还评估了一个硬件驱动的超导核心，在1000个Haar随机目标上平均保真度≥0.999。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找最短的固定核架构以实现局部普适性，在参数计数不排除的最小深度上验证维度饱和的固定核拟设的有效性。

Method: 构建光滑映射Φ_K:L^5→SU(9)，利用右平凡化微分给出局部普适性的可验证证书。构造Clifford词核心使恒等点微分成为精确等距，分类满足相同分裂准则的2304种辛作用，并分析复对称核心的结构障碍。通过硬件驱动的超导核心进行数值验证。

Result: Clifford词核心在恒等点实现精确等距；复对称核心的恒等点微分秩最多为78；超导核心非对称（K≠K^T），在1000个Haar随机目标上平均保真度≥0.999，并具有良好的采样雅可比秩、结构目标和鲁棒性诊断结果。

Conclusion: 在参数计数最小、维度饱和深度上建立了局部普适性，提供了精确的Clifford证书和硬件驱动的数值案例研究，并将精确局部证书与更广泛的合成性能数值证据分开处理。

Abstract: We study a dimension-saturating fixed-core ansatz in which four copies of a fixed, non-tunable two-qutrit core $K\in SU(9)$ are interleaved with five adjustable local layers from $L=SU(3)\otimes SU(3)$. Since $\dim SU(9)=80$ and $5\dim L=80$, this is the shortest fixed-core architecture not excluded by parameter counting. We formulate the smooth map $Φ_K:L^5\to SU(9)$ and use its right-trivialized differential to give verifiable certificates for local universality. We construct an explicit Clifford-word core whose Pauli-label splitting makes the identity-point differential an exact isometry, and we classify all 2304 symplectic actions satisfying the same splitting criterion. We also prove a structural obstruction for an important symmetry class: every complex-symmetric core $K=K^{T}$, including every core generated by a time-independent real-symmetric Hamiltonian in the chosen computational basis, has identity-point differential rank at most 78; hence any full-rank certificate for such a core must occur away from that point. We then assess a hardware-motivated superconducting core generated by a noncommuting, temporally asymmetric drive. Direct calculation verifies $K_{\rm sc}\neq K_{\rm sc}^{\mathsf T}$, and the core achieves $F_{\rm avg}\ge 0.999$ for all 1000 Haar-random targets tested under the stated restart protocol. We also report favorable sampled Jacobian-rank, structured-target, and robustness diagnostics. These results establish local universality at the parameter-counting-minimal, dimension-saturating depth, with an exact Clifford certificate complemented by a hardware-motivated numerical case study. Throughout, we separate exact local certificates from numerical evidence for broader synthesis performance.

</details>


### [120] [Parallelizable Exact Synthesis of Quantum Circuits via Semi-Tensor Product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24195)
*Chenjian Li,Dingchao Gao,Xiangzhen Zhou,Ji Guan,Pengcheng Zhu,Zhufei Chu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半张量积理论的并行精确综合框架，用于CNOT和相位多项式电路。通过枚举无向部分门拓扑并分别求解缺失的门方向，实现了两阶段并行化，在32个工作节点上达到12.8倍加速。相比基于SAT的方法，在小规模实例上快100-1000倍，在QASMBench基准测试中中位加速比1.91倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确综合方法存在编码开销大、并行扩展性差和内存瓶颈问题，亟需一种高效的并行精确综合方法。

Method: 基于半张量积理论，将电路语义转换为规范STP公式，通过从右到左的因子化过程判定可行性；将门拓扑枚举与门方向求解解耦，实现两阶段并行化。

Result: 32个工作节点上实现12.8倍并行加速；小规模实例上比SAT方法快100-1000倍；在QASMBench中89%案例优于SAT方法，中位加速比1.91倍。

Conclusion: STP-based并行精确综合框架有效克服了传统方法的编码和内存瓶颈，在量子电路重综合优化中具有显著性能优势。

Abstract: Exact synthesis is a useful tool in quantum compilation by providing optimal alternative implementations of small circuit shards and is widely used as a circuit re-synthesis optimization kernel. However, existing exact synthesis methods suffer from encoding overhead, poor parallel scalability, and memory bottlenecks. This paper introduces a parallel exact synthesis framework for CNOT and phase polynomial circuits, which is based on the semi-tensor product (STP) theory of matrices. By enumerating undirected partial-gate topologies and solving the missing gate directions separately, we are able to parallelize both stages and achieve a parallel speedup of up to $12.8\times$ with 32 workers on this NP-hard problem. More specifically, for each topology, the circuit semantics are converted into canonical STP formulas, and feasibility is decided by a right-to-left factorization procedure that removes infeasible direction assignments. On randomly generated synthesis targets, STP is typically $100\times-1000\times$ faster than the SAT-based baseline on small instances, and remains competitive for more difficult instances. When integrated in a real-world circuit optimization workflow, our algorithm outperforms the SAT-based approach on 89% of cases in QASMBench, and achieves a median speedup of $1.91\times$.

</details>


### [121] [MPStab: an hybrid stabilizers tensor-network quantum circuit simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24258)
*Giulio Crognaletti,Mattia Robbiano,Michele Grossi,Matteo Robbiati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MPStab，一个基于稳定子和张量网络混合形式主义的量子电路模拟器，并展示了其核心功能和性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发经典计算机模拟量子系统的技术具有重要意义：一方面经典计算机在某些配置下非常有效，另一方面探索经典计算的极限有助于确定量子计算机的胜任范围。

Method: MPStab采用混合形式主义，结合稳定子（stabilizers）和张量网络（tensor networks）方法进行量子电路模拟。

Result: 展示了MPStab软件包的核心功能，并在若干有趣的模拟机制下探索了其性能表现。

Conclusion: MPStab作为一种混合模拟器，能够在特定模拟机制下有效工作，有助于研究经典计算与量子计算的边界。

Abstract: The development of techniques for simulating quantum systems using classical computers is a paramount task for two primary reasons: i) there exist configurations for which classical computers are remarkably effective and will continue to be so, and ii) exploring the limits of classical computation facilitates the identification of the regimes of competence for quantum computers. In this work, we present MPStab, a quantum circuit simulator based on a hybrid formalism combining stabilizers and tensor networks, recently introduced in Ref. [1]. We present the package, its core functionalities, and explore its performances in a few interesting simulation regimes.

</details>


### [122] [Systematic Experiment Tracking in Quantum Software: A Case Study of Reservoir Computing with Error Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24264)
*Otso Kinanen,Valter Uotila,Vlad Stirbu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了量子软件开发中的实验追踪问题，指出由于量子硬件仍处于实验阶段且软件不断演进，需要监控量子执行、聚合边际收益和检测错误源。通过量子储备池计算用于混沌时间序列预测的案例研究，展示了详细的量子软件开发过程及实验追踪方法，并将此知识泛化到更广泛的量子开发流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算正变得越来越普及，开发人员来自不同领域并使用各种方法，但现有实践和工具在实验追踪方面仍存在未解决的空白。本文旨在阐明量子计算对实验追踪提出的特殊需求，并提供系统化的解决方案。

Method: 通过一个具体的案例研究——量子储备池计算（Quantum Reservoir Computing）用于混沌时间序列数据预测并结合错误缓解（error mitigation）——展示详细的量子软件开发过程，并描述如何在开发全过程中追踪实验。然后将具体经验泛化为更广泛的量子开发流程。

Result: 提出了量子软件开发中实验追踪的关键需求：监控量子执行、聚合边际收益、检测错误源。通过案例研究展示了实验追踪的实际应用，并将其推广到一般量子开发过程。

Conclusion: 量子软件开发需要专门的实验追踪实践，以应对硬件的不稳定性和软件的快速演进。实验追踪是提升量子计算可靠性和可重复性的重要手段。

Abstract: Quantum computers are more widely available than ever, making the field more accessible and widespread. Practitioners are coming from a wide range of domains, conducting experiments and research using quantum computing approaches across a variety of problems. The current literature suggests that developers follow certain methodologies in quantum software development, often with a matching set of tools provided. Yet with the novel paradigm, there are areas that remain unaddressed in practices and tools. In this article, we go into the details of experiment tracking in quantum software development. We explain the basic concept of experiment tracking and detail how, in essence, quantum computing sets demands on tracking practices. Given the experimental state of hardware and the constantly evolving software, quantum execution must be monitored, marginal gains aggregated for the best outcome, and error sources detected. In our case study, quantum reservoir computing for chaotic time series data prediction with error mitigation, we present a detailed quantum software development process and describe how experiments can be tracked throughout development. We then generalize this knowledge into the broader quantum development process.

</details>


### [123] [Coherence as a resource for $N$-box and quantum pigeonhole paradoxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24282)
*Som Kanjilal,Rafael Wagner,Ernesto F. Galvão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析非逻辑的前后选择悖论（如N-盒子和量子鸽笼悖论），证明它们要求前后选择态在中间测量基下具有相干性，且等价于特定算符的弱值异常，从而阐明了这些场景中非经典性的量化基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将逻辑型前后选择悖论与语境性（contextuality）联系起来，但对于非逻辑型悖论（如N-盒子和量子鸽笼悖论），其非经典性的本质尚不清晰。本文旨在阐明这些更一般场景中非经典性的确切来源和量化特征。

Method: 通过证明每个非逻辑悖论成立当且仅当存在特定算符的弱值异常（weak-value anomaly），并结合弱值异常要求相干性这一已知事实，将悖论的非经典性归因于前后选择态在中间测量基下的相干性。

Result: 1) N-盒子和量子鸽笼悖论均要求前后选择态在中间测量基下具有相干性；2) 每个悖论等价于一个特定算符的弱值异常；3) 这种非经典性可由量子理论中的非相干子理论（incoherent sub-theories）与经典解释的定量偏离来刻画。

Conclusion: 非逻辑型前后选择悖论的非经典性源于弱值异常所需的相干性，这为理解不同类型的前后选择悖论提供了统一的非经典性框架，并将非经典性从逻辑型悖论的语境性扩展到了更一般的量化偏离。

Abstract: Pre- and post-selection (PPS) paradoxes are striking demonstrations of quantum nonclassicality. Logical PPS paradoxes, where inferences made with the Aharonov-Bergmann-Lebowitz (ABL) rule are exactly 0 or 1, are linked to contextuality. Non-logical paradoxes lack this strong signature. In this work, we analyse more general, non-logical PPS scenarios involving mixed pre- and post-selected states. We show that two such scenarios, the $N$-box and quantum pigeonhole paradoxes, require coherence of both pre- and post-selected states in the basis of the intermediate measurement. This is done by showing each paradox holds if and only if there is weak-value anomaly for a single, paradox-specific operator, together with the fact that weak-value anomaly requires coherence. This clarifies the role of different notions of nonclassicality in these scenarios, highlighting the required quantitative departures from (strictly) classical explanations provided by incoherent sub-theories of quantum theory.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum-Level Crosstalk Characterization of a 16x16 MEMS Optical Switch for Dynamic Quantum Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24299)
*Persefoni Konteli,Nikolas Makris,Grigoris Anastasiou,Konstantinos Tsimvrakidis,George T. Kanellos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对商用16x16 MEMS光开关进行全对全量子级串扰表征，结合SNSPD实验数据与诱骗态BB84 QKD协议理论影响分析，用于多用户量子通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户量子通信网络中，光开关的量子级串扰会严重影响QKD性能，现有研究缺乏对MEMS光开关在实际量子通信场景下的系统级串扰表征与影响分析。

Method: 采用SNSPD对商用16x16 MEMS光开关进行全对全（all-to-all）通道间的量子级串扰测量，并将实验数据与诱骗态BB84 QKD协议的理论模型相结合进行影响分析。

Result: 获得了MEMS光开关各通道间的量子级串扰实验数据，并定量评估了该串扰对诱骗态BB84 QKD协议的关键性能指标（如密钥率、量子误码率等）的影响。

Conclusion: MEMS光开关的量子级串扰会对多用户QKD网络性能产生可量化的负面影响，该表征方法和分析框架为实用化量子交换网络的器件选型与系统设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We present an all-to-all quantum-level crosstalk characterization of a commercial 16x16 MEMS optical switch for multi-user quantum communications using SNSPDs, correlating experimental data with a theoretical impact analysis on the decoy-state BB84 QKD protocol.

</details>


### [125] [On the two-copy distillability of Werner states and a new partial trace inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24309)
*Thomas C. Fraser,Felix Huber,Balázs Pozsgay,István Vona*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定了《量子信息理论中的五个开放问题》中的问题5，证明了两-ququart Werner态 ϱ(4,-½) 不是两拷贝可蒸馏的，并给出了更一般的不等式条件，最终表明 ϱ(d,α) 的单拷贝和两拷贝可蒸馏区域一致（α ≥ -½）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决《量子信息理论中的五个开放问题》[PRX Quantum 3, 010101 (2022)] 中的问题5，即确定两-ququart Werner态 ϱ(4,-½) 是否为两拷贝可蒸馏态。

Method: 首先证明一个更强的矩阵迹不等式：对任意秩 ≤ r ≤ d₁d₂ 的矩阵 C，有 tr₁(C)∥²_F + ∥tr₂(C)∥²_F ≤ r∥C∥²_F + (1/r)|tr(C)|²。然后利用Costa Rico关于该不等式在r=2时与两拷贝不可蒸馏性等价的结论，直接否定问题5。

Result: ϱ(4,-½) 不是两拷贝可蒸馏的。进一步，对所有 d ≥ 2，ϱ(d,α) 是两拷贝不可蒸馏的当且仅当 α ≥ -½，即单拷贝与两拷贝的可蒸馏区域完全一致。

Conclusion: 完全解决了问题5，并且指出这些结果是借助AI工具发现和撰写的，暗示这可能对量子信息与计算领域带来结构性变革。

Abstract: Problem 5 in {\it Five Open Problems in Quantum Information Theory} [PRX Quantum 3, 010101 (2022)], asks whether the two-ququart Werner state $\varrho(4,-\tfrac12)$ is two-copy distillable, where $\varrho(d,α)=(I+αF)/(d^2+αd)$. We answer it in the negative. To this end, we show the following stronger statement: for all $C\in M_{d_1d_2}(\mathbb{C})$ of rank at most $r \le d_1 d_2$, $\mathrm{tr}_1(C)\|_F^2+\|\mathrm{tr}_2(C)\|_F^2 \le r\|C\|_F^2+\frac{1}{r}|\mathrm{tr}(C)|^2$. A result by Costa Rico on the equivalence of this inequality with two-copy undistillability at $r = 2$ then settles Problem 5: $\varrho(4,-\tfrac{1}{2})$ is not two-copy distillable. Furthermore, we show that $\varrho(d,α)$ is two-copy undistillable for every $d\ge2$, if and only if $α\ge-\tfrac{1}{2}$. Thus, the one and two-copy distillability regions of $\varrho(d,α)$ coincide. These results have been found and written up with AI tools, pointing towards a structural change affecting the field of quantum information and computation.

</details>


### [126] [Fast Generation of Metrologically Relevant Fock State Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24318)
*Gonzalo Reina Rivero,Marcel Morillas-Rozas,Alberto López-García,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速激光脉冲序列，用于在捕获离子运动中产生非热Fock态混合，目标是在位移计量中超越标准量子极限。通过在极化子框架下描述离子-激光相互作用，找到了零失谐且拉比频率匹配阱频的共振工作点，使得选择性布居俘获在强驱动下仍然存在，无需基态冷却即可实现超越弱驱动极限的制备速度。数值模拟表明，通过脉冲持续时间重聚焦和布洛赫-西格特失谐补偿两种常规校准，可将制备误差控制在10%以内（η≈0.5），并恢复位移传感的Fisher信息，相比未校正序列恢复高达9 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位移计量方案受限于标准量子极限，且已有Fock态制备协议需要在弱驱动极限下工作，制备速度慢且需要基态冷却。作者希望开发一种快速、无需基态冷却的方案，用于产生非热Fock态混合，以提升位移计量的灵敏度。

Method: 采用极化子框架（polaron-frame）描述离子-激光相互作用，识别出零失谐且拉比频率匹配阱频的共振工作点。通过分析发现强驱动下残余不保真度主要来自旋转波近似中忽略的反旋蓝边带项，并提出两种校准方案：脉冲持续时间的百分比级重聚焦和小型布洛赫-西格特失谐补偿。

Result: 数值模拟显示，经过校准的脉冲序列可在Lamb-Dicke参数η≈0.5时仍将制备误差控制在10%以下；位移传感的Fisher信息得以恢复，相比未校正序列获得高达9 dB的提升。

Conclusion: 通过两种常规校准技术（脉冲重聚焦和Bloch-Siegert失谐补偿），可以有效抑制强驱动下反旋项带来的误差，实现快速、高保真度的非热Fock态混合制备，为超越标准量子极限的位移计量提供实用方案。

Abstract: We propose a fast laser pulse sequence for the generation of non-thermal Fock state mixtures of the motion of a trapped ion, targeted at displacement metrology beyond the standard quantum limit. Using a polaron-frame description of the ion-laser interaction, we identify a resonant operating point-zero detuning and a Rabi frequency matching the trap frequency-at which selective population trapping survives strong driving, enabling preparation speeds beyond the weak-driving limit of previous protocols without requiring ground-state cooling. We trace the residual infidelity at large Lamb-Dicke parameter $η$ to a single coherent process, the counter-rotating blue-sideband term neglected in the rotating-wave approximation, and show that it is suppressed by two routine calibrations: a percent-level refocusing of the pulse duration and a small compensating Bloch-Siegert detuning. Numerical simulations of the full sequence show that this refinement keeps the preparation error at or below the $10\%$ level up to $η\approx0.5$ and restores the displacement-sensing Fisher information that the uncorrected protocol loses at strong coupling, recovering up to 9 dB relative to the nominal sequence.

</details>


### [127] [Electron Shuttle Waiting Times for Electric Field Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24350)
*Alberto Mayorgas,Alberto López-García,Enamul Haque,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在量子电子穿梭机中利用等待时间统计进行电场传感。通过分析电荷传输波动与机电参数的关系，发现从隧穿到穿梭的交叉区域具有增强的涨落和参数灵敏度，从而显著提升了Fisher信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子电子穿梭机中等待时间统计在电场传感中的应用潜力，研究传输波动如何编码机电参数，以实现更高灵敏度的电场测量。

Method: 使用强库仑阻塞和高偏压条件下的单能级量子穿梭机模型，结合马尔可夫量子主方程，在相空间中分析稳态动力学和等待时间分布。通过估计与电场成正比的机电耦合，计算等待时间中的经典Fisher信息，并与稳态的量子Fisher信息进行比较。

Result: 从隧穿到穿梭的交叉区域表现为涨落增强和参数灵敏度提高，导致Fisher信息显著增强。将计量响应与平均等待时间、方差和Fano因子相关联。

Conclusion: 等待时间统计可用于电场传感，穿梭机交叉区域的增强涨落有助于提高参数估计的灵敏度。

Abstract: We explore the use of waiting-time statistics in a quantum electron shuttle for electric-field sensing. Electron shuttles convert nanomechanical motion into charge transport, showing a noise-broadened crossover between stochastic tunneling and mechanically assisted charge transfer. This allows investigation of how transport fluctuations encode electromechanical parameters. Using a single-level quantum shuttle in strong-Coulomb-blockade and high-bias regimes with a Markovian quantum master equation, we analyze stationary dynamics in phase space and waiting time distributions. By estimating the electromechanical coupling, proportional to the electric field, we evaluate the classical Fisher information in waiting times and compare it with the quantum Fisher information of the stationary state. We relate the metrological response to mean waiting time, variance, and Fano factor. Our results show that the crossover from tunneling to shuttling is characterized by enhanced fluctuations and increased parameter sensitivity, leading to a pronounced enhancement of the Fisher information.

</details>


### [128] [Multivariate Time Series Forecasting with Adaptive Non-Local Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24399)
*Yu-Ting Lee,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出MTSF-ANO混合模型，将变分量子电路与自适应非局域可观测量(ANO)相结合用于多变量时间序列预测，在4个ETT数据集上20个设置中17个排名前二，相比最强基线提升最高20%，证明了ANO是量子时间序列预测的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子神经网络应用于多变量时间序列预测时，通常依赖固定的局域测量，这限制了模型的表达能力和性能。

Method: 提出MTSF-ANO混合模型，将变分量子电路与自适应非局域可观测量(ANO)集成，引入非局域观测来增强量子模型的表达能力。

Result: 在四个ETT数据集上，MTSF-ANO在20个设置中17个排名第一或第二，在ETTh1上相比最强基线提升最高20%，在所有设置中均优于或匹配使用固定局域观测量的对应模型。消融实验揭示了量子电路设计和ANO非局域性对性能的影响。

Conclusion: ANO是量子时间序列预测中有前景的研究方向，自适应非局域可观测量能有效提升量子模型在MTSF任务上的性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting (MTSF) predicts future values of multiple variables from historical data. While quantum neural networks have been increasingly applied to this task, they typically rely on fixed local measurements, which restrict their expressivity. We propose MTSF-ANO, a simple hybrid model for MTSF that integrates variational quantum circuits with adaptive non-local observables (ANO). On the four ETT datasets, MTSF-ANO ranks first or second in MSE in 17 of 20 settings, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 20% on ETTh1, and outperforms or matches its fixed local observable counterpart across all settings. Our ablations show how the quantum circuit design and ANO non-locality affect performance. These results suggest that ANO is a promising direction for quantum time series forecasting.

</details>


### [129] [Floquet time-convolutionless master equation for non-Markovian driven quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24406)
*Pietro Follia,Heinz-Peter Breuer,Bassano Vacchini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合Floquet理论与时间卷积无投影算子技术，推导出非马尔可夫驱动开放量子系统的Floquet时间卷积无主方程，以驱动自旋-玻色子系统为例展示了其在强非马尔可夫效应下的有效性，并揭示了准能级交叉导致的耗散解耦机制及其对非马尔可夫度量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理周期驱动开放量子系统时，难以同时精确描述非马尔可夫记忆效应和周期性驱动的影响，尤其是在强非马尔可夫区域。本文旨在发展一种能够精确处理周期驱动、同时无需马尔可夫近似的局域时间主方程。

Method: 将Floquet理论与时间卷积无（TCL）投影算符技术相结合，在系统-环境耦合强度的二阶展开下推导出Floquet时间卷积无主方程（Floquet-TCL主方程），不进行马尔可夫近似。以驱动自旋-玻色子系统为例，通过数值计算验证其有效性。

Result: 该主方程能描述强非马尔可夫效应，在马尔可夫极限下退化为Floquet-Lindblad主方程。发现驱动耗散系统中记忆效应的特征表现为：基于迹距离的非马尔可夫度随驱动振幅变化出现尖锐峰值，这些峰值源于准能级交叉，进而通过准能级诱导的耗散解耦机制产生几乎无退相干的保护子空间。

Conclusion: Floquet-TCL主方程为研究周期驱动开放量子系统中的非马尔可夫动力学提供了有效框架，准能级交叉驱动的耗散解耦机制为记忆效应的调控和退相干保护提供了新的物理图像。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of open quantum systems driven by an external time-periodic force. Combining Floquet theory and the time-convolutionless projection operator technique we derive a time-local quantum master equation which exactly takes into account the periodic driving, while treating the system-environment interaction within second order in the coupling strength without performing the Markov approximation. The resulting equation of motion for the reduced density matrix is called Floquet time-convolutionless master equation. Employing the example of the driven spin-boson system, we demonstrate that this master equation is capable of describing strong non-Markovian effects, while yielding the Floquet-Lindblad master equation in the Markovian limit. A characteristic feature of memory effects in such driven dissipative systems is the emergence of sharp peaks of the trace-distance based non-Markovianity measure as a function of the driving amplitude, which can be traced back to quasienergy crossings leading to almost decoherence-protected subspaces through a quasienergy-induced dissipative decoupling mechanism.

</details>


### [130] [QuantumFCS.jl: Efficient Full-Counting Statistics for Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24429)
*Aaron Daniel,Marcelo Janovitch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QuantumFCS.jl 是一个开源的 Julia 包，用于高效灵活地计算全计数统计（FCS），支持粒子流、电流和热流，实现了递归累积量算法，并通过两个量子系统示例展示了其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏一个灵活、高效且易于使用的开源工具来对量子输运、量子光学和开放量子系统中的电流涨落进行全计数统计计算，特别是对于直接稠密方法难以处理的模型。

Method: 开发 QuantumFCS.jl Julia 包，通过监测跳跃算子和权重定义电流，实现统一的粒子流、电流和热流处理框架；采用递归累积量算法，支持稠密、稀疏和迭代求解器后端。

Result: 在驱动-耗散 Jaynes-Cummings 系统中展示了光子计数（揭示亮/暗发射间歇性、反聚束）和非线性电路 QED 热引擎中的热流涨落（探测热力学不确定关系），基准测试显示相对于现有 FCS 实现有显著加速。

Conclusion: QuantumFCS.jl 为量子系统中的全计数统计计算提供了高效、灵活且易于使用的工具，能够处理多种电流类型和复杂模型，高阶累积量可以揭示间歇性和热力学不确定关系等物理现象。

Abstract: Full-counting statistics (FCS) provides a systematic framework for characterising current fluctuations in quantum transport, quantum optics, and open quantum systems. We introduce QuantumFCS.jl, an open-source Julia package for efficient and flexible numerical FCS calculations. The package defines currents through monitored jump operators and weights, allowing particle, electric, and heat currents to be treated within the same workflow. It implements a recursive cumulant algorithm with dense, sparse, and iterative solver backends, making it suitable for models that are challenging for direct dense methods. We demonstrate the package on photon counting in a driven-dissipative Jaynes-Cummings system near the blockade-breakdown phase transition and on heat-current fluctuations in a non-linear circuit-QED heat engine. These examples show how higher cumulants reveal intermittency between bright and dim emission, antibunching, and provide probes of thermodynamic uncertainty relations. Benchmarks against an existing FCS implementation show substantial speed-ups.

</details>


### [131] [Two-copy nondistillability of Werner states: sharp partial-trace inequalities and finite-copy extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24479)
*Kishor Bharti,Rishikesh Gajjala,Tobias Haug*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了Werner态在任意局域维数下的两拷贝可蒸馏性问题，证明了Werner态当且仅当α < -1/2时是两拷贝可蒸馏的，并给出了多拷贝情况下的精确刻画与算子不等式层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子信息论中Werner态的两拷贝可蒸馏性问题，特别是Horodecki等人提出的Problem 5（关于两ququart态ρ^{(4)}_{-1/2}的两拷贝不可蒸馏性）。

Method: 推导了一个关于秩不超过2的算子的锐利维数无关不等式（Hilbert-Schmidt范数与部分迹的不等式），利用该不等式刻画Werner态两拷贝可蒸馏性的精确边界；对多拷贝情况，给出了三个等价表述、端点算子不等式层次，以及构造性常数γ_k。

Result: Werner态ρα是两拷贝可蒸馏的当且仅当α < -1/2；两ququart态ρ^{(4)}_{-1/2}是两拷贝不可蒸馏的；对任意有限拷贝数k，给出了问题的三个精确表述；构造了显式常数γ_k > 0使得α ≥ -γ_k时在所有维数下k拷贝不可蒸馏。

Conclusion: 完全解决了Werner态的两拷贝可蒸馏性问题，并为多拷贝情况提供了系统的理论框架，包括算子不等式、端点刻画和构造性常数。

Abstract: We solve the two-copy distillability problem for Werner states in every local dimension. Our main matrix result is a sharp, dimension-free inequality: for every rank-at-most-two operator, the sum of the squared Hilbert--Schmidt norms of its two partial traces is bounded by twice its squared Hilbert--Schmidt norm plus one half of the squared modulus of its trace. This implies that a Werner state $ρ_α$ is two-copy distillable if and only if $α<-1/2$. In particular, the two-ququart state $ρ^{(4)}_{-1/2}$ is two-copy undistillable, resolving Problem 5 of Horodecki, Rudnicki, and Życzkowski. For an arbitrary finite number $k$ of copies, we give three exact formulations of the remaining problem. At the endpoint $α=-1/2$, undistillability is equivalent to nonnegativity of the endpoint partial-trace form on every rank-at-most-two operator. We also derive an equivalent hierarchy of operator inequalities $H_k(ψ)\succeq0$ for pure states with a maximally mixed qubit marginal. The two-copy proof does not formally induct, because partial trace can increase rank and $2$-positivity is not generally preserved by tensor products. We prove two rigorous many-copy extensions. First, the quadratic form factorizes exactly on tensor-factorized witnesses; for any such decomposition, the endpoint inequality holds if its possible rank-two factor is supported on a block containing at most two copies. Second, we construct explicit constants $γ_k>0$ such that $α\ge-γ_k$ implies $k$-copy undistillability in every dimension. The initial proofs were generated by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol; the authors have verified and rewritten them to enhance readability and provide additional context.

</details>


### [132] [A Bohmian version of a 2-state quantum system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24480)
*Matthew Dickau*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Gillespie(1994)声称量子力学无法被表述为马尔可夫过程的论点，证明玻姆框架下的随机过程可以产生良定义的概率。

Method: 构建2态量子系统的玻姆版本，在标准量子态之外引入一个确定的物理态，该物理态在量子态的引导下发生随机变化。

Result: 该随机过程产生的概率是良定义的，反驳了Gillespie(1994)的观点。

Conclusion: 玻姆方法可以成功构建具有良定义概率的2态量子系统随机过程，量子力学在特定框架下可以被表述为马尔可夫过程。

Abstract: I construct a Bohmian version of a 2-state quantum system, where in addition to the quantum state, the system is characterized by a definite physical state which changes stochastically under the guidance of the quantum state. I argue that the probabilities produced by this process are well-defined, contrary to claims in "Why quantum mechanics cannot be formulated as a Markov process" by D. Gillespie (1994) about a related model.

</details>


### [133] [Bulk spectra and the non-Hermitian skin effect in systems with long-range couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24496)
*Jonathan Sturm,Pia Kreß,Adriana Pálffy*
