TL;DR: 本文比较了无限晶格和周期边界条件两种平移对称性处理方法在长程耦合晶格模型能带计算中的差异。研究发现，对于长程耦合系统（如自由空间和波导中的一维量子发射器链），周期边界条件在物理和数学上更为稳健，而无限链方法存在收敛问题且无法合理解释非厄米趋肤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证在长程耦合系统中，无限晶格与周期边界条件两种能带计算方法是否仍然等价，并探究哪种方法能更准确地描述非厄米趋肤效应等物理现象。

Method: 对比分析两种平移对称性处理方法（无限晶格 vs 周期边界条件），以一维量子发射器链为模型，分别在自由空间和波导耦合两种场景下进行计算和比较，特别针对非厄米趋肤效应进行检验。

Result: 周期边界条件在长程耦合系统中能给出物理和数学上均稳健的结果；无限链方法存在收敛问题；波导系统中出现非星形广义布里渊区、零绕数强局域本征态等反常发现。

Conclusion: 在长程耦合晶格模型中，周期边界条件是更可靠的计算方法，而无限链方法无法充分解释波导系统中非厄米趋肤效应的起源。

Abstract: To achieve translational symmetry for the computation of the band structure of a lattice model, one can either consider an infinite lattice or impose periodic boundary conditions. While in sys- tems with short-range couplings these two approaches are equivalent, we show that for long-range couplings one can obtain considerably different results. We compare the two methods on the basis of one-dimensional quantum emitter chains both in free space and when coupled to a waveguide. The latter system allows for asymmetric couplings enabling the non-Hermitian skin effect, which we analyze using the two different band-structure calculation methods. We find that only periodic boundary conditions lead to physically and mathematically robust results, while the infinite-chain approach entails convergence issues and is unable to satisfyingly explain the emergence of the non- Hermitian skin effect in the waveguide system. In addition, the waveguide system yields unusual findings like a non-star shaped generalized Brillouin zone and strongly localized eigenstates with zero winding number.

</details>


### [134] [Thermal Quantum Correlations in Coupled Andreev Spin Qubits: Interplay of Superconducting Phase and Spin-Orbit Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24521)
*Mohammad Reza Pourkarimi,Mina Shiri,Mehdi Monemi,Vadim Ohanyan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个耦合超导自旋量子比特系统中的热量子关联，利用局部量子Fisher信息（LQFI）和局部量子不确定性（LQU）量化超导相位差、隧穿振幅、自旋-轨道耦合及温度对非经典性质的影响。结果表明：量子关联随温度升高单调递减，而强隧穿和自旋-轨道耦合可增强其鲁棒性；超导相位通过调制有效交换耦合引入周期性行为；LQFI始终大于LQU，反映其对量子涨落更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Andreev自旋量子比特中热量子关联的微观机制，探究超导相位工程、自旋-轨道耦合和隧穿效应在保护混合超导量子器件量子资源中的作用。

Method: 采用LQFI和LQU作为量子关联量化指标，解析求解热密度矩阵，分析有效Andreev自旋量子比特Hamiltonian的能量谱，并系统扫描超导相位差、隧穿振幅、自旋-轨道耦合强度及温度等参数空间。

Result: (1) 量子关联随温度升高单调递减；(2) 强隧穿和自旋-轨道耦合显著增强量子关联的鲁棒性；(3) 超导相位通过调制有效交换耦合引入周期性干涉行为；(4) 量子关联增强与基态和第一激发态间能隙增大密切相关；(5) LQFI始终大于LQU。

Conclusion: 揭示了Andreev自旋量子比特中热量子关联的微观机制，强调了相位工程、自旋-轨道耦合和隧穿在保护混合超导量子器件量子资源中的重要作用。

Abstract: We investigate thermal quantum correlations in a system of two coupled superconducting spin qubits described by an effective Andreev spin-qubit Hamiltonian in the presence of spin-orbit interaction. Using Local Quantum Fisher Information (LQFI) and Local Quantum Uncertainty (LQU) as quantum-correlation quantifiers, we analyze the effects of the superconducting phase difference, tunneling amplitude, spin-orbit coupling, and temperature on the nonclassical properties of the system. Analytical expressions for the thermal density matrix are obtained and employed to evaluate both quantities. Our results show that quantum correlations decrease monotonically with increasing temperature, while stronger tunneling and spin-orbit interaction significantly enhance their robustness. Moreover, the superconducting phase introduces a pronounced periodic behavior through the modulation of the effective exchange couplings, leading to constructive and destructive interference regimes that strongly influence the correlations. By analyzing the energy spectrum of the effective Hamiltonian, we demonstrate that the enhancement of quantum correlations is closely associated with an increased energy gap between the ground and first excited states, which suppresses thermal excitations and stabilizes the correlated ground state. Furthermore, LQFI is consistently larger than LQU throughout the investigated parameter space, reflecting its higher sensitivity to quantum fluctuations and local parameter estimation. These findings reveal the microscopic mechanism governing thermal quantum correlations in Andreev spin qubits and highlight the important roles of phase engineering, spin-orbit interaction, and tunneling in protecting quantum resources in hybrid superconducting quantum devices.

</details>


### [135] [Entanglement Distillation and Swapping Scheduling in Quantum Repeaters with Noisy Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24557)
*Siddharth Chander,Xinan Chen,Allen Zang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子记忆相干时间有限的情况下，量子纠缠蒸馏与纠缠交换的最优时序策略。在单跳场景中，低相干时间下丢弃旧纠缠态策略取得最高保真度，高相干时间下延迟蒸馏策略取得最佳加权相干信息。在两跳中继链中，延迟蒸馏策略表现最优，而丢弃旧态策略从未获得正的加权相干信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设完美量子记忆，但近期的量子网络受限于有限相干时间的量子记忆，导致操作时机和顺序的选择变得复杂。本文旨在阐明记忆退相干所引发的基本权衡。

Method: 在最小单跳场景中进行解析比较"尽早蒸馏"、"尽晚蒸馏"与"丢弃旧态"三种策略；在两跳中继链中使用蒙特卡洛仿真扩展分析。

Result: 单跳场景：低相干时间下丢弃旧态策略保真度最高，高相干时间下延迟蒸馏策略保真度和加权相干信息最优。两跳场景：延迟蒸馏策略（Distill-ALAP-then-Swap-ALAP和Swap-ASAP-then-Distill-ALAP）性能最佳，丢弃旧态策略从未获得正的加权相干信息。

Conclusion: 退相干重新塑造了量子网络中的最优操作时序，研究结果为理解更大规模架构的链路级原理提供了指导性见解。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation and entanglement swapping have been extensively studied assuming perfect quantum memories. However, near-term quantum networks will be fundamentally limited by quantum memories with a finite coherence time, resulting in complex choices for the timing and ordering of these operations. In this work, we study entanglement distillation and entanglement swapping at the level of the elementary building blocks of noisy quantum repeater networks, with the goal of elucidating the fundamental tradeoffs induced by memory decoherence. First, we focus on a minimal one-hop setting, where we analytically compare ``distill-as-soon-as-possible'' and ``distill-as-late-as-possible'' strategies against a baseline strategy that simply discards the older entangled state. We find that in the low memory coherence time regime, discarding the older entangled state achieves higher expected output fidelity, while in the high coherence time regime, delaying distillation until the end achieves the highest expected output fidelity and, over most of the deadline range, the highest weighted coherent information, at the expense of a lower success probability. We then extend our analysis to two-hop repeater chains using Monte Carlo simulation. In this setting, we find that the highest weighted coherent information is achieved by strategies that defer distillation to the end of the time window, with \textsf{Distill-ALAP-then-Swap-ALAP} and \textsf{Swap-ASAP-then-Distill-ALAP} leading at different operating points, while \textsf{Discard-Oldest-then-Swap} never reaches positive weighted coherent information in any regime tested. Together, these results clarify how decoherence reshapes the optimal operation timing in quantum networks and provide instructive insights into the link-level principles that govern larger-scale architectures.

</details>


### [136] [Unravelling time-resolved Interparticle Coulombic Decay: From spectral formation to decay lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24597)
*Alexander V. Riegel,Max K. Humm,Jan-Thore Kahle,Elke Fasshauer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过解析模型展示了核动力学如何影响电子衰变过程的时间分辨光谱，发现信号中包含exp(-t/τ)和exp(-t/(2τ))两种贡献，但单指数拟合仍可很好地描述信号却得到错误的寿命值。以氖二聚体中的粒子间库仑衰变(ICD)为例，将实验提取的寿命从150(50) fs修正为73 fs，使实验值与理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从时间分辨电子衰变光谱中提取潜在的电子动力学信息需要理解核运动如何塑造可观测量信号，但目前缺乏对包含离解核动力学的电子衰变光谱的解析描述。

Method: 将时间分辨电子衰变光谱的解析描述扩展到包含离解核动力学，应用于氖二聚体的粒子间库仑衰变(ICD)，推导时间分辨信号的解析表达式，分析不同振动共振态路径间的干涉效应。

Result: (1) 光谱再现了实验观测到的谱形，揭示了不同振动共振态路径间干涉的重要作用；(2) 建立了谱结构时间积累与衰变电子态核波包动力学之间的清晰联系；(3) 解析表达式显示时间分辨ICD信号同时包含exp(-t/τ)和exp(-t/(2τ))贡献，但单指数拟合仍能很好描述信号却给出与真实值显著偏离的寿命；(4) 将理论模型应用于实验数据，将ICD寿命从150(50) fs修正为73 fs。

Conclusion: 核动力学对电子衰变过程的时间分辨光谱有显著影响，传统单指数拟合提取的寿命值可能严重偏离真实值。该发现具有超越ICD的普适意义，适用于各类电子衰变过程的寿命提取。

Abstract: Electronic decay processes provide a fascinating window into correlated electronic rearrangements occurring on ultrafast timescales. Following these dynamics in real time has become increasingly accessible experimentally, but extracting the underlying electronic dynamics from measured spectra requires understanding how nuclear motion shapes the observable signal. Here, we extend an analytical description of time-resolved electronic decay spectra to include dissociative nuclear dynamics and apply it to Interparticle Coulombic Decay (ICD) in the neon dimer. The resulting spectra reproduce the experimentally observed spectral shape and reveal an unexpectedly important role of interference between pathways involving different vibronic resonance states. We further establish a clear connection between the temporal build-up of the spectral structure and the nuclear wavepacket dynamics in the decaying electronic state. Most strikingly, our analytical expressions reveal that the time-dependent integrated ICD signal contains contributions proportional to both $\exp(-t/τ)$ and $\exp[-t/(2τ)]$. Nevertheless, a conventional mono-exponential fit can describe the temporal signal remarkably well while yielding a decay lifetime that differs substantially from the underlying value. Applying the theoretically derived fitting model to experimental data for the neon dimer yields an ICD lifetime $τ$ of 73 fs, rather than the previously extracted 150(50) fs, placing the experimental value within the range of previous theoretical predictions. Since the underlying temporal structure is common to electronic decay processes, our findings have implications for extracting lifetimes from time-resolved decay spectra well beyond ICD.

</details>


### [137] [Experimental Protocol Fingerprinting in Quantum Networks via Physical Layer Side Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24624)
*Lance Young,Contessa Wilburn,Carrie Houston,Blaine Keyton,Marwan Elawady,Mohamed Shaban,Muhammad Ismail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验证明，通过被动侧信道观测（如单光子检测统计和光功率测量），可以在不破坏量子纠缠的情况下，以高达96%的准确率识别四种不同量子通信协议（纠缠分发、量子门序列、 heralded QKD、量子身份认证），揭示了量子网络中协议级信息泄露的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统中侧信道分析已被证明可泄露敏感信息，而量子通信协议对物理层侧信道分析的暴露程度尚不明确。本文旨在探究量子网络中不同协议是否可通过被动侧信道观测被区分识别。

Method: 1) 在偏振纠缠光子链路上实现四种代表性协议；2) 收集物理层特征（单光子检测统计、光功率测量）作为协议指纹；3) 构建数据驱动框架进行协议识别分类；4) 通过贝尔不等式测量验证观测过程不破坏纠缠。

Result: 30:70采样/光抽头配置下协议识别准确率达96%；10:90配置下准确率70-89%；贝尔不等式测量确认采样过程保持纠缠不变。

Conclusion: 侧信道分析可在不破坏量子关联的情况下暴露协议级信息，为量子网络安全引入了新的安全考虑维度。

Abstract: Quantum communication is a key enabler of next-generation networks, leveraging quantum entanglement to enable a new class of information exchange. While prior work has focused on the theoretical analysis of communication protocols, their exposure to physical layer side channel analysis remains largely unexplored. In classical systems, side channel analysis has been shown to reveal sensitive information without accessing the underlying data, raising the question of whether similar risks exist in quantum networks. In this work, we investigate whether different quantum communication protocols exhibit distinguishable signatures that can be inferred through passive side channel observations. We consider a threat model in which an observer accesses only a fraction of the optical signal without directly measuring the encoded quantum states. Under this setting, we experimentally examine four representative protocols, namely entanglement distribution, quantum gate sequences, heralded quantum key distribution, and quantum identity authentication, realized on a polarization entangled photon link. Observable physical layer features, including single photon detection statistics and optical power measurements, are collected and used to construct protocol fingerprints. We develop a data-driven framework for protocol identification based on these observations. Our results show that protocol identity can be inferred with accuracy reaching up to 96% under 30:70 sampling configuration/optical tapping, while remaining distinguishable at 10:90 with accuracy ranging from 70-89%. Bell inequality measurements confirm that the sampling/tapping process preserves entanglement, validating the non-destructive nature of the observation model. These findings demonstrate that side channel analysis can expose protocol-level information without disrupting quantum correlations, introducing new security considerations.

</details>


### [138] [Experimental Side Channel Analysis of Protocol Stages in Quantum Identity Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24639)
*Marwan Elawady,Lance Young,Contessa Wilburn,Blaine Keyton,Carrie Houston,Mohamed Shaban,Muhammad Ismail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过量子通信测试实验，发现基于分束器的物理层侧信道可以高精度（F1值97%/30%采样，94%/10%采样）推断量子身份认证协议的阶段，从而让攻击者绕过认证窃取数据，揭示了一个此前被忽视的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中需要身份认证抵御中间人攻击，但现有工作仅分析认证的安全性及MitM检测保证，尚未探索物理层侧信道攻击的可行性。若攻击者能推断协议阶段，则可绕过认证量子比特直接提取数据量子比特，使认证失效。

Method: 在量子通信测试平台上，使用分束器分流30%或10%的光信号，通过单光子探测器和功率计采集光子到达时间与光功率数据，提取时序和强度变化特征，训练机器学习模型分类协议阶段。

Result: 30%采样下分类准确率98%（F1 97%），10%采样下准确率96%（F1 94%），表明协议阶段推断高度可行。

Conclusion: 发现了一个被忽视的物理层侧信道推理漏洞，强调需要设计鲁棒方案抵御此类攻击。

Abstract: Quantum networks can enable distributed computing and sensing. To realize these capabilities securely, quantum identity authentication is essential. Without authentication at the quantum layer, malicious repeaters may retain entanglement instead of performing swapping, enabling man-in-the-middle attacks (MitM) between communicating parties. Authentication mitigates this threat by embedding authentication qubits within data qubits at positions and bases based on a secret key shared a priori. While prior work analyzes security and MitM detection guarantees, physical layer side channel analysis remains unexplored. If an attacker infers protocol stages, it can avoid authentication qubits and extract data qubits, rendering authentication ineffective. To this end, we carry out experimental studies using a quantum communication testbed. A beam splitter is used to tap a portion of the optical signal, allowing the observer to collect side channel data without disrupting the quantum state. We evaluate two sampling settings, where 30% or 10% of the signal is diverted. The collected side channel data includes photon arrival timing and optical power data obtained using a single-photon detector and a power meter. Using this dataset, we extract and engineer features that capture both timing dynamics and signal intensity variations. We then train machine learning models to classify protocol stages based solely on side channel observations. Our results show that protocol-stage inference is feasible with high accuracy, reaching 98% (F1-score 97%) at 30% sampling and 96% (F1-score 94%) at 10% sampling. These findings reveal an overlooked vulnerability and highlight the need for robust designs against side channel inference attacks.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum-Limited Symbol-Blind Channel Estimation for Coherent State Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24644)
*Hongxu Chen,Renzhi Yuan,Haifeng Yao,Mugen Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在光子匮乏相干链路中，残余色散如何破坏时间模态匹配。针对等概率M进制PSK相干态，在已知光谱模式但符号和载波相位未知的情况下，建立了盲联合估计群延迟和二阶色散的量子极限，并提出了多输出量子脉冲门结合光子数分辨检测的方案，该方案可在湍流衰落条件下达到该极限并支持低于标准量子极限的接收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残余色散会破坏光子匮乏相干链路中的时间模态匹配，进而影响通信性能，因此需要建立相应的量子极限并设计可行的接收方案。

Method: 建立了盲联合估计群延迟和二阶色散的量子极限，形式为4N_s C，由中心生成元的协方差决定。提出了多输出量子脉冲门（multi-output quantum pulse gate）结合光子数分辨检测（photon-number-resolving detection）的本地实现方案。

Result: 该量子极限在消除公共相位后为4N_s C，其中C为中心生成元的协方差矩阵。所提方案可在本地达到该量子极限，并在湍流衰落条件下支持低于标准量子极限的接收性能。

Conclusion: 多输出量子脉冲门结合光子数分辨检测方案能够有效克服残余色散带来的时间模态失配问题，实现接近量子极限的盲联合估计性能，并能在实际衰落信道中超越标准量子极限。

Abstract: Residual dispersion breaks temporal-mode matching in photon-starved coherent links. For equiprobable $M$-ary PSK coherent states in a known spectral mode, with unknown symbols and carrier phase, we establish the quantum limit for blind joint estimation of group delay and second-order dispersion: after eliminating the common phase, it is $4N_s\mathbf{C}$, set by the covariance of the centered generators alone. A multi-output quantum pulse gate with photon-number-resolving detection locally attains it and supports reception below the standard quantum limit under turbulent fading.

</details>


### [140] [Geometric bounds on multiparameter Heisenberg scaling in optical metrology with limited squeezed resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24657)
*Atmadev Rai,Paolo Facchi,Vincenzo Tamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在被动线性光学网络中，使用k个单模压缩态和至少一个相干态作为探针时，能够实现海森堡标度灵敏度的独立参数组合数量的上限为min{p, k(k+3)/2}，其中协方差部分贡献最多k(k+1)/2个，一阶矩部分贡献最多k个独立参数组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多参数量子计量学、分布式传感以及大型光子干涉仪校准中，一个基本问题是：给定有限数量的压缩探针，在 multimode 高斯网络中，有多少个独立参数组合能够继承海森堡标度。本文旨在回答任意无源线性光学网络中的这一关键问题。

Method: 通过分析量子Fisher信息矩阵的海森堡标度系数秩，将贡献分解为协方差部分（压缩增强的涨落）和一阶矩部分（相干态的位移），分别推导各自的参数组合数量上限，并构造了一族可饱和这些上界的被动干涉仪。

Result: 海森堡标度系数秩的上界为n_HS ≤ min{p, k(k+3)/2}。其中协方差贡献最多k(k+1)/2个参数组合，一阶矩贡献最多k个参数组合。给出了饱和这些上界的条件，并构造了相应的被动干涉仪家族。

Conclusion: 在无源线性光学网络中，使用k个单模压缩态和至少一个相干态，最多可同时估计min{p, k(k+3)/2}个独立参数组合达到海森堡标度灵敏度，该上界由协方差和一阶矩两部分几何贡献构成，且存在可饱和的物理实现。

Abstract: The simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters is a central task in quantum metrology, distributed sensing, and the calibration of large photonic interferometers. A fundamental question is how many independent parameter combinations can inherit Heisenberg scaling from a given number of squeezed probes in a multimode Gaussian network. Here, we answer this question for arbitrary passive linear optical networks. For a $p$-parameter, $M$-channel interferometer probed by $k$ single-mode squeezed states and at least one coherent state in the remaining channels, we show that the rank of the Heisenberg-scaling coefficient of the quantum Fisher information matrix is bounded by $n_{\rm HS}\le \min\{p,k(k+3)/2\}$, which corresponds to the maximum number of independent combinations of parameters that can be estimated with Heisenberg-scaling sensitivity. The bound separates into two geometrically distinct contributions. The covariance contribution of the quantum Fisher information, which describes squeezing-enhanced fluctuations, provides at most $k(k+1)/2$ parameter combinations estimable at Heisenberg-scaling sensitivity, while the first-moment contribution provides at most $k$ additional independent parameter combinations with Heisenberg-scaling sensitivity. We identify the conditions for saturating these bounds and construct a passive family of interferometers that saturates these bounds.

</details>


### [141] [Exact and Fixed-Point Grover Search with Qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24658)
*Tanay Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，用于在任意维度的量子比特系统（qudit）上实现Grover搜索算法，包括异构系统。详细介绍了Oracle和扩散算子的构造、有无辅助qubit的实现、确定性和固定点搜索变体、相位匹配技术及电路分解，并通过Bloch球轨迹直观展示不同相位选择对目标态放大的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子平台从传统qubit（二能级）向qudit（多能级）发展，甚至异构系统（不同维度的qudit混合），需要将Grover搜索算法推广到这些新型架构中，以充分发挥多能级量子系统的计算潜力。

Method: 1. 建立了qudit Grover搜索的统一框架；2. 构建了无辅助qubit和有辅助qubit的Oracle与扩散算子；3. 推广了确定性和固定点搜索变体；4. 分析了相位匹配技术；5. 提供了适用于不同硬件平台的显式电路分解；6. 利用Bloch球轨迹可视化不同相位选择的效果。

Result: 提出了一个灵活的、面向硬件的qudit Grover搜索协议工具包，能够降低电路深度、提高成功概率，适用于量子计算和量子感知应用。

Conclusion: 该框架为在多能级量子系统上实现Grover搜索提供了系统化的方法，有助于推动qudit处理器的实际应用。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm provides a quadratic speedup for searching unstructured databases and is traditionally implemented with qubits in Hilbert spaces whose dimensions are powers of two. With the advent of quantum platforms utilizing qudits---quantum systems with more than two levels---there is a need to generalize Grover search to these architectures, including heterogeneous systems with qudits of varying dimensions. Here, we present a unified framework for qudit-based Grover search, detailing the construction of oracles and diffusion operators with and without ancilla qubits and generalizing deterministic and fixed-point search variants that ensure exact or bounded success probabilities. We analyze phase-matching techniques and provide explicit circuit decompositions suitable for diverse hardware platforms. We also compare the corresponding trajectories on the Bloch sphere to provide an intuitive visualization of how the different phase choices amplify the target state. These results facilitate flexible, hardware-oriented protocols for implementing Grover search on qudit processors, potentially reducing circuit depth and enhancing success probabilities, thereby offering a practical toolkit for quantum computation and sensing applications leveraging multilevel quantum systems.

</details>


### [142] [Stacking the Deck: Tunable Trainability in Stacked LCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24686)
*Nikhil Khatri,Stefan Zohren,Gabriel Matos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出堆叠线性酉组合（S-LCU）作为变分量子电路的一种新方案，通过层数l这一单一参数，在量子优势所需的表达能力与经典可模拟性之间实现可调谐的权衡，证明了损失景观方差下界与经典模拟代价之间的明确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分量子电路面临根本性困境：足够表达以抵抗经典模拟的ansätze往往出现贫瘠高原（难以训练），而能避免贫瘠高原的结构又通常可被经典高效模拟。亟需一种可系统性调节复杂度与可训练性的方案。

Method: 提出堆叠线性酉组合（S-LCU）作为变分ansatz，其中元素为费米子高斯酉（Free Fermion S-LCU）。采用图解分析方法，推导损失景观方差的下界，并对比经典模拟代价与量子门复杂度。

Result: 证明方差下界为Ω(1/(n k^{3l}))，经典模拟代价为O(k^{2l} n^3)，而量子门复杂度仅为O(l k n^2)。层数l作为单一旋钮，可连续调节计算复杂度与代价集中速率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: S-LCU为实践者提供了一种系统化方法，可根据具体应用和硬件条件，构建具有所需复杂度-可训练性权衡的变分ansatz。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits have been central to many proposed near-term applications of quantum computing, but a growing body of evidence suggests that trainability and quantum advantage are fundamentally at odds: ansätze expressive enough to resist efficient classical simulation tend to exhibit barren plateaus, while structures that provably rule out barren plateaus typically render them classically simulable. We propose a stacked linear combination of unitaries (S-LCU) as a variational ansatz which provides a tunable trade-off between barren plateaus and classical simulability. Using a diagrammatic analysis, we bound the loss-landscape variance of the Free Fermion S-LCU, whose elements are fermionic Gaussian unitaries. We prove a variance lower bound of $Ω(1/(n k^{3l}))$, with a simulation cost of $O(k^{2l} n^3)$ using the best known classical algorithm, compared to a quantum gate complexity of only $O(lkn^2)$. The number of layers $l$ serves as a single dial that trades computational complexity against the rate of cost concentration. This offers practitioners a systematic method for constructing ansätze with a complexity-trainability trade-off that best suits their application and hardware.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Incapacity beyond No-Cloning and PPT Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24693)
*Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个明确的三能级量子信道，其私有容量和量子容量均为零，但该信道既不是反退化信道，也不是部分转置正定信道，解决了量子信息理论中两个长期悬而未决的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两个长期开放问题：1) 零量子容量是否可出现在PPT和反退化类之外；2) 反退化是否是导致私有容量消失的唯一非平凡机制。

Method: 构造一个显式的qutrit信道Λ(X)=1/2X+1/4(Tr(X)I−X^T)，利用非正保伴随符号提升的加权缺陷存在精确秩三完全正分解，结合Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori/相对熵比较结果和完全少噪声张量积结果，证明在每个张量积幂下优化私有和相干信息为零。

Result: 证明了该信道在所有张量幂和所有有限维量子参考系统下，互补输出在相对熵意义上主导接收者输出，因此P(Λ)=Q(Λ)=0；同时证明该信道既非PPT也非反退化。

Conclusion: 该信道实现了超越标准PPT基束缚纠缠机制和无克隆机制的精确量子与私有容量为零，表明零容量现象比已知类别更为广泛。

Abstract: We show an explicit qutrit channel whose private and quantum capacities both vanish, although it is neither antidegradable nor positive under partial transposition (PPT). This resolves two longstanding open problems in quantum information theory: whether zero quantum capacity can occur outside the PPT and antidegradable classes, and whether antidegradability is the only nontrivial mechanism that forces the private capacity to vanish. For qutrit systems $A$ and $B$, the channel is \[ Λ_{A\to B}(X)= \frac{1}{2}X+\frac{1}{4}\left(\operatorname{Tr}(X)\mathbb{1}_{B}-X^{\mathsf T} \right). \] We prove that, for every tensor power and every finite-dimensional quantum reference system, the complementary output dominates the receiver output in relative entropy. Consequently, we show that the optimized private and coherent information vanish at every blocklength, and hence $P(Λ)=Q(Λ)=0$. The proof uses a nonpositive, adjoint-preserving signed lift whose weighted defect admits an exact rank-three completely positive factorization. Applying established Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori/relative-entropy comparison results and complete less-noisy tensorization results then yields the stated order at every tensor power, leading to zero quantum and private capacities. Meanwhile, we show that the channel is neither PPT nor antidegradable. The channel therefore realizes exact quantum and private incapacity beyond the standard PPT-based bound-entanglement mechanism and the no-cloning mechanism.

</details>


### [144] [Almost all pure entangled states enable unbounded nonlocality sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24700)
*Anna Steffinlongo,Nicola D'Alessandro,Martin J. Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Hardy悖论与顺序双粒子场景中非局域性共享的联系，证明几乎所有纯纠缠态（除最大纠缠态外）通过辅助少量局域辅助系统，使用投影测量即可实现任意多对观测者之间的CHSH不等式顺序违背，从而实现非局域性共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Hardy悖论与非局域性共享之间的内在联系，寻求更通用的非局域性共享协议。

Method: 通过建立Hardy悖论与非局域性共享的理论关联，利用两输入两输出场景中的Hardy关联，结合投影测量和小型局域辅助系统，在顺序测量场景中实现多对观测者间的CHSH不等式违反。

Result: 几乎所有纯纠缠态（除最大纠缠态外）在辅助局域系统后，使用投影测量即可实现任意多对观测者之间的非局域性共享。

Conclusion: Hardy悖论与非局域性共享存在深刻的理论联系，为更广泛的非局域性共享提供了通用方案。

Abstract: We establish a connection between Hardy's paradox and nonlocality sharing in sequential bipartite scenarios, where each subsystem is measured in turn by a chain of observers. We show that any correlations exhibiting a Hardy paradox in the two-input two-output scenario enable sequential violations of the CHSH inequality between arbitrarily many pairs of observers, using only projective measurements and the assistance of a small local ancilla system. Since almost all pure entangled states, with the only exception of the maximally entangled one, admit Hardy correlations, our protocol applies generically: almost all pure entangled states, if assisted by a local ancilla, allow for nonlocality sharing between arbitrarily many observer pairs using only projective measurements.

</details>


### [145] [How Many Shots Does It Take? A Noise-Aware Quantum Resource Allocation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24704)
*Prateek P. Kulkarni,Sumit K. Mandal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个闭式解析表达式来确定量子计算机上算法可靠执行所需的最优shots数量，并设计了在量子电路不同分区之间分配固定shots预算的理论方法。该方法可将可靠执行所需的shots减少约58%，能耗降低高达62%，同时将总误差降低高达73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机上执行算法需要大量重复执行（shots）才能获得可靠结果，当前实践中shots数量的选择缺乏理论指导，存在资源浪费和能耗过高的问题。

Method: 提出了一个精确的闭式解析表达式来确定最优shots数量，并基于理论推导设计了在量子电路不同分区之间分配固定shots预算的技术，以最小化总误差。

Result: 1) 可靠执行所需的shots减少约58%；2) 能耗降低高达62%；3) 最优shots分配技术使总误差降低高达73%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的解析模型和最优分配技术在减少量子计算资源消耗和误差方面具有显著效果，为量子算法的可靠执行提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Any algorithm execution on quantum computers requires several repeated and costly executions (known as shots) to obtain reliable results. In this work, we propose a closed-form accurate analytical expression to determine optimal number of shots required for reliable execution of any algorithm on a quantum computer. We also present a theoretically grounded technique to distribute fixed shot budget across different partitions in a quantum circuit minimizing the total error. Our proposed analytical model helps to reduce the shots associated with reliable execution of quantum algorithms by about 58\% compared to current practice, in turn reducing the energy consumption by upto 62\%. Furthermore, our proposed optimal shot allocation technique across different partitions reduces total error by up to 73\% compared to conventional approaches.

</details>


### [146] [Efficient LLM-Generated Shuttling Compilers for Complex Trapped-Ion Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24714)
*Fabian Kreppel,Reza Salkhordeh,Ferdinand Schmidt-Kaler,André Brinkmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次使用前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7和Claude Fable 5）自动生成并迭代优化离子阱量子计算机的穿梭编译器完整Python代码，涵盖线性分段阱、带结阱和任意连接阱图三类架构。LLM生成的编译器在手动手动编译器的基准测试中，前两类架构的穿梭时间步最多减少76%和39%，第三类架构中高连通性设计比走廊式设计减少一个数量级的穿梭步数。结果表明未经修改的前沿LLM可以自动生成正确且有竞争力的穿梭编译器，将新架构的开发时间从数月缩短至数天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子阱量子计算机依赖穿梭编译器将输入算法映射为离子量子比特在给定架构内的移动序列。传统上，这类编译器需要大量人工算法工程开发，开发周期长达数月。本文旨在探索是否可以利用未经修改的前沿大语言模型自动生成高效、正确且有竞争力的穿梭编译器，从而显著缩短新架构的软件开发时间。

Method: 使用前沿LLM（Claude Opus 4.7和Claude Fable 5）从书面规格说明生成并迭代完善完整的Python穿梭编译器代码。采用递增式开发策略：首先为线性分段阱生成编译器，然后扩展至带结阱，最后推广到任意连接阱图（自由连接的阱架构）。更一般情况的编译器以先前版本的代码为种子。使用一组通用量子电路对LLM生成的编译器与手工编译器进行基准测试，对比穿梭时间步数。整个生成和评估流程使用第二个前沿LLM重复执行以验证可复现性。

Result: 与手工编译器相比：线性分段阱架构穿梭时间步减少高达76%；带结阱架构减少高达39%；自由连接架构中，高连通性、富结的架构相比走廊式架构可减少一个数量级的穿梭步数。Claude Fable 5生成的编译器在最大规模电路上超越手工编译器的频率更高。总体而言，LLM生成的编译器在正确性和性能上均具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 未经修改的前沿LLM能够自动生成正确、高效且有竞争力的离子阱穿梭编译器，无需额外的手动算法工程。该方法可将新架构的开发时间从数月缩短至数天，显著提升离子阱量子计算软件栈的开发效率。

Abstract: Trapped-ion quantum computers rely on shuttling compilers, which cast an input algorithm into a sequence of ion-qubit movements within a given architecture. We present the first study in which a single frontier large language model (LLM), Claude Opus 4.7, generates and iteratively refines the full Python code of shuttling compilers from written specifications. We start with a compiler for (i) a linear segmented trap, extend it to (ii) a trap with junctions, and finally achieve efficient compilation for (iii) a broad class of connected trap graphs. The compilers for the more general cases are seeded with code from the previous ones. We benchmark the LLM-generated compilers against state-of-the-art hand-crafted ones using a common suite of quantum circuits. The number of shuttling timesteps is reduced by up to 76% for (i) and up to 39% for (ii). For the broad case (iii) of freely connected architectures, we find large variations in the required number of shuttling timesteps, depending on the connectivity. A densely connected, junction-rich architecture yields an order-of-magnitude reduction in shuttling timesteps compared to a corridor-like one. Repeating the complete generation and evaluation with a second frontier LLM, Claude Fable 5, reproduces these findings, with the Fable 5 compilers surpassing the hand-crafted ones more often on the largest circuits. Our results show that an unmodified frontier LLM can produce working, correct, and competitive shuttling compilers without additional manual algorithmic engineering, thus reducing the development time for new architectures from several months to a few days.

</details>


### [147] [Coincidence free certification and quantification of spatial entanglement with stimulated parametric down conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24718)
*M. G. Damaceno,G. H. dos Santos,N. Rubiano da Silva,S. P. Walborn,P. H. Souto Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用受激辐射替代双光子符合计数来表征光子对源的空间纠缠特性，仅需强度测量即可认证连续横向空间自由度上的纠缠，并通过方差纠缠见证、导引见证和Fedorov比进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通过双光子符合计数来表征光子对源纠缠的方法在对准和光子计数测量上困难且耗时，尤其是在连续空间自由度场景下。本文旨在利用受激辐射和经典强度测量来简化纠缠认证过程。

Method: 在信号模式中注入明亮的经典种子光束，通过受激辐射测量受激闲散场，用经典强度探测替代双光子符合计数。将该方法应用于下转换源的连续横向空间自由度，使用方差纠缠导引见证和Fedorov比来认证空间纠缠。

Result: 仅通过强度测量成功认证了空间纠缠，并通过方差纠缠见证、导引见证及Fedorov比等指标验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为在条件受限（如对准困难、光子计数耗时）场景下研究光子对源的纠缠特性提供了一种实用替代方案。

Abstract: Using stimulated emission, a photon pair source can be characterized by seeding the signal mode with a bright classical beam and measuring the stimulated idler field, thus replacing two-photon coincidence counting with classical intensity detection. We apply this approach to the continuous transverse spatial degrees of freedom of a down conversion source and show that it is possible to certify spatial entanglement with only intensity measurements. We demonstrate this capability through variance-based entanglement and steering witnesses, as well as the Fedorov ratio. This method is useful for studying entanglement properties of photon pair sources in conditions where alignment and photon counting measurements are difficult and time-consuming.

</details>


### [148] [Scalable Variational Quantum Optimization via Pauli Correlation Encoding: Application to Large-Scale Power Demand Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24722)
*Takuya Yoshioka,Keita Sasada,Riku Usuki,Yuichiro Nakano,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于泡利关联编码（PCE）的可扩展变分框架，用于电力需求投资组合优化。通过用泡利关联算子的期望值表示二元变量，实现连续松弛和少量子比特紧凑编码。两阶段混合公式（时间平均初始化+时间分辨优化）在18至10,296规模问题上达到近最优性能（归一化成本差距~10^{-4}）。在俘获离子量子处理器上展示了对噪声和有限采样的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法在组合优化中受限于量子比特资源，难以编码大规模问题。

Method: 1) 泡利关联编码（PCE）：用泡利关联算子的期望值表示二元变量，编码多体关联，实现连续松弛和少量子比特紧凑表示。2) 两阶段混合公式：时间平均问题提供初始化，再进行时间分辨优化。

Result: 在m=18至10,296规模问题上归一化成本差距约10^{-4}，接近最优性能。性能由连续松弛与离散化之间的权衡决定：关联表示的有效分辨率影响连续损失改进转化为离散解的程度，大规模系统表现更一致。在俘获离子量子处理器上对噪声和有限采样具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PCE是一种物理动机驱动、量子比特高效的大规模组合优化框架。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms offer a promising route to combinatorial optimization, but their applicability is limited by the challenge of encoding large-scale problems within restricted qubit resources. In this work, we introduce a scalable variational framework based on Pauli correlation encoding (PCE) and apply it to electric power demand portfolio optimization. Binary variables are represented through expectation values of Pauli correlation operators, which encode multi-body correlations of the quantum state and provide a continuous relaxation enabling compact representations with few qubits. We further propose a two-stage hybrid formulation, in which a time-averaged problem provides initialization for a time-resolved optimization. Numerical simulations demonstrate near-optimal performance across problem sizes ranging from $m$=18 to 10,296, with normalized cost gaps on the order of $10^{-4}$ relative to solutions with certified optimality. We show that the performance is governed by the interplay between continuous relaxation and discretization: the effective resolution of the correlator representation determines how reliably improvements in the continuous loss translate into better discrete solutions, with larger systems exhibiting more consistent behavior. Finally, we demonstrate robustness on a trapped-ion quantum processor, where high-quality solutions are obtained despite noise and finite sampling. These results establish PCE as a physically motivated and qubit-efficient framework for large-scale combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [149] [Classical simulation and model concentration in passive linear optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24728)
*Léo Monbroussou,Hugo Thomas,Hela Mhiri,Zoë Holmes,Elham Kashefi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过表示论框架分析了被动线性光学量子计算中期望值的浓度现象，揭示了浓度与经典可模拟性之间的权衡关系，发现大多数可观测量仍可高效经典模拟，仅存在部分非指数衰减但信号分量较小的量子优势区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计算中，贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）与经典可模拟性之间存在密切联系，但这一权衡关系在玻色子系统（尤其是被动线性光学）中尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于近期发展的随机被动线性光学电路矩的表示论框架，通过将可观测量投影到酉群的不可约表示上并分析其渐近缩放，来表征粒子数守恒可观测量期望值的浓度特性。

Result: 浓度由输入态和可观测量在不可约表示中投影的错位程度决定；识别出可高效经典模拟的可训练可观测量类别；发现Fock态输入和某些可观测量可避免指数浓度，但其大部分信号仍可经典处理，剩余部分虽非指数压制但可通过截断以多项式小误差近似。

Conclusion: 本文提供了系统性搜索既无指数浓度又超越已知高效经典模拟方法的量子优势区域的路径，但当前仅实现了部分分离。

Abstract: Passive linear optics is a restricted model of quantum computation, with complexity-theoretic evidence of quantum advantage for sampling tasks and low losses that make it attractive for near-term algorithms. In qubit architectures, a body of work has revealed a close connection between barren plateaus and classical simulability. Whether an analogous tradeoff exists for bosonic systems remains largely unexplored. Building on a recently developed representation-theoretic framework for moments of random passive linear-optical circuits, we characterize the concentration of expectation values for relevant families of particle-number-preserving observables by evaluating their projections into irreducible representations of the unitary group and analyzing their asymptotic scaling. We show that concentration is governed by the misalignment of the projections into irreducible representations of the input state and the observable, giving a unified representation-theoretic interpretation of generalized entanglement and locality in the bosonic setting. We further relate these concentration properties to existing classical simulation techniques, identifying broad classes of trainable observables that admit efficient classical simulation. Conversely, we identify Fock-state inputs and observables that appear to evade exponential concentration while retaining a polynomially large signal component not accessible to known efficient classical simulation methods. The separation is only partial: most of the signal remains classically tractable, and the residual part, while not exponentially suppressed, is small enough that a truncation serves as a classical surrogate with polynomially small error. Our framework nonetheless provides a systematic route for searching for regimes that unambiguously combine the absence of exponential concentration and lies beyond known efficient classical simulation methods.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum simulacra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24739)
*L. F. Alves da Silva,M. H. Y. Moussa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"量子拟像"概念：通过改变量子系统的度量（从标准L²度量切换为其他度量），可以重新定义可观测量集和实验测量安排，使量子上下文依赖性和微观实在性变得依赖于度量。量子拟像一方面重新调整了量子测量的地位，另一方面提供了实现L²度量无法实现的量子操作的途径，并解释了近期关于非纠缠光子违反贝尔不等式的实验观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索超越标准L²度量的量子系统分析框架，解释传统框架下看似矛盾的量子现象（如可分离光子违反贝尔不等式），并扩展量子操作的可实现范围。

Method: 通过改变量子系统的度量（Hermitian或非Hermitian），重新定义可观测量集和实验测量安排；提出结合正算子取值测度（POVM）与后选择子系综的方案来实现度量重定义的可观测量测量。

Result: 量子拟像可解释近期实验：在L²度量下可分离的光子，在新度量框架下分析时变为纠缠态，从而违反贝尔不等式。此外，量子拟像能够实现L²度量下无法实现的量子操作。

Conclusion: 量子拟像揭示了量子上下文依赖性和微观实在性的度量依赖性，为扩展量子操作和解释非常规量子现象提供了新框架，代价是需要实现度量重定义的可观测量测量。

Abstract: Here we analyze the creation of quantum simulacra: phenomena that emerge from treating a Hermitian or non-Hermitian quantum system in metrics other than the standard $L^{2}$. Changing the metric redefines the set of system observables and thus the experimental arrangement for their measurement, making quantum contextuality and microscopic reality metric-dependent. The simulacra therefore consist, on the one hand, of a resizing of the status of quantum measurement, which has always occupied a central role in quantum mechanics: beyond the connection between quantum and classical dynamics, measurements performed in an appropriate metric can emulate a microscopic reality distinct from that prescribed by the Hamiltonian. On the other hand, simulacra provide a route to implementing quantum operations that lie beyond the reach of the $L^2$ metric. Quantum simulacra offer, as an example, an explanation for the recent observation of the violation of Bell inequalities with unentangled photons [Sci. Adv. \textbf{11}, eadr1794 (2025)]: photons that are separable in $L^2$ metric, become entangled when analyzed within a new metric framework. Simulacrum comes at the cost of implementing measurements of the metric-redefined observables; to address this challenge, we propose a scheme combining positive operator-valued measures with postselected subensembles.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [A rigidity theorem for $π_*$-étale $\mathbb E_k$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22680)
*Xiansheng Li*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于$\mathbb E_{k+1}$-环谱上的$\pi_*$-étale $\mathbb E_k$-代数的刚性定理：$\mathbb E_k$-代数的$\pi_*$-étale扩张范畴等价于其分级同伦Dirac环上的普通étale Dirac代数范畴。证明在合成谱中发展了相对Goerss-Hopkins型障碍理论（含$I$-完备版本）。作为应用，该完备障碍理论构造了Lubin-Tate理论的$I_n$-完备$\mathbb E_3$-$MU_{(p)}$-代数实现，从而得到$\mathbb E_4$-定向$MU_{(p)}\to E_n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立$\pi_*$-étale $\mathbb E_k$-代数与普通étale Dirac代数之间的对应关系，将同伦论中的刚性现象归结为更易处理的代数分类问题。

Method: 在合成谱框架下发展相对Goerss-Hopkins型障碍理论，包括$I$-完备版本，以此证明刚性定理并构造具体实现。

Result: 证明了$\mathbb E_k$-代数的$\pi_*$-étale扩张范畴与分级同伦Dirac环上的普通étale Dirac代数范畴的等价性，并构造了Lubin-Tate理论的$\mathbb E_3$-$MU_{(p)}$-代数实现及$\mathbb E_4$-定向。

Conclusion: 该工作为$\mathbb E_k$-代数的étale扩张提供了完全的分类，并通过具体应用展示了障碍理论在构造高度结构化的环谱定向中的有效性。

Abstract: We prove a rigidity theorem for $π_*$-étale $\mathbb E_k$-algebras over an $\mathbb E_{k+1}$-ring spectrum: the category of $π_*$-étale extensions of an $\mathbb E_k$-algebra is identified with the ordinary category of étale Dirac algebras over its graded homotopy Dirac ring. The proof develops a relative Goerss-Hopkins type obstruction theory in synthetic spectra, including an $I$-complete version. As an application, the completed obstruction theory constructs the $I_n$-complete $\mathbb E_3$-$MU_{(p)}$-algebra realization of the Lubin-Tate theory, hence an $\mathbb E_4$-orientation $MU_{(p)}\to E_n$.

</details>


### [152] [A Cohomological Obstruction to the Existence of $φ$-Equivariant Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23230)
*Thales Fernando Vilamaior Paiva*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究紧李群之间的连续同态φ: G→H，针对G-空间X和H-空间Y，提出了一个过滤的变群障碍，用于判断是否存在φ-等变映射X→Y。并将该理论应用于有限循环群到圆盘的包含映射以及环面之间作用于奇数维球面乘积的坐标态同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在等变拓扑中，判断是否存在与群同态相容的等变映射是一个基本问题。作者旨在建立一套系统的过滤障碍理论，以解决不同群作用下的映射存在问题。

Method: 提出过滤的变群障碍（filtered change-of-groups obstruction），通过群同态φ将G-作用与H-作用联系起来，在空间X和Y之间构造障碍类，逐次过滤来判断映射的存在性。

Result: 成功构建了过滤的变群障碍理论，并将其应用于两种具体情形：有限循环群到圆盘的包含映射，以及环面之间作用于奇数维球面乘积的坐标态同态。

Conclusion: 该理论为判断不同群作用下等变映射的存在性提供了有效的障碍方法，并且通过具体例子验证了其适用性。

Abstract: Let $φ: G\to H$ be a continuous homomorphism between compact Lie groups, let $X$ be a $G$-space, and let $Y$ be an $H$-space. We formulate a filtered change-of-groups obstruction to the existence of a $φ$-equivariant map $X\to Y$. We apply them to the inclusion of a finite cyclic group into the circle and to homomorphisms between tori acting coordinatewise on products of odd-dimensional spheres.

</details>


### [153] [Magnitude homology and Euler characteristics of directed acyclic graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23357)
*Steve Huntsman*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反范畴化的可扩展方法，用于计算有向无环图的量值同调欧拉特征，并将其应用于多层感知器的动态分析，在保持简单子图属性不变的同时恢复了类别判别结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有向无环图（DAG）的量值同调欧拉特征计算缺乏可扩展方法的问题，作者旨在开发一种基于反范畴化（decategorification）的高效计算途径，并将其应用于神经网络分析中，以揭示隐藏在复杂网络结构中的类别判别信息。

Method: 采用反范畴化技术将量值同调抽象为更易于计算的代数不变量，从而实现对有向无环图欧拉特征的可扩展计算。方法包括理论推导、受控实验验证，以及在多层感知器（MLP）动态分析中的应用验证。

Result: 在保持简单子图属性固定的前提下，成功恢复了类别判别性结构，证明了该方法在神经网络动态分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的可扩展欧拉特征计算方法不仅能高效处理有向无环图，还能在多层感知器分析中捕捉到具有类别判别能力的拓扑结构，为图神经网络的拓扑数据分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a scalable approach to computing the magnitude homology Euler characteristic for directed acyclic graphs based on decategorification. Along with motivating mathematical results and some simple controlled examples, we deploy the Euler characteristic in a proof of concept application to the dynamic analysis of multilayer perceptrons, recovering class-discriminative structure while holding simpler subgraph properties fixed.

</details>


### [154] [Odd-primary torsion in the homology of unordered configurations on the torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23459)
*Najib Idrissi,Victor Roca i Lucio*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇数素数 p 下环面 Σ₁ 上 k 个点的无序配置空间 B_k(Σ₁) 的同调群的 p-挠。主要发现：当 k ≤ 2p-1 时无 p-挠；当 k=2p 时，H_{2p-2} 在 p≥5 时存在 p-挠（源于 punctured torus 上的 Bianchi-Stavrou 类），而 p=3 时该类消失；同时 H_{2p} 和 H_{2p+1} 对任意奇素数 p 均无 p-挠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解环面上配置空间的整系数同调的挠结构，特别是素数 p 与点数 k 之间的阈值关系，以及 puncture filling 映射对 torsion class 的影响。

Method: 利用代数拓扑工具，通过 punctured torus 上的配置空间与完整环面配置空间之间的 puncture filling 映射，结合 Bianchi-Stavrou 关于 punctured torus 上阶为 p 的同调类的结果，以及 Napolitano 对 p=3 的计算，进行分类讨论和比较。

Result: (1) 对任意奇素数 p，k ≤ 2p-1 时 H_*(B_k(Σ₁); Z) 无 p-挠；(2) k=2p 时，H_{2p-2} 在 p≥5 时有 p-挠（来自 punctured torus 的 Bianchi-Stavrou 类经填充映射的像），p=3 时该类消失；(3) k=2p 时，H_{2p} 和 H_{2p+1} 对任意奇素数 p 均无 p-挠。

Conclusion: 揭示了环面配置空间同调群中 p-挠出现的精确阈值条件（k=2p, p≥5），并阐明了该挠类的几何来源（punctured torus 上的阶-p 类在填充下的命运取决于 p 的大小）。

Abstract: Let $B_k(Σ_1)$ denote the unordered configuration space of $k$ points in the torus $Σ_1$. For every odd prime $p$, we prove that $H_*(B_k(Σ_1);\mathbb{Z})$ has no $p$-torsion for $k\leq 2p-1$. At the threshold $k=2p$, we prove that $H_{2p-2}(B_{2p}(Σ_1);\mathbb{Z})$ has $p$-torsion if and only if $p\geq 5$. For $p\geq5$, the class is the image under puncture filling of the unique Bianchi--Stavrou order-$p$ class on the once-punctured torus; for $p=3$, Napolitano's calculation shows that this punctured class dies after filling. We also prove that $H_{2p}(B_{2p}(Σ_1);\mathbb{Z})$ and $H_{2p+1}(B_{2p}(Σ_1);\mathbb{Z})$ have no $p$-torsion for every odd $p$.

</details>


### [155] [On Realisability of Twisted Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24462)
*Mark Grant,Michael Jung,Baylee Schutte*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了流形上带扭整数系数的（上）同调类何时能被子流形实现的问题。通过引入扭配边理论和扭Thom空间M^twO(n) over BO(1)，利用参数化同伦论和参数化Pontryagin-Thom构造，得到了可实现的充要条件：Poincaré对偶是扭Thom类的参数化映射像。进一步构建参数化Postnikov塔导出阻碍类，并首次给出了不可定向流形中不可实现的整数同调类例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典问题：流形上的同调类何时能被子流形实现？需结合现代参数化同伦论技术回答。

Method: 引入扭系数配边理论，定义扭Thom空间M^twO(n) over BO(1)；利用参数化Pontryagin-Thom构造建立分类对象；构建参数化Postnikov塔推导阻碍类。

Result: 得到充要条件：扭同调类可实现当且仅当其Poincaré对偶是扭Thom类在参数化映射X→M^twO(n) over BO(1)下的像；给出首个不可定向流形中不可实现整数同调类的例子。

Conclusion: 完整回答了带扭整数系数的同调类实现问题，展示了参数化同伦论在几何拓扑中的新应用。

Abstract: We discuss the question of when a homology or cohomology class with twisted integer coefficients of a manifold $X$ is realised by a submanifold. While this question is classical in nature, providing an answer requires relatively modern techniques from parametrised homotopy theory. More specifically, we introduce cobordism classes twisted by a coefficient system and then define a twisted Thom space $\operatorname{M^\mathrm{tw}O}(n)$ over $\operatorname{BO}(1)$, which serves as the classifying object for this cobordism theory under a twisted Pontryagin-Thom construction. As a result, a twisted homology class is realisable if and only if its Poincaré dual is the image of the twisted Thom class in $\operatorname{M^\mathrm{tw}O}(n)$ under a parametrised map $X \to \operatorname{M^\mathrm{tw}O}(n)$ over $\operatorname{BO}(1)$. Finally, we construct the parametrised Postnikov tower of $\operatorname{M^\mathrm{tw}O}(n)$ over $\operatorname{BO}(1)$ to derive obstructions to realisability and conclude by giving the first known examples of non-realisable integer homology classes in non-orientable manifolds.

</details>


### [156] [Coskeletality and the higher Segal conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24476)
*Philip Hackney*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了单纯集是 d-Segal 的当且仅当它是 (d+1)-余骨架的，并且在最低的两个相关单纯维度上满足 d-Segal 条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化 d-Segal 单纯集的验证条件，将其归结为有限维度的可检验条件。

Method: 通过理论推导，建立 d-Segal 性质与 (d+1)-余骨架性质以及低维 Segal 条件之间的等价关系。

Result: d-Segal 单纯集等价于 (d+1)-余骨架单纯集加上两个最低相关维度的 d-Segal 条件。

Conclusion: d-Segal 条件的验证可以大大简化，只需检查低维条件和余骨架性质。

Abstract: A simplicial set is $d$-Segal if and only if it is $(d{+}1)$-coskeletal and satisfies the $d$-Segal condition in the two lowest relevant simplicial dimensions.

</details>


### [157] [Density-Robust Spherical Coordinates from Persistent Cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24650)
*Nick Nordwald,Inés García-Redondo,Anthea Monod*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个密度鲁棒的球面坐标构建方法，通过拒绝采样获得均匀子样本、计算局部球面坐标，再利用欧几里得松弛的Procrustes问题进行对齐，克服了非均匀采样对持续上同调拓扑坐标的扭曲，同时避免了经典方法的四重内存瓶颈，可扩展至万点规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续上同调构建的非线性拓扑坐标在实际应用中被非均匀采样密度严重扭曲。虽然密度鲁棒的圆坐标已被提出，但球面坐标的扩展面临根本性困难：球面坐标通过球面值映射的非线性变分问题获得，已有的密度校正机制无法直接适用。

Method: 采用子采样-对齐框架：1) 通过拒绝采样获得近似均匀的子样本；2) 在子样本上独立计算球面坐标；3) 将对齐问题建模为球面Procrustes问题，并通过欧几里得松弛获得可计算近似解，保证近似误差有界。

Result: 该方法对非均匀采样具有鲁棒性，在均匀采样下保持经典球面坐标的精度。通过仅在固定大小子样本上计算持续上同调，避免了经典方法中随数据规模增长的四重内存瓶颈，可扩展到10,000点数据集。

Conclusion: 本文成功将密度鲁棒机制从圆坐标拓展至球面坐标，解决了球面值映射非线性变分问题带来的对齐困难，为持续上同调在真实世界非均匀数据上的应用提供了可行的球面拓扑坐标方案。

Abstract: Persistent cohomology provides a principled framework for constructing nonlinear coordinates that reflect the topology of data. However, these topological coordinates can be severely distorted by non-uniform sampling density, limiting their applicability to real-world data. While density-robust circular coordinates have recently been developed, the extension to spherical coordinates remains an open challenge: unlike the circular case, spherical coordinates are obtained through a nonlinear variational problem for sphere-valued maps, to which existing density-correction mechanisms are not directly applicable. In this paper, we introduce the first density-robust construction of spherical coordinates from persistent cohomology. Rather than modifying the coordinate optimization itself, we extend a subsampling-and-alignment framework for circular coordinates to $S^2$, which first computes spherical coordinates on approximately uniform subsamples obtained by rejection sampling and then combines them into a global consensus map. The principal mathematical difficulty is the alignment of independently computed sphere-valued coordinates. We formulate this challenge as a spherical Procrustes problem and establish approximation guarantees for a computationally tractable Euclidean relaxation. Our resulting construction is robust to non-uniform sampling and retains the accuracy of classical spherical coordinates under uniform sampling. Moreover, by computing persistent cohomology only on fixed-size subsamples, our approach avoids the quartic memory bottleneck of the classical spherical coordinate pipeline and scales to substantially larger datasets. We conduct experiments on synthetic data and demonstrate accurate coordinate recovery under severe sampling bias and scalability to datasets of 10,000 points.

</details>

### [158] [Application of flavor moonshine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22744)
*Hirotaka Sugawara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在前期工作的基础上，引入超对称性和模对称性，通过定义不同阶的两值模函数，计算了轻子、夸克及其超对称伙伴的质量。给出了超电子、超d、s、b夸克等质量预测值，其中部分可在KEKB或LHC上测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前期Moonshine在味物理中的应用[1]扩展到包含超对称性和模对称性的情形，以统一计算轻子、夸克及其超伙伴的质量。

Method: 定义不同阶数K的两值模函数（方程(7)），分别用于不同粒子类型：K=1用于带电轻子，K=2用于中微子和上型夸克，K=3用于下型夸克。通过每族一个输入粒子质量，利用模对称性推导所有质量。

Result: 超电子质量~847 MeV（略低于质子质量）；超d夸克质量0 GeV；超s夸克质量5.56 GeV（可在KEKB和LHC测量）；超b夸克质量2193.4 GeV（仅可在LHC测量）。

Conclusion: 该超对称模对称性框架给出了可实验检验的粒子质量预测，部分质量值可在KEK B工厂或CERN LHC的现有加速器上测量。

Abstract: In our previous paper [1], we presented the application of Moonshine to the flavor physics based on the work of Cohn and Deutsch [2] and Bagger and West. Conway and S. Norton [3] and T.Eguchi, H.Ooguri and Y.Tachikawa[4]. We present here the extension of our previous paper [1] by including the supersymmetry and the modular symmetry. We calculate all the lepton masses and the quark masses and their super partner masses by using the one input particle mass for each family. The two valued modular function is defined in equation(7). We define the two valued modular function of degree K in equation (7). We use K = 1 two valued modular function for the charged leptons and k = 2 two valued modular function for the neutrino and for the up quarks and k = 3 two valued modular function for the down quarks. We emphasize that we have three particle masses which can be measured by the existing accelerators such as KEK B factory or CERN LHC. One is super electron mass "Me,s =847 MeV" which is slightly lower than the proton mass. The Super d, s and b quark masses are 0 GeV, 5.56 GeV, and 2193.4 GeV. Super down quark mass is 0 and super strange quark mass can be measured both in KEKB and in CERN LHC. The super bottom quark mass can be measured only in LHC.

</details>


### [159] [Axion isocurvature and the model-building problem of low-scale inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22809)
*Sarunas Verner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了QCD轴子作为暗物质时来自等曲率扰动的广义上限，发现即使轴子构成全部暗物质，暴胀哈勃尺度H_I的上限也可低至2.2×10^7 GeV（θ_i=1时），并结合张量谱导出轴子-利思界，将暴胀子场位移限制在Δφ_CMB/M_Pl≲10^{-7}-10^{-4}，要求势导数极小而二阶导接近-10^{-2}，这有利于混合暴胀、running-mass和拐点模型等独立控制暴胀尺度的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统"预暴胀QCD轴子要求低标度暴胀"的模糊认知，在更一般化的参数空间（暴胀归一化f_I与晚期衰变常数f_a独立、任意暗物质分数、非平凡扰动传输、完整非谐响应）下系统推导等曲率扰动对暴胀尺度及暴胀子动力学的约束边界。

Method: 建立广义等曲率界，将暴胀轴子的标度不变谱与晚期轴子暗物质丰度解耦；考虑任意初始错位角θ_i、anharmonic因子及不同CMB观测数据（Planck、BK15等）的联合约束；进一步将等曲率界与张量谱结合导出"轴子-张量利思界"及"轴子条件利思界"，推导暴胀子场位移和势导数受限条件。

Result: 最宽松CMB约束下H_I<1.25×10^10 GeV（轴子全暗物质，f_I=f_a≤M_Pl，角扰动守恒）；θ_i=1时H_I<2.2×10^7 GeV，hilltop附近更强；轴子-张量联合界要求Δφ_CMB/M_Pl≲10^{-7}-10^{-4}，|M_Pl V'/V|~10^{-8}-10^{-5}，M_Pl^2 V''/V~-10^{-2}；再加热和PQ对称性未恢复进一步加强约束，非最小场景可放松约束。

Conclusion: 预暴胀QCD轴子与高标度暴胀存在尖锐矛盾，等曲率界将暴胀尺度压至远低于传统张量约束，暴胀子必须具有极平缓的势（一阶导极小）但适当的负曲率，这指向混合暴胀等具有独立控制暴胀尺度和退出机制的模型；PQ对称性未恢复和再加热动力学进一步压缩参数空间。

Abstract: The pre-inflationary QCD axion is often said to require low-scale inflation. We derive a general isocurvature bound for a light axion with a scale-invariant spectrum, treating its inflationary normalization $f_I$ independently of the late-time decay constant $f_a$ and allowing for an arbitrary axion dark matter fraction, nontrivial perturbation transfer, and the full anharmonic abundance response. In the minimal benchmark, where axions constitute all of the dark matter, $f_I=f_a\leq M_{\rm Pl}$, and the angular perturbation is conserved, the least restrictive CMB limit considered gives the 95% C.L. upper bound $H_I<1.25\times10^{10}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ on the inflationary Hubble scale. For an initial misalignment angle $θ_i=1$, the bound strengthens to $H_I<2.2\times10^7\,\mathrm{GeV}$, with still stronger constraints near the hilltop. Combining the isocurvature and tensor spectra yields axion-tensor and axion-conditioned Lyth bounds. If the observed curvature perturbation is generated by a canonical cold single-field slow-roll inflaton and the tensor amplitude varies slowly across the observable CMB window, these relations limit the inflaton excursion to $Δφ_{\rm CMB}/M_{\rm Pl}\lesssim10^{-7}\text{-}10^{-4}$ and require $|M_{\rm Pl}V'/V|\sim10^{-8}\text{-}10^{-5}$ while $M_{\rm Pl}^2V''/V\sim-10^{-2}$. This hierarchy favors models with independent control of the inflationary scale, potential derivatives, exit, and reheating, including hybrid, running-mass, and inflection-point models. Reheating and the requirement of Peccei-Quinn non-restoration sharpen these conditions, while nonminimal scenarios can relax them by modifying the primordial fluctuation, its transfer, or the relic abundance.

</details>


### [160] [Agentic Re-Casting using Agentic Re-Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22813)
*Sascha Diefenbacher,Tilman Plehn,Daniel Schiller,Nikita Schmal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用基于MadAgents.v3的智能体系统（含物理学家在环）来更新全局SFitter分析，使先进方法更易用且可推广至其他重铸研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC分析重铸虽然高度标准化，但仍需要大量资源、时间和物理输入，现有方法门槛较高，需要更高效、更易用的方式来进行全局SFitter分析更新。

Method: 基于MadAgents.v3构建智能体系统，采用"物理学家在环"（physicist in the loop）的交互模式，将物理学家引入智能体决策流程，实现SFitter分析的自动化更新。

Result: 成功展示了智能体系统如何更新全局SFitter分析，使得先进的SFitter方法论能够被更广泛的研究人员使用，且该系统可轻松推广至其他重铸研究。

Conclusion: 该智能体重铸研究方法的所有物理和技术方面都可以轻松超越SFitter进行泛化，为LHC分析重铸提供了更高效、更易用的解决方案。

Abstract: Analysis re-casting at the LHC is highly standardized and nevertheless requires resources, time, and physics input. Building on the new MadAgents.v3, we show how a global SFitter analysis can be updated by an agentic system with a physicist in the loop. The agentic interface allows us to make the advanced SFitter methodology available to a wider audience. All physical and technical aspects of this agentic re-casting study can be trivially generalized beyond SFitter.

</details>


### [161] [$\texttt{SNuDD}$: Solar Neutrinos for Direct Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22817)
*Dorian W. P. Amaral,David Cerdeño,Andrew Cheek,Valeria Costa,Patrick Foldenauer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为SNuDD的开源Python软件包，用于计算直接探测实验中的太阳中微子速率谱，并利用该软件包对暗物质实验中的非标准相互作用进行了新限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接探测实验的灵敏度已接近专用中微子实验水平，但缺乏统一工具来计算太阳中微子在直接探测实验中的预期信号，特别是考虑到标准模型外的新物理效应（如非标准相互作用）。作者开发SNuDD旨在填补这一空白，将直接探测实验纳入更广泛的中微子物理研究框架。

Method: 开发了开源Python包SNuDD，计算太阳中微子在直接探测实验中的微分速率谱，涵盖核反冲和电子反冲，同时考虑太阳和地球内的物质效应以及散射截面的修正。利用该工具，结合LZ、XENONnT、PandaX-4T等现有氙基暗物质实验数据，以及未来的XLZD和PandaX-xT探测器，对有效NSI耦合参数施加新的限制。

Result: 当前直接探测实验的灵敏度正迅速接近专用中微子实验的水平；未来的氙基探测器能够提供领先的约束能力。利用SNuDD对有效NSI耦合参数给出了新的限制。

Conclusion: 建议将SNuDD用于未来全局拟合中，将直接探测数据与中微子实验数据相结合，使直接探测实验融入中微子物理学的更广泛领域。

Abstract: We introduce Solar Neutrinos for Direct Detection ($\texttt{SNuDD}$): an open-source Python package that enables the computation of the solar neutrino rate spectrum at direct detection experiments. $\texttt{SNuDD}$ can be used to determine the differential rate for both nuclear and electron recoils within the Standard Model and in the presence of beyond Standard Model physics effects, such as those arising from neutral-current non-standard interactions (NSI). The package accounts for matter effects during neutrino propagation through both the Sun and the Earth and for modifications to the scattering cross sections at the interaction site. We employ $\texttt{SNuDD}$ to place new limits on the effective NSI couplings using results from the xenon-based direct detection experiments LZ, XENONnT, and PandaX-4T, and we project the sensitivity of a future xenon detector based on the planned XLZD and PandaX-xT observatories. We find that current direct detection experiments are rapidly approaching sensitivities comparable to those of dedicated neutrino experiments and that future xenon detectors can provide leading constraints. We recommend that $\texttt{SNuDD}$ be used to combine incoming direct detection data with those from neutrino experiments in future global fits, placing direct detection within the broader landscape of neutrino physics.

</details>


### [162] [Properties of $Ω_{ccb}$ and $Ω_{bbc}$ baryons in a quark-diquark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23072)
*Chaitanya Anil Bokade,Bhaghyesh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在相对论性屏蔽势模型下，采用夸克-双夸克近似，系统研究了Ω_{ccb}和Ω_{bbc}重子的质量谱和辐射跃迁，发现双夸克内部激发的能量低于夸克-双夸克相对运动激发，并分析了不同跃迁模式的主导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论对三重重子（尤其是包含不同重味夸克的Ω_{ccb}和Ω_{bbc}）的能谱和跃迁特性理解尚不充分，特别是双夸克系统的刚性假设需要被检验，因此需要更精确的理论模型来预测其性质，并为未来实验提供指导。

Method: 采用相对论性屏蔽势模型（relativistic screened potential model），结合夸克-双夸克近似（quark-diquark approximation），计算了S波、P波和D波激发态的质量，并分析了辐射衰变宽度。

Result: 双夸克内部激发的能量低于夸克-双夸克相对运动激发，挑战了传统上对重双夸克系统刚性的认知；自旋翻转跃迁在同一轨道多重态内被抑制，而涉及轨道或径向量子数变化的跃迁则占据主导且宽度显著更大。

Conclusion: 该模型为三重重子的激发动力学提供了精细区分，对未来实验数据的解读和进一步理论研究具有重要价值。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the mass spectra and radiative transitions of $Ω_{ccb}$ and $Ω_{bbc}$ baryons within a relativistic screened potential model using the quark-diquark approximation to capture key aspects of their internal structure. The model describes the $S$-, $P$-, and $D$-wave excitations, provides corresponding mass predictions, and offers a comparison with other theoretical approaches. Our findings suggest that excitations involving the diquark occur at lower energies than those requiring relative motion between the quark and diquark, challenging conventional assumptions about the rigidity of heavy diquark systems. Analysis of radiative decays of these states, shows that spin-flip transitions within the same orbital multiplet are suppressed, while transitions involving orbital or radial changes dominate with significantly larger widths. The model offers subtle distinctions in excitation dynamics and highlights its value for future studies and for interpreting potentially upcoming experimental results on triply heavy baryons.

</details>


### [163] [Exact Phenomenology of the Neutrino Cuboid: Normal Mass Ordering, the Tribimaximal Limit, and Cosmological Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23100)
*Jianlong Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何中微子长方体模型，通过质量-混合角对齐（ξ=θ₁₂, ζ=θ₂₃）导出封闭形式的一致性关系。研究发现sin²θ₁₂<1/3要求正常质量排序，而0<Δm₂₁²/Δm₃₁²<1迫使θ₂₃位于低八分象限。JUNO输入给出sin²θ₂₃≈0.440和∑mᵢ≈0.294 eV。研究表明，三双最大点附近的一阶展开对太阳-大气质量分裂比数值不稳定，当前大气角数据与精确对齐仅存在轻微张力，但ΛCDM宇宙学中的中微子质量边界强烈否定该模型。最后将几何对齐残差构建为通用零检验可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个由几何中微子长方体（m₁=m₀sinξ, m₂=m₀cosξsinζ, m₃=m₀cosξcosζ）定义的精确现象学，其立方点对应质量简并和三双最大混合角值。通过施加质量-混合对齐（ξ=θ₁₂, ζ=θ₂₃），建立不依赖于立方极限展开的封闭形式一致性关系，以检验该几何模型与中微子振荡及绝对质量观测数据的兼容性。

Method: 采用几何中微子长方体参数化，设定质量-混合角对齐条件，推导封闭形式的一致性关系。通过分析sin²θ₁₂<1/3和0<Δm₂₁²/Δm₃₁²<1的物理约束，确定质量排序和θ₂₃八分象限。利用代表性JUNO输入进行数值计算，并对比ΛCDM宇宙学边界。构建几何对齐残差作为通用零检验可观测量。

Result: 1) sin²θ₁₂<1/3条件强制选择正常质量排序；2) 在正常排序下，0<Δm₂₁²/Δm₃₁²<1要求θ₂₃位于低八分象限；3) JUNO输入给出sin²θ₂₃≈0.440, (m₁,m₂,m₃)≈(0.0938,0.0942,0.1063)eV, ∑mᵢ≈0.294eV；4) 三双最大点附近一阶展开因前导阶抵消而对太阳-大气质量分裂比数值不稳定；5) 当前大气角数据与精确对齐只有轻微张力，但ΛCDM宇宙学中微子质量边界强烈否定该模型。

Conclusion: 几何中微子长方体模型在精确对齐下与振荡数据仅存轻微张力，但受限于ΛCDM宇宙学的中微子质量上限，其可行性取决于扩展宇宙学模型。提出的几何对齐残差可为未来振荡和绝对质量测量提供系统检验框架。

Abstract: We investigate the exact phenomenology of a geometric neutrino cuboid defined by $m_1=m_0\sinξ$, $m_2=m_0\cosξ\sinζ$, and $m_3=m_0\cosξ\cosζ$, whose cubic point corresponds to mass degeneracy and the tribimaximal values of the solar and atmospheric mixing angles. Imposing the mass-mixing alignment $ξ=θ_{12}$ and $ζ=θ_{23}$, we derive closed-form consistency relations without relying on an expansion about the cubic limit. These relations show that the observed condition $\sin^2θ_{12}<1/3$ selects normal mass ordering and, in the resulting normal-ordering regime, the physical condition $0<Δm_{21}^2/Δm_{31}^2<1$ requires $θ_{23}$ to lie in the lower octant. Representative JUNO-based inputs give $\sin^2θ_{23}\simeq0.440$, $(m_1,m_2,m_3)\simeq(0.0938,0.0942,0.1063)\mathrm{eV}$, and $\sum_i m_i\simeq0.294\mathrm{eV}$. We demonstrate that the first-order expansion around the tribimaximal point is numerically unstable for the solar-to-atmospheric mass-splitting ratio because of a leading-order cancellation. Present atmospheric-angle data yield only mild tension with exact alignment, whereas stringent neutrino-mass bounds in baseline $Λ$CDM cosmology strongly disfavor it; its viability under extended cosmological models remains model dependent. We finally formulate geometric alignment residuals as general null-test observables, providing a systematic framework for testing the neutrino cuboid with future oscillation and absolute-mass measurements.

</details>


### [164] [Testing selectively enhanced QCD axions couplings as an explanation of the RX J1856.5-3754 hard X-ray excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23140)
*Charul Rathod,Madhukar Mishra,P. K Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究磁星（Magnificent Seven）中子星的硬X射线数据，探索QCD轴子转化为光子的解释。发现meV级轴子无法解释观测，而微eV级增强耦合模型与数据更吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Magnificent Seven中子星在2-8 keV能段观测到的硬X射线过剩，探索QCD轴子作为潜在解释。

Method: 采用核子-核子轫致辐射作为基准轴子产生机制，并加入库珀对破缺与形成（PBF）作为附加过程；对比KSVZ轴子模型与选择性增强QCD轴子耦合模型的解释能力。

Result: meV质量QCD轴子无法解释硬X射线观测；微eV尺度的选择性增强耦合模型与硬X射线数据更接近。

Conclusion: 在增强耦合场景下，微eV级QCD轴子可能解释M7孤立中子星在2-8 keV能段的硬X射线发射。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of explaining hard X-ray data obtained from one of the Magnificent Seven (M7) neutron-stars (NSs) employing QCD axion-converted photons. The emission of thermal axions along with neutrinos from the core has been considered. We adopt the nucleon-nucleon bremsstrahlung process as the baseline axion production mechanism and Cooper-pair breaking and formation (PBF) as an additional process. We investigate here whether the selectively enhanced QCD axion coupling model can better explain the hard X-ray excess than the commonly used KSVZ axion model. Our results suggest that meV-mass QCD axions cannot explain the hard X-ray observation within the adopted framework. The enhanced selective model in the micro-eV scale provides closer agreement with the hard X-ray data. We thus conclude that the emission of hard X-rays in the $2-8$ keV range from isolated M7 stars could be explained by QCD axions under an enhanced coupling scenario.

</details>


### [165] [High-frequency gravitational waves from axion inflation in the weak-backreaction regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23152)
*Xun-Jie Xu,Junyu Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子暴胀中弱反作用机制下的规范场放大和引力波产生，特别是非瞬时再加热阶段的贡献，发现该机制可在高频波段产生很强的原初引力波信号，对未来的精密测量和高频引力波探测器发展具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注强反作用（SB）机制以最大化引力波信号，但该机制存在计算复杂性和过产生标量扰动的风险。作者希望在理论上更安全的弱反作用（WB）机制下研究引力波产生，并填补非瞬时再加热阶段这一研究空白。

Method: 作者连续追踪了从慢滚暴胀、再加热到辐射主导时期的完整演化过程，特别关注暴胀结束前后规范场的放大行为。通过分析再加热期间暴胀子振荡导致的不稳定参数ξ符号频繁翻转这一特征，研究了两种螺旋模式规范场的激发。

Result: 发现轴子暴胀可以在弱反作用机制下自然产生高频波段最强的原初引力波信号之一。这一结果对未来测量有效中微子种类数N_eff有重要意义，并强烈激励新型高频引力波探测器的发展。

Conclusion: 弱反作用机制下的轴子暴胀能够产生可观测的高频引力波信号，再加热阶段对引力波产生有重要贡献，因此需要发展新型高频探测技术。

Abstract: Axion inflation, characterized by a Chern-Simons interaction between the inflaton and a gauge field, provides a powerful mechanism for generating primordial gravitational waves (GWs) through tachyonic enhancement of the gauge field. While recent literature has predominantly focused on the Strong Backreaction (SB) regime to maximize GW signals for future interferometers, this regime suffers from computational complexities as well as the risk of overproducing scalar perturbations. In this work, we investigate gauge field amplification and GW production strictly within the theoretically safer Weak Backreaction (WB) regime, with a particular focus on the largely unexplored non-instantaneous reheating phase. Because the tachyonic enhancement during slow-roll typically increases as inflation approaches its end, it is crucial to investigate how the production of GWs behaves at the very end of inflation and thereafter. By continuously tracking the evolution from slow-roll through reheating to radiation domination, we present a complete picture of inflationary and post-inflationary GW production in this framework. A particularly interesting feature of the post-inflationary phase is that the oscillatory behavior of the inflaton during reheating leads to frequent sign-flips of the instability parameter $ξ$, exciting both helical modes of the gauge field. Our analysis reveals that axion inflation can naturally generate one of the strongest known primordial GW signals at high-frequency bands. The yield is relevant for future precision measurements of the effective number of neutrino species, $N_{\rm eff}$, and also strongly motivates the development of novel high-frequency GW detectors.

</details>


### [166] [Quasi-two-body decays $B_c^+ \to χ_{c0,c1} [ρ(K^*) \to] ππ(Kπ)$ in the PQCD approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23163)
*Jun Deng,Xian-Qiao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在微扰QCD框架下首次系统研究$B_c^+ \to χ_{c0,c1} [ρ(K^*) \to] π^+π^0(K^0π^+)$准两体衰变过程。主导的$ρ(770)$道给出$10^{-3}$量级分支比，$Kπ$道被卡比博压制至$10^{-6}$。$χ_{c1}$模的纵向极化分数约94%，且随共振质量增大而降低。$χ_{c1}/χ_{c0}$的$ππ$比值约1.30，与两体结果~4.7形成鲜明对比，表明共振$ρ$产生重塑了P波粲偶素的相对产额。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有$B_c$衰变研究多集中于两体过程，而对涉及矢量介子共振的准两体衰变缺乏系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过研究$B_c^+ \to χ_{c0,c1} ρ(→ππ)$及类似过程，揭示共振结构对P波粲偶素产额的影响机制。

Method: 采用微扰QCD（pQCD）框架在领头阶进行计算。利用Gounaris-Sakurai和相对论Breit-Wigner模型参数化$ππ$和$Kπ$对的P波共振，涵盖$ρ(770)$、$ρ(1450)$、$ρ(1700)$和$K^*(892)$等共振态。

Result: 主导道$ρ(770)$的分支比为$\mathcal{B}(B_c^+ \to χ_{c0} π^+π^0)=3.24×10^{-3}$和$\mathcal{B}(B_c^+ \to χ_{c1} π^+π^0)=4.19×10^{-3}$，其中$ρ$共振干涉贡献约25%。$Kπ$道分支比为$10^{-6}$量级。$χ_{c1}$纵向极化分数约94%且随共振质量增大而下降。$R_{χ_{c1}/χ_{c0}}^{ππ}≈1.30$，$R_{K/π}≈2×10^{-3}$。

Conclusion: 共振$ρ$的干涉效应显著改变$χ_{c0}$和$χ_{c1}$的相对产额，准两体比值1.30与两体结果4.7的差异凸显了共振动力学的重要性。$χ_{c1}$的纵向极化主导特性及$K/π$比值的一致性为理解$B_c$衰变中的强相互作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the quasi-two-body decays $B_c^+ \to χ_{c0,c1} [ρ(K^*) \to] π^+π^0(K^0π^+)$ in the perturbative QCD framework at leading order. The $ππ$ and $Kπ$ pairs are produced via the P-wave resonances $ρ(770)$, $ρ(1450)$, $ρ(1700)$, and $K^*(892)$, parametrized by the Gounaris-Sakurai and relativistic Breit-Wigner models.
  The dominant $ρ(770)$ channels yield $\mathcal{B}(B_c^+ \to χ_{c0} π^+π^0) = 3.24 \times 10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(B_c^+ \to χ_{c1} π^+π^0) = 4.19 \times 10^{-3}$, with constructive $ρ$ interference contributing $\sim 25\%$ of the total. The $Kπ$ channels are Cabibbo-suppressed to $10^{-6}$. For $χ_{c1}$ modes, $f_L \approx 94\%$ dominates and decreases with increasing resonance mass. The ratio $R_{χ_{c1}/χ_{c0}}^{ππ} \approx 1.30$ contrasts with the two-body result $\sim 4.7$, showing that resonant $ρ$ production dramatically reshapes the relative yields of P-wave charmonia. The ratio $R_{K/π} \approx 2\times10^{-3}$ is consistent across $χ_{c0}$ and $χ_{c1}$.

</details>


### [167] [Novel Multilepton Signatures from the Fermionic Portal to Vector Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23166)
*Alexander Belyaev,Manimala Chakraborti,Claire Shepherd-Themistocleous,Chang-Yuan Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了矢量类轻子对产生后通过暗区级联衰变产生的多缪子新信号，重点分析了六缪子通道，利用基于拓扑的重构方法，在HL-LHC上可将矢量类缪子质量探测范围提升至约1.9 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质费米子门户矢量暗物质模型中矢量类轻子对产生后通过暗区级联衰变产生的多缪子新信号，填补LHC上六缪子末态研究的空白。

Method: 基于Drell-Yan过程的矢量类缪子对产生，通过暗矢量V'和暗标量H_D级联衰变产生多缪子末态；开发基于拓扑的重构方法，利用双缪子、三缪子和五缪子共振结构；在探测器层面模拟信号与标准模型背景，包括稀有重味缪子处理。

Result: 六缪子通道在参数空间中信号显著且背景可忽略；现有Run-2多轻子搜索仅覆盖低质量区域；专用六缪子搜索可显著扩展探测范围，HL-LHC下矢量类缪子质量可达约1.9 TeV。

Conclusion: 六缪子通道是对矢量类轻子对产生和暗区物理最有效的探测通道，专用搜索可在未来对撞机实验中大幅提升灵敏度。

Abstract: We perform a collider study of a novel multilepton signature arising from pair production of heavy vector-like leptons followed by cascade decays through a dark sector. In the muonic realisation of the Fermionic Portal to Vector Dark Matter, this process can lead to final states with four, six, eight, or ten visible muons, depending on the dark-sector spectrum and branching pattern. We identify the six-muon channel as the most powerful target: it remains sizeable over a broad region of parameter space, while being less rate-suppressed than the higher-multiplicity channels and much cleaner and more reconstructable than the four-muon final state. The signal arises from Drell--Yan pair production of vector-like muons, $pp\toμ'^{+}μ'^{-}$, followed by decays through the dark vector $V'$ and the dark scalar $H_D$. The six-muon final state receives contributions from symmetric decay topologies in which each $μ'$ yields three visible muons, and from the asymmetric topology in which one chain yields one muon and the other yields five. The six-lepton signature from vector-like-lepton pair production has not previously been explored at the LHC. We therefore develop a topology-based reconstruction which exploits the repeated dimuon, trimuon, and five-muon resonance structure of the signal. We simulate signal and Standard Model backgrounds at detector level, including a dedicated treatment of rare heavy-flavour muons. The resulting background after the six-muon selection and topology reconstruction is negligible. Existing Run-2 multilepton searches already constrain part of the low-mass parameter space, but they do not exploit the repeated resonance structure of the signal. A dedicated six-muon search can substantially extend the reach. At the HL-LHC, the proposed analysis can probe vector-like muon masses up to about $1.9$ TeV for favourable spectra.

</details>


### [168] [A Chromomagnetic Mechanism for the Rotational Phase Transition of Gluonic Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23199)
*Zhibin Li,Yidian Chen,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用全息框架中的旋转-磁场对应，研究纯胶子物质在旋转下的响应。发现真实旋转增强色磁弦张力并提高退禁闭温度，揭示了色磁诱导惯量反转（CII）机制，解释了弱旋转下的反常Barnett效应和负转动惯量现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转作为QCD物质的关键控制参数，但有效模型与格点QCD对退禁闭相变的影响给出冲突性预言，亟需澄清旋转的真实效应。

Method: 采用全息框架下的旋转-磁场对应方法，通过虚角速度格点QCD数据校准，研究纯胶子物质的旋转响应。

Result: 真实旋转增强色磁弦张力并提高退禁闭温度；低温柔弱张力导致负Barnett效应；略高于转变温度时自旋贡献占优产生反常负总转动惯量；强旋转下反常区消失；高温下弦张力完全恢复，常规Barnett行为重建。

Conclusion: 建立了色磁诱导惯量反转（CII）机制，阐明非微扰色磁通量管的熔化和热恢复是反常旋转响应的微观起源。

Abstract: Rotation serves as a pivotal control parameter for QCD matter, yet effective models and lattice QCD yield conflicting predictions regarding its effect on the deconfinement transition. Using a rotation-magnetic correspondence within a holographic framework, we investigate the rotational response of pure gluonic matter. Calibrated against lattice QCD data at imaginary angular velocity, we find real rotation enhances chromomagnetic string tension and raises deconfinement temperature, consistent with lattice QCD analytic-continuation predictions. The temperature dependence of chromomagnetic string tension dominates the system's Barnett response: weak low-temperature tension induces the negative Barnett effect, and slightly above the transition, spin contributions prevail to generate an anomalous negative total moment of inertia. Since growing angular velocity further strengthens chromomagnetic string tension and suppresses spin-dominated inversion, this anomalous regime only survives at weak real rotation and vanishes at large angular velocity. At high temperature, fully restored strong string tension stabilizes conventional Barnett behavior. Stemming from the melting and thermal restoration of nonperturbative chromomagnetic flux tubes, our results establish the chromomagnetic-induced inertia inversion (CII) mechanism as the microscopic origin of this anomalous rotational response.

</details>


### [169] [Status of light inflaton: from inflation to laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23200)
*Niloy Mondal,Shashwat Sharma,Mathew Thomas Arun,Basabendu Barman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在ACT最新宇宙学观测数据以及多种对撞机和强度前沿实验的约束下，研究了轻质量暴胀子（light inflaton）场景的可行性。在四次方暴胀子势的假设下，确定了与ACT观测一致的参数空间，并利用NA62、KOTO、BaBar、Belle、LHCb、MATHUSLA、FASER2、SHiP等实验以及中性介子振荡的结果，推导了暴胀子质量及其与希格斯粒子混合的约束。此外，还探讨了在重加热过程中产生暗物质的前景，同时保持与暴胀可观测量的自洽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ACT等实验对早期宇宙观测精度的提升，以及对弱耦合轻标量粒子的实验搜索不断推进，需要重新评估轻暴胀子模型在观测和实验约束下的可行性，并探索其作为暗物质产生渠道的可能性。

Method: 采用四次方暴胀子势模型，结合ACT的最新暴胀约束，以及来自对撞机和强度前沿实验（NA62, KOTO, BaBar, Belle, LHCb等）对弱耦合亚GeV标量粒子的搜索上限，同时利用中性介子振荡数据，系统扫描暴胀子质量与暴胀子-希格斯混合角参数空间，并计算重加热过程中暗物质的产生量。

Result: 识别出了与ACT观测一致的参数区域，给出了暴胀子质量和暴胀子-希格斯混合的联合约束边界，并展示了在满足暴胀可观测量约束下暗物质产生的可能参数空间。

Conclusion: 轻暴胀子场景在结合宇宙学和实验室数据后，参数空间受到显著压缩，但仍存在与所有现有约束兼容的区域，且在该区域内重加热过程可能产生暗物质，为暗物质候选者提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the viability of the light inflaton scenario in light of the latest inflationary constraints from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT), together with bounds from collider and intensity-frontier experiments searching for a feebly coupled light scalar with a sub-GeV mass. Assuming a quartic inflaton potential, we identify the region of parameter space consistent with the ACT observations and derive constraints on the inflaton mass and inflaton-Higgs mixing using results from NA62, KOTO, BaBar, Belle, LHCb, MATHUSLA, FASER2, SHiP, and neutral meson oscillations. We also explore the prospects for dark matter production during reheating within this framework, while remaining consistent with the inflationary observables.

</details>


### [170] [Faddeev description of baryons in two-dimensional QCD with $N_c=3$. I. Chiral spectrum, isospin, and strangeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23435)
*Ismail Zahed,Haocheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维QCD（N_c=3）中建立了光前Faddeev重子描述，通过精确耦合道表示将夸克-双夸克图像作为单道截断，并利用变分法求解。在手征极限下发现精确零质量重子，激发谱与Webber谱完全一致；扩展到两味简并和奇异夸克情况，系统给出了重子谱、Regge轨迹、Gell-Mann-Okubo关系及Coleman-Glashow关系等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维QCD中建立重子的光前Faddeev描述，超越传统的夸克-双夸克单道近似，发展精确耦合道方法，并系统研究重子谱的结构，包括手征极限、不同味数下的质量和谱系关系。

Method: 基于光前Faddeev方程，构造精确的耦合道表示将夸克-双夸克图像作为单道截断，使用变分法配合精确矩阵元求解。在N_c=3的二维QCD中分别处理手征极限、两味简并和奇异夸克情况，并将结果与DLCQ、玻色化、Webber谱等已知结果对比验证。

Result: 手征极限下存在精确零质量重子，激发谱与Webber谱态态对应，价隙为1.371倍介子质量；零质量重子为单道态，有能隙态为多道态。强耦合下介子-重子质量比与玻色化吻合至0.3-4.3%，弱耦合下价质量与DLCQ吻合至0.04%（介子）和0.15%（重子）。两味简并时零质量重子为Δ-like四重态，N-like双重态为好坏双夸克道精确等权叠加。含奇异夸克时首次得到QCD_2超子谱：Σ-like态低于Λ-like态（与四维排序相反），手征阈值每奇异夸克计算两个Kaon单位，Gell-Mann-Okubo关系在质量平方下成立。三味非简并时质子-like态低于中子-like态，出现Σ^0-Λ混合，Coleman-Glashow关系在百分量级成立。

Conclusion: 光前Faddeev耦合道方法在二维QCD中成功描述了重子谱，揭示了夸克-双夸克图像作为单道截断的近似性质，系统给出了从手征极限到多味情况的重子能谱、通道结构和质量关系，为理解低维QCD重子物理提供了精确的变分解。

Abstract: We develop a light-front Faddeev description of baryons in two-dimensional QCD at $N_c=3$, in which an exact coupled-channel representation identifies the quark--diquark picture as a single-channel truncation, and solve it variationally with exact matrix elements. In the chiral limit we find an exactly massless lightest baryon, in agreement with the classic valence solutions, DLCQ, and bosonization; the excitation tower reproduces Webber's spectrum state by state and sets the valence gap $1.371\,M^{\ast}_{\rm mes}$. The channel content distinguishes these states: the massless baryon is single-channel, the gapped states are genuinely multi-channel, and all baryon Regge trajectories are of the meson class. The calculation is anchored at both ends of the coupling range: the meson-to-baryon mass ratio agrees with strong-coupling bosonization to $0.3$--$4.3\%$, while the valence masses reproduce the full discretized light cone quantization (DLCQ) masses to $0.04\%$ (meson) and $0.15\%$ (baryon) at weak coupling. Extending to two degenerate flavors, we find the massless baryon to be the $Δ$-like quartet, while the $N$-like doublet at $0.977\,M^{\ast}_{\rm mes}$ is an exact $50\!:\!50$ superposition of good- and bad-diquark channels. Adding a massive strange quark, we obtain, to our knowledge for the first time at the valence three-quark level, the hyperon spectroscopy of QCD$_2$: $Σ$-like states fall below $Λ$-like ones, inverting the four-dimensional ordering, the chiral thresholds count two kaon units per strange quark, and the Gell-Mann--Okubo relation is protected in mass squared. With all three quark masses non-degenerate, the proton-like state falls below the neutron-like one, $Σ^0$--$Λ$ mixing appears, and the Coleman--Glashow relation holds at the percent level.

</details>


### [171] [Simultaneous Color Glass Condensate fit to deep inelastic scattering and forward hadron production at HERA, RHIC, and the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23485)
*Piotr Korcyl,Truong My Hau Le,Farid Salazar,Tomasz Stebel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次对HERA的DIS数据和RHIC/LHC的前向单举强子产生数据进行了联合拟合，使用BK演化偶极子振幅，在考虑跑动耦合和运动学约束的条件下，通过每个对撞机引入常数K因子，实现了全局χ²/d.o.f.接近1的拟合效果。发现RHIC所需的K因子约为LHC的两倍，与阈值重求和单圈研究一致。前向强子产生数据约束了偶极子振幅的演化速度，与DIS描述互补。系统不确定性主要来自碎裂函数和因子化标度的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次将HERA的深度非弹性散射（DIS）数据与RHIC和LHC的前向单举强子产生（SIHP）数据在Color Glass Condensate有效理论框架下进行全局联合拟合，以检验BK演化偶极子振幅对两类不同可观测量的统一描述能力。

Method: 采用LO BK方程（含跑动耦合，有/无运动学约束）作为偶极子振幅的演化方程，对每个对撞机引入常数K因子以吸收高阶修正，使用Hessian方法进行完整的不确定性分析，并用蒙特卡洛贝叶斯推断进行验证。

Result: 两种BK方案（有/无运动学约束）均实现了全局χ²/d.o.f.接近1；RHIC的K因子约为LHC的两倍，与阈值重求和单圈研究一致；SIHP数据有效约束了偶极子振幅的演化速度，与DIS数据互补；碎裂函数和因子化标度的选择是主要的系统不确定性来源。

Conclusion: 在CGC有效理论框架下，BK演化偶极子振幅能够同时描述DIS和前向强子产生数据，两类观测量的联合拟合增强了参数约束能力。未来精度分析中需要重点解决碎裂函数和因子化标度带来的系统不确定性。

Abstract: We present the first simultaneous fit to deep inelastic scattering (DIS) reduced cross sections from HERA and forward single inclusive hadron production (SIHP) from RHIC and the LHC in which the dipole amplitude is obtained from Balitsky--Kovchegov (BK) evolution within the Color Glass Condensate effective theory. We demonstrate that, using the LO BK equation with running coupling (with or without kinematical constraint) and a constant per-collider $K$-factor accounting for higher-order corrections, a global $χ^2/\mathrm{d.o.f.}$ close to unity is achieved in both schemes. The required $K$-factors are about a factor of two larger at RHIC than at the LHC, remarkably in agreement with threshold-resummed one-loop studies of forward production. In our setup and with the data available, we find the two observables to be complementary: the SIHP data tighten the constraint on the evolution speed of the dipole amplitude without introducing tension with the DIS description. We further quantify the dependence of the $K$-factors on the fragmentation-function set and the factorization scale, identifying these as the dominant systematic uncertainties to be addressed in future precision analyses of forward hadron production. We perform a full uncertainty analysis using the Hessian method, validated against a Monte Carlo Bayesian inference study.

</details>
