### [172] [Effects of flavor-mixings on charged kaon and pion parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23497)
*Fabio L. Braghin,Parada T. P. Hutauruk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在U(3) NJL模型中研究了带电Kaon和Pion的部分子分布函数（PDFs），引入了一种源于真空极化的新型味道混合相互作用。采用本征时间正则化方案，计算了带味道混合相互作用的能隙方程、介子质量和介子-夸克耦合常数，并与实验数据和JAM分析结果进行了比较。研究发现混合效应强度与夸克有效质量及其差异成正比，在特定x值处效应显著，且在不同能标下混合效应强度相似，有效改善了介子PDFs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的instanton-induced 't Hooft相互作用是研究味道混合的主要机制，但本文旨在探索一种新的源于真空极化的味道混合相互作用对介子PDFs的影响，以更全面地理解味道混合在强子结构中的作用。

Method: 采用U(3) Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL)模型，引入新型味道混合相互作用（源于真空极化而非't Hooft相互作用），使用本征时间正则化方案（有效考虑夸克禁闭），计算能隙方程、介子质量和介子-夸克耦合常数，进而计算带电Kaon和Pion的价夸克分布。

Result: 1) 混合效应强度与夸克有效质量及其差异成正比；2) 对于Pion，主导味道混合效应在μ²=27 GeV²时位于x≈0.3，在μ²=4 GeV²时位于x≈0.8；3) 对于Kaon，效应位于x≈0.2和x≈0.7；4) 两种能标下混合效应强度相似；5) 混合相互作用确实改善了Pion和Kaon的PDFs。

Conclusion: 新型真空极化诱导的味道混合相互作用能够有效改善介子PDFs的理论描述，且这种效应在不同能标下保持稳定，为理解强子结构中的味道混合机制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We investigate the charged kaon and pion parton distribution functions (PDFs) in the U(3) Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, considering a novel flavor-mixing interaction arising from vacuum polarization that differs from the instanton-induced 't~Hooft interaction. In this work, the proper-time regularization scheme is employed, and, effectively, it might take quark confinement into account. The gap equations, the meson masses, and the meson--quark coupling constants are calculated in the presence of flavor mixing interactions. The valence-quark distributions of the charged kaon and pion are calculated and compared with existing experimental data and JAM analysis results at scales $μ^{2} =$ 4 and 27 GeV$^{2}$. The strength of mixing effects on the PDFs is found to be proportional to the quark effective masses and their differences. We further find that the dominant flavor mixing effects in the pion valence up-quark distribution at scales $μ^{2} =$ 27 and 4 GeV$^2$ are around $x \simeq 0.3$ and $x \simeq 0.8$, while for the kaon, the effects are shown at around $x \simeq 0.2$ and $x \simeq 0.7$. We also find similar strength of mixing effects on the PDFs at both scales $μ^{2} =4$ and 27 GeV$^{2}$; however, it certainly improves the pion and kaon PDFs.

</details>


### [173] [Probing collective behaviour of Heavy Quarks through $p_T$-differential radial flow $v_0(p_T)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23499)
*Salvatore Plumari,Maria Lucia Sambataro,Santosh K. Das,Vincenzo Greco*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将粲强子的微分径向流 $v_0(p_T)$ 作为新观测量，通过朗之万动力学与相对论玻尔兹曼输运方法的事件-by-事件模拟，研究重夸克与膨胀夸克-胶子等离子体的相互作用强度。结果表明 $v_0(p_T)$ 在中等 $p_T$ 区间对重夸克输运系数敏感，在低 $p_T$ 区间受强子化影响，且 $Λ_c$ 重子的 $v_0(p_T)$ 大于 $D$ 介子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测量对重夸克与QGP相互作用的约束尚不充分，需要新的敏感观测量来探测重夸克的输运性质。

Method: 采用朗之万动力学与相对论玻尔兹曼输运结合的方法，在事件-by-event框架下模拟粲强子的产生和演化，并比较不同温度依赖的空间扩散系数 $D_s(T)$ 情形。

Result: $v_0(p_T)$ 在中等 $p_T$ 区间对 $D_s(T)$ 的选取敏感，在低 $p_T$ 区间受强子化机制影响，且重子 ($Λ_c$) 的 $v_0(p_T)$ 大于介子 ($D$)。

Conclusion: 粲强子的径向流 $v_0(p_T)$ 可作为约束重夸克输运系数和强子化机制的有效新观测量。

Abstract: We discuss the $p_T$-differential radial flow $v_0(p_T)$ of charmed hadrons within a Langevin dynamics coupled to relativistic Boltzmann transport approach in an event-by-event basis. We propose heavy flavour $v_0(p_T)$ as a novel observable to probe the strength of the interaction of heavy quarks with the expanding Quark-Gluon Plasma. By comparing different temperature dependence for the spatial diffusion coefficient $D_s(T)$ we show that the $v_0(p_T)$ keep a strong sensitivity to the heavy-quark transport coefficients at intermediate $p_T$. At low $p_T$, the observable is also sensitive to hadronization where we observe a larger $v_0(p_T)$ for $Λ_c$ baryons than for $D$ mesons.

</details>


### [174] [Neural Control Variates at LO and NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23591)
*Theo Heimel,Tilman Plehn,Rebecca Revelli,Sophia Vent,Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用神经控制变量法最小化事件权重范围并消除负权重，结合神经重要性采样显著降低LO和NLO预测的计算成本，其中NLO的条件神经控制变量可作为可训练减法项补充物理减法方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决相空间积分和事件生成中事件权重范围过大、负权重导致计算效率低下的问题，提高LO和NLO预测的计算效率。

Method: 采用两个归一化流构建有符号控制变量，结合神经重要性采样实现神经控制变量法，在NLO中引入条件神经控制变量作为可训练减法项。

Result: 最小化了事件权重范围，消除了负权重，显著降低了LO和NLO预测的计算成本。

Conclusion: 神经控制变量法有效提升相空间积分和事件生成的效率，NLO条件变量可作为物理减法方案的补充。

Abstract: We employ neural control variates to minimize the range of event weights and avoid negative weights for phase-space integration and event generation. A signed control variate, built from two normalizing flows, fulfills both tasks. Combined with neural importance sampling, it significantly reduces the computational cost of LO and NLO predictions. For the NLO case, our conditional neural control variate can be viewed as a trainable subtraction term, complementing the established physics subtraction schemes for enhanced sampling performance.

</details>


### [175] [Mass and Decay Properties of Toponium Using SUSY QM Factorization Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23643)
*Bhavishya Tepan,Manan Shah,P C Vinodkumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用超对称量子力学（SUSY QM）因式分解方法研究Cornell势下的toponium（$t\bar{t}$）系统，成功确定了其质量、结合能、自旋依赖的超精细分裂，给出了赝标量态（$\eta_t$）和矢量态（$\Theta_t$）的质量分别为344.114 GeV和344.141 GeV。衰变特性分析显示双胶子衰变通道（$\eta_t\rightarrow gg$）为主导模式，衰变宽度约2.57 MeV。空间特征方面，平均半径、均方根半径和最概然半径均位于0.015–0.025 fm范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采用SUSY QM因式分解方法研究重夸克偶素系统toponium的能谱和衰变性质，旨在精确描述这一最重夸克偶素系统的物理特性。

Method: 应用超对称量子力学（SUSY QM）因式分解方法，结合Cornell势处理toponium（$t\bar{t}$）束缚态问题。

Result: 赝标量态$\eta_t$质量为344.114 GeV，矢量态$\Theta_t$质量为344.141 GeV；$\eta_t\rightarrow gg$衰变宽度约2.57 MeV，为主导衰变模式；空间半径参数在0.015–0.025 fm范围内。

Conclusion: SUSY QM因式分解方法能有效描述toponium系统，给出了合理的质量谱和衰变宽度预测，为未来实验探测toponium提供了理论参考。

Abstract: The Supersymmetric quantum mechanics (SUSY QM) factorization method is applied to study the Cornell potential in the context of toponium $(t\bar{t})$, the heaviest quarkonium system. The method is successfully applied to determine the mass, binding energy, and spin-dependent hyperfine splitting, giving pseudoscalar $(η_t)$ and vector $(Θ_t)$ state masses of 344.114 GeV and 344.141 GeV, respectively. The decay properties of both the pseudoscalar and vector states are also investigated through their respective decay widths, highlighting the crucial role of the bound-state wavefunction. Among the decay channels, the di-gluonic $(η_t\rightarrow gg)$ mode is found to be dominant, with an estimated decay width of 2.57 MeV. In addition, the spatial characteristics of toponium are examined by evaluating the mean radius, root-mean-square (RMS) radius, and most probable radius, all of which are found to lie within the range of $0.015\text{--}0.025\,\mathrm{fm}$.

</details>


### [176] [T-odd transverse momentum dependent gluon distributions for tensor polarized deuteron in a spectator model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23692)
*Xiupeng Xie,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模型，用于计算张量极化氘核内部胶子的T-odd横向动量依赖分布函数（TMDs）。该模型假设一个在壳氘核可以发射一个类时离壳胶子，剩余系统被视为一个在壳旁观粒子，通过谱函数描述其质量分布。通过旁观粒子与出射部分子之间的单胶子交换实现末态相互作用，从而产生非零T-odd函数。研究推导了六个T-odd胶子TMDs的解析表达式，并给出了它们对纵向动量分数x和横向动量k_T依赖关系的数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究张量极化氘核中胶子的T-odd TMDs，这些函数在核子结构和自旋物理中具有重要价值，但目前尚缺乏系统的模型计算和理解。

Method: 采用旁观粒子模型：假设在壳氘核发射类时离壳胶子，剩余旁观粒子质量通过谱函数在连续范围内取实数值；末态相互作用通过旁观粒子与出射部分子间的单胶子交换实现。

Result: 推导出六个T-odd胶子TMDs的解析表达式，并给出了它们随纵向动量分数x和横向动量k_T变化的数值结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型能够有效计算张量极化氘核中胶子的T-odd TMDs，解析表达式和数值结果为理解这些函数的行为提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present a model calculation of the T-odd transverse momentum dependent distribution functions (TMDs) for gluons inside a tensor polarized deuteron. The model is built on the assumption that an on-shell deuteron can emit a time-like off-shell gluon, with the residual system treated as a single on-shell spectator particle. The spectator mass is described via a spectral function, which allows it to take real values over a continuous range. The final-state interaction required to generate nonvanishing T-odd functions are implemented via single-gluon exchange between the spectator and the outgoing parton. We derive analytical expressions for six T-odd gluon TMDs, and present numerical results characterizing their dependence on the longitudinal momentum fraction $x$ and the transverse momentum $\bm{k}_T$.

</details>


### [177] [Radiative decays $J/ψ,\,ψ(2S)\rightarrowγη^{(\prime)}$ in perturbative QCD with relativistic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23737)
*Jun-Kang He,Chao-Jie Fan,Cong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在微扰QCD框架下计算了J/ψ和ψ(2S)到γη^(′)的辐射衰变，包含了所有三种短程贡献（夸克-反夸克、双胶子、QED）的q²阶相对论修正。研究发现振幅对光锥分布振幅和轻夸克质量极不敏感，具有很好的可靠性。相对论修正将J/ψ分支比提高了约2倍，缩小了与实验的差距；但对ψ(2S)修正更大且低阶展开收敛性差。通过引入η_c混合贡献，可解释ψ(2S)速率与数据的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次在微扰QCD框架下系统计算J/ψ, ψ(2S)→γη^(′)辐射衰变中所有短程贡献的q²阶相对论修正，以解决现有理论预测与实验数据之间的差异。

Method: 采用微扰QCD方法，在夸克-反夸克、双胶子和QED三种短程贡献中均引入q²阶相对论修正；使用两种代表性的η-η′混合方案；引入η_c混合贡献作为超出微扰过程的物理机制。

Result: 振幅对光锥分布振幅和轻夸克质量高度不敏感；相对论修正使J/ψ分支比增强约2倍，缩小了与实验的差距；ψ(2S)的相对论修正约为J/ψ的两倍，且低阶展开收敛性差；R_{1S}比值对混合角敏感；η_c混合贡献可解释ψ(2S)与数据的差异但受混合参数敏感性限制。

Conclusion: 包含q²阶相对论修正的微扰QCD计算能改善J/ψ衰变的理论预测，但ψ(2S)需要引入η_c混合等超出硬微扰过程的机制才能与实验数据吻合，表明ψ(2S)衰变中存在更复杂的物理过程。

Abstract: We present the first calculation of the radiative decays $J/ψ,ψ(2S)\rightarrowγη^{(\prime)}$ in perturbative QCD that includes the order-$q^{2}$ relativistic corrections in all three short-distance contributions, namely the quark-antiquark, two-gluon, and QED contributions. The amplitudes are found to be remarkably insensitive to the light-cone distribution amplitude and to the light-quark mass, a robustness that persists through order $q^{2}$ and makes the predictions correspondingly reliable. The relativistic correction enhances the $J/ψ$ branching ratios by roughly a factor of two, narrowing their shortfall from experiment, whereas for the $ψ(2S)$ it is about twice as large as for the $J/ψ$ and the low-order expansion converges poorly. In two representative $η$--$η'$ mixing schemes, the ratio $\mathcal{R}_{1S}=\mathcal{B}(γη')/\mathcal{B}(γη)$ proves sharply sensitive to the mixing angle and favours the smaller of the two. The predicted $ψ(2S)$ rates lie well above the data in both channels, already at leading order, and most severely for the anomalously small $γη$ channel. Such a discrepancy suggests that a mechanism beyond the hard perturbative process is at work. As a physically motivated attempt, we explore the $η_{c}$-mixing contribution, which adds coherently to the perturbative one and is comparable to it in the $γη$ channel, and find that the interference can bring the $ψ(2S)$ rates into agreement with the data, although its extraction is limited by a strong sensitivity to the mixing parameters.

</details>


### [178] [The Higgs boson decay $h \rightarrow bs$ in the NB-LSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23862)
*Cai Guo,Xing-Xing Dong,Zhan Cao,Song Gao,Shu-Min Zhao,Tai-Fu Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在次最小B-L超对称模型（NB-LSSM）中，研究了$\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$味跃迁过程，并在此基础上进一步讨论了在该过程约束下$h \to bs$的Higgs衰变过程。结果表明，$h \to bs$的分支比可能显著偏离标准模型预期，这取决于模型中新参数的值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，具体研究NB-LSSM中Higgs味改变衰变$h \to bs$，并利用$\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$过程进行约束。

Method: 在NB-LSSM框架下，计算$\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$味跃迁过程，并以此为约束条件，进一步分析Higgs衰变过程$h \to bs$的分支比。

Result: $h \to bs$的分支比可能显著偏离标准模型预期，其大小依赖于NB-LSSM中的新参数取值。

Conclusion: 新物理参数对Higgs味改变衰变具有调制作用，为通过味观测量探索标准模型以外的新物理提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: Within the framework of the next to minimum B-L supersymmetric model (NB-LSSM), we investigate the flavor transition process $\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$. Building upon this foundation, we further discuss the Higgs decay process $h \to bs$ under the constraint from the $\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$ process. Our study reveals that the branching ratio of $h \to bs$ can significantly deviate from the Standard Model (SM) expectation, depending on the values of the new parameters introduced in the model. This finding highlights the modulation of new physics parameters on the Higgs flavor-violating decay and provides important theoretical grounds for exploring new physics beyond the SM through flavor observables.

</details>


### [179] [Electromagnetic form factors of singly charmed baryons $Σ_c$ and $Λ_c$ in a covariant quark-diquark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23943)
*Ye Cao,Cheng Chen,Dongyan Fu,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在协变夸克-双夸克模型下系统研究了基态单粲重子（Σ_c^{++}, Σ_c^+, Σ_c^0, Λ_c^+）的类空电磁形状因子，获得了磁矩、电荷半径和磁矩半径。主要发现包括：粲重子的电形状因子随动量转移Q²的衰减比质子慢得多，表明更紧凑的电荷分布归因于重粲夸克作为局域化核心；Σ_c的磁形状因子由光轴矢量双夸克主导，而Λ_c^+的磁形状因子意外地由粲夸克主导，凸显了光双夸克自旋构型在决定磁性质中的关键作用。最后通过类空-类时渐近关系预测了e⁺e⁻→Σ_c\barΣ_c的总截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单粲重子的内部结构和内部动力学，特别是电磁形状因子、磁矩及其半径等可观测量，对于揭示重味重子的夸克结构和相互作用机制至关重要。

Method: 采用协变夸克-双夸克模型，系统计算了基态单粲重子（Σ_c和Λ_c^+）的类空电磁形状因子，并利用类空-类时渐近经验关系预测了e⁺e⁻→Σ_c\barΣ_c的总截面。

Result: 单粲重子的电形状因子随Q²衰减比质子慢得多，表明更紧凑的电荷分布；Σ_c的磁形状因子由光轴矢量双夸克主导，Λ_c^+的磁形状因子由粲夸克主导；理论结果与格点QCD结果定性一致。

Conclusion: 重粲夸克作为局域化核心导致更紧凑的电荷分布；光双夸克的自旋构型在决定磁性质中起决定性作用；预测的截面可在BESIII、Belle II、LHCb及未来Super Tau-Charm Facility等设施上检验。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the spacelike electromagnetic form factors of the ground-state singly charmed baryons, $Σ_c$ ($Σ_c^{++},Σ_c^+,Σ_c^0$) and $Λ_c^+$, within a covariant quark-diquark model. Based on this framework, we obtain their magnetic moments as well as electric charge and magnetic moment radii. Such observables are important to understand the internal structure and the inner dynamics of these heavy baryon states. Our theoretical calculations are in qualitative agreement with available lattice QCD results of $Σ_c^{++}$ and $Σ_c^0$ baryons. We also discuss the mechanism behind the dependence of the numerical results on the charm quark and light diquark. A key finding is that the electric form factors of the singly charmed baryons fall off much more slowly with momentum transfer $Q^2$ than that of the proton, indicating a more compact electric charge distribution, which is attributed to the heavy charm quark acting as a localized core. More importantly, we observe a striking difference in the magnetic structure: while the magnetic form factors of $Σ_c$ are dominated by the light axial-vector diquark, those of $Λ_c^+$ are unexpectedly governed by the charm quark due to the vanishing contribution of the scalar diquark. This highlights the decisive role of the light-diquark spin configuration in determining the magnetic properties. Finally, using an empirical asymptotic relation connecting the spacelike and timelike regions, we predict the total cross section for $e^+e^-\toΣ_c\barΣ_c$. Our findings can be tested at existing facilities including the BESIII, Belle II and LHCb, as well as the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility.

</details>


### [180] [Effects of Axion Interactions on Quark Stars in 4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23948)
*Rui Zhou,Ming-zheng-xuan Wu,Xin-ran Yang,Chong-long Xie,Min Zhou,Zhi-yang Liu,Shi-jun Mao,Guo-yun Shao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合轴子扩展的PNJL模型与4D EGB引力，发现轴子相互作用硬化夸克物质状态方程，提高声速和最大质量；4D EGB修正减弱引力压缩，增大半径和最大质量，降低致密性和红移。两者联合效应使质量-半径序列更符合观测约束，为描述大质量夸克星提供了可行的唯象框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轴子动力学与4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力对夸克星性质的联合影响，检验该组合框架能否更好地解释大质量夸克星的观测数据。

Method: 将轴子扩展的Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (PNJL)微观模型与四维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (4D EGB)引力宏观框架相结合，计算夸克星的结构性质。

Result: 轴子相互作用硬化状态方程，提高声速和最大质量；4D EGB修正减弱引力压缩，增大半径和最大质量，降低致密性和表面引力红移；联合效应使质量-半径序列更符合当前观测约束。

Conclusion: 轴子动力学与4D EGB引力的协同效应为描述大质量夸克星提供了一个可行的唯象理论框架。

Abstract: We explore the properties of quark stars by combining the microscopic axion-extended Polyakov--Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model with the macroscopic framework of four-dimensional Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (4D EGB) gravity. Our results show that the inclusion of axion-induced interactions stiffens the equation of state of quark matter, thereby increasing the sound speed and the maximum mass of quark stars. The inclusion of the 4D EGB correction effectively weakens gravitational compression and further modifies the stellar structure, allowing for larger radii and higher maximum masses while reducing the compactness and surface gravitational redshift. Notably, the combined effects yield mass-radius sequences that are more compatible with current observational constraints than those obtained under standard general relativity with conventional quark-matter equations of state. These findings suggest that the interplay between axion dynamics and 4D EGB gravity may provide a viable phenomenological framework for describing massive quark stars.

</details>


### [181] [Electroweak Baryogenesis: Advances in Sphaleron Rate Calculations and Implications of Thermal Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24026)
*Yanda Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了sphaleron速率的近期研究进展，重点关注电弱重子产生。提供了关于sphaleron和instanton诱导的重子数破坏、非阿贝尔规范理论的真空结构及其他拓扑场构型的教学性介绍，并建议"sphaleron"的中文译名可为"an zi"。还讨论了一级相变对对撞机搜索和原初黑洞形成的广泛影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理sphaleron速率计算的最新进展，为电弱重子产生研究提供理论基础，并促进相关拓扑概念的理解与中文术语的规范化。

Method: 综述近期文献，从规范场论的真空结构出发，阐述sphaleron和instanton诱导的重子数破坏机制，并介绍一级相变的相关物理后果。

Result: 总结了sphaleron速率计算的最新进展，提出了"sphaleron"的中文译名建议"an zi"，并阐明了一级相变在对撞机物理和原初黑洞形成中的潜在影响。

Conclusion: sphaleron速率研究对理解宇宙重子数不对称性至关重要，相关概念的教学整理和术语规范化有助于推动该领域发展，一级相变的研究则具有跨领域的物理意义。

Abstract: This thesis reviews recent advances in calculating the sphaleron rate, with particular emphasis on electroweak baryogenesis. It also provides pedagogical introductions to sphaleron- and instanton-induced baryon-number violation, the vacuum structure of non-Abelian gauge theories, and other topological field configurations, and suggests "an zi" as a possible Chinese term for "sphaleron" (see p. 3). Broader implications of first-order phase transitions for collider searches and primordial black hole formation are also discussed.

</details>


### [182] [Quantum entanglement within quarkonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24068)
*Wenyu Zhang,Yiyu Zhou,Yang Li,Qun Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非微扰光前哈密顿框架下研究重夸克偶素中的夸克-反夸克纠缠，证明了自旋0和自旋1夸克偶素的纠缠熵分别与未极化TMD和极化/张量极化TMD相关，并通过BLFQ方法计算了粲偶素和底偶素的纠缠熵，揭示了矢量介子极化敏感性和红外参数匹配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子信息度量（纠缠熵）作为非微扰夸克偶素结构的新型探针，建立量子纠缠与部分子观测量之间的直接联系。

Method: 在非微扰光前哈密顿框架下，通过对强子态矢量中的反夸克自由度求迹，构建夸克子系统的约化密度矩阵并计算冯·诺依曼熵；利用基光前量子化（BLFQ）方法获得光前波函数；通过谐振子表示匹配动量空间熵中的红外参数。

Result: 自旋0夸克偶素的纠缠熵可简化为未极化横动量依赖部分子分布（TMD）的香农熵（加上常数色和自旋贡献）；自旋1夸克偶素的纠缠熵具有显式极化依赖性，并与极化及张量极化TMD相关联；BLFQ计算表明纠缠熵对矢量介子的极化高度敏感；实现了红外参数的解析匹配。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵可作为非微扰夸克偶素结构的新型探针，建立了量子信息度量与部分子可观测量之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We investigate quark-antiquark entanglement in heavy quarkonium within a nonperturbative light-front Hamiltonian framework. By tracing over the antiquark degrees of freedom in the hadronic state vector, we construct the reduced density matrix of the quark subsystem and compute the associated von Neumann entropy. For spin-0 quarkonia, we show that this entropy reduces to the Shannon entropy of the unpolarized transverse momentum dependent parton distribution (TMD), up to constant color and spin contributions. For spin-1 quarkonia, we derive the explicit polarization dependence of the entropy and connect it to polarized and tensor-polarized TMDs. Using light-front wave functions obtained via basis light-front quantization (BLFQ), we evaluate the entanglement entropy for charmonium and bottomonium states, revealing a pronounced sensitivity to the polarization of vector mesons. Furthermore, we resolve the infrared parameter by matching the momentum-space entropy to a harmonic-oscillator representation. Ultimately, these results establish entanglement entropy as a novel probe of nonperturbative quarkonium structure, forging a direct link between quantum information measures and partonic observables.

</details>


### [183] [Prospects for observing the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ decay at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24203)
*Yueling Yang,Bingbing Yang,Junfeng Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了LHC上观测到的赝标量顶夸克偶素η_t的W+W-衰变过程，估计了分支比，发现未来HL-LHC实验中可通过双轻子末态通道观测到数十个信号事件，并提出了无b喷注、干净双轻子特征的新搜索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LHC实验观测到了赝标量顶夸克偶素η_t，其性质引起持续关注，需要系统研究其特征衰变过程。

Method: 基于两种衰变常数f_{η_t}方案估计η_t→W+W-的分支比，并考虑带电轻子识别效率，利用完整MC模拟分析信号可观测量。

Result: 在HL-LHC可获得超过3×10^7个η_t事件的条件下，η_t→W+W-→ℓ⁻ν̄_ℓℓ'⁺ν_{ℓ'}衰变链可产生数十个双轻子末态信号事件。

Conclusion: η_t→W+W-→ℓ⁻ν̄_ℓℓ'⁺ν_{ℓ'}通道具有无b喷注、干净双轻子特征，可作为互补且更灵敏的η_t探针。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of the pseudoscalar toponium $η_{t}$ at the LHC and by the ongoing interest in its properties, we phenomenologically investigate the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ process, one of the characteristic decays of toponium $η_{t}$. The branching ratio for the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ decay is estimated with two scenarios of the decay constant $f_{η_{t}}$. It is found that tens of dilepton events from the cascade decays, $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ ${\to}$ ${\ell}^{-}\barν_{\ell}{\ell}^{{\prime}+}ν_{{\ell}^{\prime}}$ with ${\ell}$, ${\ell}^{\prime}$ $=$ $e$ and $μ$, could be experimentally observed, when considering the realistic identification efficiency of the charged lepton and assuming more than $3{\times}10^{7}$ $η_{t}$ events available at the future HL-LHC. We propose searching for $η_{t}$ via the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ ${\to}$ ${\ell}^{-}\barν_{\ell}{\ell}^{{\prime}+}ν_{{\ell}^{\prime}}$ channel, which features no $b$-jets and clean dilepton signatures, thus offering a complementary and potentially more sensitive probe.

</details>


### [184] [Studying the tensor resonance contributions in $B \to PP\ell^+\ell^-$ and $B \to PV\ell^+\ell^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24315)
*Ru-Min Wang,Xiu-Ping Fan,Si-Yu Xu,Yi Qiao,Xiao-Dong Cheng,Yuan-Guo Xu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于SU(3)味对称性分析了B介子到轻张量介子（T）的轻子衰变，并通过窄宽度近似和有限宽度效应预测了B→T(→PP)ℓ⁺ℓ⁻和B→T(→PV)ℓ⁺ℓ⁻的分支比。有限宽度效应在近阈值模式中显著，且张量共振的有限宽度可在阈下通道打开非零贡献。与已有测量相比，张量共振贡献较小，提示其他共振态可能主导相关衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用SU(3)味对称性系统研究B→Tℓ⁺ℓ⁻及其级联衰变，通过实验数据约束非微扰参数，并考察张量共振在稀有衰变中的贡献大小。

Method: 基于SU(3)味对称性关联不同衰变道的强子振幅；利用B⁰_s→f′₂(1525)μ⁺μ⁻的实验分支比拟合非微扰参数；采用窄宽度近似并进一步引入有限宽度效应处理中间张量共振的传播。

Result: 得到了三种情况下B→Tℓ⁺ℓ⁻衰变的分支比；有限宽度效应在大多数道中略微压低分支比，但在近阈值模式中影响显著；阈下K₂*(1430)→Kη'道因有限宽度获得非零贡献；张量共振贡献普遍小于矢量、标量等共振态。

Conclusion: 张量共振对B→PPℓ⁺ℓ⁻和B→PVℓ⁺ℓ⁻的贡献较小，其他共振（矢量、标量、轴矢等）可能起主导作用，结果可供未来实验检验。

Abstract: We analyze the semileptonic $B \to T\ell^+\ell^-$, $B \to T(\to PP)\ell^+\ell^-$, and $B \to T(\to PV)\ell^+\ell^-$ decays with $\ell=e,μ,τ$ based on flavor SU(3) analysis in the standard model ($T$ denotes the light tensor meson, $P$ denotes the light pseudoscalar meson, and $V$ denotes the light vector meson). The hadronic amplitudes of the $B \to T\ell^+\ell^-$ decays are related by the nonperturbative parameters, and all branching ratios of the $B \to T\ell^+\ell^-$ decays are obtained by the experimental data of the branching ratio of $B^0_s\to f^{\prime}_{2}(1525)μ^+μ^-$ in three cases, and then the branching ratios of the $B \to T(\to PP)\ell^+\ell^-$ and $B \to T(\to PV)\ell^+\ell^-$ decays are predicted by the narrow width approximation and further considering finite width effects of the intermediate resonances. Compared with the narrow width results, the finite width effects slightly reduce the branching fractions for most decays. However, sizeable finite width effects are found in some near threshold modes. For the subthreshold $K_2^*(1430)\to Kη'$ relevant channels, the finite width of the tensor resonance can open a nonzero contribution. Compared with the measured $B \to PP\ell^+\ell^-$ and $B \to PV\ell^+\ell^-$ decays, we find that the branching ratios with the tensor resonance states are small. Therefore, other resonances, for example, the vector mesons, the scalar mesons, the axial-vector mesons or their excited states, might give the dominant contributions to the relevant decays. Our results might be tested in current and future experiments.

</details>


### [185] [Ultraviolet boundary condition and the Higgs mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24329)
*Jinku Guo,Junqiang Bai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在出现论框架下，以普朗克尺度下希格斯四次耦合为零为唯一边界条件，利用完整两圈标准模型计算得到希格斯质量为121.99 GeV，比实验测量值125.20 GeV低3.21 GeV（2.6%），理论不确定度约为±1.5 GeV。该假设在现有精度下未被排除，但需要三圈计算和更精确的顶夸克质量测量来进一步检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验出现论框架下最简单的紫外边界条件——普朗克尺度下希格斯四次耦合为零这一假设，是否与实验测量的希格斯质量一致。

Method: 采用完整两圈重整化群演化与两圈阈值匹配计算，以λ(M_P)=0为唯一边界条件，所有其他输入由实验固定，计算希格斯质量。

Result: 计算得到m_h=121.99 GeV，比实验值125.20 GeV低3.21 GeV（2.6%），理论不确定度±1.5 GeV来自未知三圈效应。

Conclusion: 该假设在现有精度下有意义且未被排除，但需要完整三圈计算和改进的顶夸克质量测量来进行更严格的检验。

Abstract: Within the emergence framework, in which infrared physics is not fixed by ultraviolet Lagrangian parameters, the hypothesis that the Higgs quartic coupling vanishes at the Planck scale is tested within the full two-loop Standard Model. With lambda(M_P) = 0 imposed as the sole boundary condition and all other inputs fixed by experiment, a Higgs mass of m_h = 121.99 GeV is obtained from complete two-loop renormalisation-group evolution and full two-loop threshold matching. This lies 3.21 GeV (2.6%) below the measured value of 125.20 GeV. The dominant theoretical uncertainty, +/- 1.5 GeV, arises from unknown three-loop effects and is estimated by perturbative power counting. The calculation tests the simplest ultraviolet boundary condition consistent with the emergence framework of Ref. [1]. The agreement is meaningful at the available precision, since the hypothesis could easily have been excluded by a wide margin. A sharper test requires a complete three-loop calculation and an improved top-quark mass measurement.

</details>


### [186] [A High-Precision Numerical Framework for Time-Varying Solar Neutrino Flux with Full Earth Matter Oscillation Corrections for Global Underground Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24421)
*Keyu Han,Isabella Yin,Kaoru Yagi,Kevin Yifan Jiang,Xiangpan Ji,Shaomin Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一、高效的计算框架，用于高精度预测太阳中微子通量，该框架整合了地日距离变化、地球物质效应（1D/3D电子密度模型）以及基于Strang分裂的快速MSW传播算法，并给出了包括中国锦屏地下实验室（CJPL）在内的多个地下实验室的站点特定预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳中微子研究已进入高精度测量时代，需要亚百分比精度的理论预测才能进行有意义的比较，但现有计算框架在精度和效率上尚不能满足这一需求。

Method: 提出了一个统一的计算框架，包含：(i) 时变地日距离；(ii) 基于1D和3D地球电子密度分布的地球物质效应建模；(iii) 基于Strang分裂算法的快速MSW中微子传播机制实现，支持中微子轨迹和能量网格的大规模快速扫描。

Result: 给出了针对中国锦屏地下实验室（CJPL）以及其他从事太阳中微子项目的多个地下实验室的站点特定通量预测。

Conclusion: 该框架能够以高保真度和计算效率生成太阳中微子通量预测，满足当前高精度实验对理论预测亚百分比精度的需求。

Abstract: Solar neutrinos have been studied for over half a century to test both the Standard Solar Model and the electroweak sector of the Standard Model of particle physics. Contemporary experiments are now entering an era of high-precision measurements, demanding corresponding theoretical predictions with sub-percent accuracy to enable meaningful comparison. In this paper, we identify and analyze the essential physical and computational components required to compute solar neutrino fluxes with high fidelity, and present a unified, computationally efficient framework. This framework incorporates: (i) the time-varying Earth-Sun distance; (ii) Earth matter effects modeled using both one-dimensional (1D) and three-dimensional (3D) Earth electron-density profiles; and (iii) a fast, Strang-splitting-based implementation of the Mikheyev-Smirnov-Wolfenstein (MSW) neutrino propagation formalism, enabling rapid, large-scale scans over neutrino trajectories and energy grids. We deliver site-specific predictions for the China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL) and other underground laboratories actively engaged in solar neutrino programs.

</details>


### [187] [Revisiting relativistic corrections to inclusive $J/ψ$ production at B factories: Complete expansion for three-body quarkonium production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24432)
*Sheng-Juan Jiang,Sai Cui,Guang-Zhi Xu,Kui-Yong Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新计算了B工厂中J/ψ产生的相对论修正，通过完整的运动学展开得到自洽的截面结果。发现O(v²)修正对J/ψ+X_{c\bar{c}}道抑制约14.55%，对J/ψ+X_{non-c\bar{c}}道增强约12.18%。即使加入现有已知修正，理论与实验仍存在张力，需要更完整的微扰计算来解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对B工厂J/ψ产生的相对论修正进行重新计算，解决此前计算中存在的理论不自洽和依赖积分变量选择的问题，并试图缓解当前理论预言与实验测量之间的持续张力。

Method: 对具有三体末态的夸克级子过程进行完整的末态运动学参数展开，采用自洽的积分方法消除变量选择依赖；在计算中纳入O(v²)相对论修正，并与碎裂函数方法进行高能极限下的对比验证。

Result: O(v²)修正使J/ψ+X_{c\bar{c}}道截面抑制约14.55%，使J/ψ+X_{non-c\bar{c}}道截面增强约12.18%；高能极限下截面大小及修正曲线形状与碎裂函数计算高度一致。

Conclusion: 即使纳入O(α_s)修正、色八重态贡献、双光子产生道和feed-down效应，理论与实验的张力依然存在，需进行完整到O(α_s, v²)的瞬子偶素计算或引入更高阶修正才能解决。

Abstract: We revisit the calculations of relativistic corrections to inclusive $J/ψ$ production at B factories. For quark-level subprocesses with three-body final states, we carry out a full expansion of the final-state kinematic parameters. The resulting cross sections are theoretically self-consistent and independent of the choice of integration variables. In the high-energy limit, both cross-section magnitudes and the line shapes of correction curves for energy and momentum distributions agree remarkably well with fragmentation-function calculations. We find that $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ corrections suppress the cross section by roughly $14.55\%$ in the $J/ψ+ X_{c\bar{c}}$ channel and enhance it by approximately $12.18\%$ for the $J/ψ+ X_{\text{non-}c\bar{c}}$ channel. After incorporating published $\mathcal{O}(α_s)$ corrections, color-octet contributions, two-photon production channels, and feed-down effects, tension persists between theoretical predictions and experimental measurements. This indicates that complete prompt-quarkonium calculations through $\mathcal{O}(α_s, v^2)$, or evaluations incorporating higher-order corrections, are required to resolve this tension.

</details>


### [188] [Constraints from the SM-like Higgs boson in a flavor-dependent $U(1)$ extension of the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24446)
*Duong Van Loi,N. T. Duy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在依赖味型的U(1)_X扩展标准模型中，对类希格斯玻色子H进行了现象学研究，分析了H与标准模型粒子及新带电标量玻色子的相互作用，并研究了单圈级H→γγ衰变参数κγ。结果表明模型预测的κγ值与ATLAS和CMS在1σ水平上的约束一致，同时满足味、对撞机和暗物质研究的多重边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索依赖味型的U(1)_X扩展标准模型中类希格斯玻色子的现象学，检验该模型是否能在满足现有实验约束的前提下，对H→γγ衰变给出符合ATLAS和CMS测量结果的预测。

Method: 在依赖味型的U(1)_X扩展标准模型中，计算H与标准模型粒子及新带电标量玻色子的相互作用；通过单圈级计算分析H→γγ衰变参数κγ，并与ATLAS和CMS的实验约束进行对比；同时结合味物理、对撞机数据和暗物质研究的多重边界条件进行一致性检验。

Result: 模型预测的κγ值与ATLAS和CMS在1σ置信水平上的约束一致，并且满足味物理、对撞机以及暗物质研究的相关边界条件。

Conclusion: 依赖味型的U(1)_X扩展标准模型能够在味、对撞机和暗物质的多重约束下，对H→γγ衰变给出与实验观测一致的预测，为超出标准模型的新物理提供了可行的候选方案。

Abstract: This work presents a phenomenological study of the Standard Model-like Higgs boson $H$ in a flavor-dependent $U(1)_X$ extension of the Standard Model, where the $X$ charge is assigned according to fermion flavors. In particular, we analyze the interactions of $H$ with Standard Model particles as well as with new charged scalar bosons. In addition, the parameter $\ka_{\ga}$, which characterizes the $H\to \ga\ga$ decay at the one-loop level, is investigated. The results show that the model predicts $\ka_{\ga}$ values consistent with the ATLAS and CMS constraints at the $1σ$ level, while satisfying several bounds derived from flavor, collider and dark matter studies.

</details>


### [189] [Generation-separated hypercharges and dark charges: origin of flavor, neutrino masses, and dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24452)
*Duong Van Loi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含代分离超荷和暗荷的框架，通过扩展的U(1)规范对称性自发破缺到标准模型U(1)_Y，利用涉及超子场的高维算符产生带电费米子的层级质量和小夸克混合。研究了四种超子实现方案，重点分析了通过跷跷板或暗跷跷板机制产生中微子质量，并利用暗规范对称性破缺剩余的Z_2对称性稳定暗物质候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型中带电费米子质量层级、小夸克混合、中微子质量起源以及暗物质存在等未解之谜，试图在一个统一框架内同时解释这些现象。

Method: 引入代分离的超荷对称性U(1)_Y1⊗U(1)_Y2⊗U(1)_Y3和暗规范对称性U(1)_D，通过自发对称性破缺得到标准模型超荷。利用超子场（hyperons）的高维算符产生费米子质量层级，通过跷跷板或暗跷跷板机制产生中微子质量，暗对称性破缺残余的Z_2对称性稳定暗物质。

Result: 展示了多种超子扇区实现方案，其中四种超子的实现可同时容纳微小中微子质量和大的中微子混合角，暗物质候选者（费米子或标量）在TeV能级有符合观测暗物质余量和直接探测约束的参数空间。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在一个自洽的扩展规范模型中同时解释带电费米子质量层级、中微子质量与混合以及暗物质问题，具有较好的理论一致性和现象学可行性。

Abstract: We study a framework with generation-separated hypercharges and dark charges. After the extended hypercharge symmetry $U(1)_{Y1}\otimes U(1)_{Y2}\otimes U(1)_{Y3}$ is spontaneously broken to the Standard Model $U(1)_Y$, hierarchical masses of charged fermions and small quark mixing can arise from higher-dimensional operators involving hyperon fields. Several realizations with different hyperon sectors are presented to illustrate possible flavor structures. Focusing on the realization with four hyperons, we examine neutrino mass generation through either a seesaw or a scotoseesaw mechanism, which can accommodate tiny neutrino masses and large neutrino mixing. The breaking of the dark gauge symmetry $U(1)_D$ leaves a residual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry that stabilizes dark matter, allowing either fermionic or scalar candidates with viable TeV-scale parameter regions consistent with the observed dark matter relic abundance and current direct detection constraints.

</details>


### [190] [Implications of $K\toπν\barν$ for new physics in $B$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24494)
*Lukas Allwicher,Marzia Bordone*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: NA62合作组对$K^+\toπ^+ν\barν$分支比的新测量是精密味物理的重要里程碑。本研究探讨该结果在两种新物理假设（Z耦合修正和半轻子相互作用效应）下对相关观测量（如$B\to Kν\barν$、$B_s\toμμ$）的影响，并分析其对未测量的$K_L\toπ^0ν\barν$模式的 implications。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NA62合作组最新测量的$K^+\toπ^+ν\barν$分支比是精密味物理领域的突破性进展，为新物理探索提供了关键实验约束。

Method: 在两种新物理假设下分析：①Z玻色子耦合修正；②半轻子相互作用效应。通过味物理关联性，将$K^+\toπ^+ν\barν$与其他味改变观测量（$B\to Kν\barν$、$B_s\toμμ$）建立联系。

Result: 基于NA62新数据，在两种新物理场景下对相关观测量给出了定量约束，并预测了$K_L\toπ^0ν\barν$分支比的预期范围。

Conclusion: NA62的精确测量为超越标准模型的新物理提供了重要限制，结合其他味物理观测量可有效甄别不同的新物理场景，并为未来$K_L\toπ^0ν\barν$实验测量提供理论导向。

Abstract: The new measurement of the $K^+\toπ^+ν\barν$ branching ratio by the NA62 collaboration represents an important milestone in precision flavour physics. Under different flavour assumptions on beyond the standard model physics, this decay mode can be related to many other observables with underlying flavour-changing currents, such as $B\to Kν\barν$ or $B_s\toμμ$, effectively providing an additional handle in our exploration of flavour-violating new physics effects. We study the impact of this new result, under the hypotheses that deviations from the standard model arise from modified $Z$ couplings, or from effects in semileptonic interactions. Finally, we review the implications of our scenarios for the yet to be measured partner mode $K_L\toπ^0ν\barν$.

</details>


### [191] [Light Dark Matter Discovery Potential and Model Selection at LDMX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24524)
*Adam Berger,Riccardo Catena,Jan Conrad,Taylor R. Gray*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了LDMX实验在轻暗物质搜索中的发现潜力，包括沿热 relic 目标四个基准点的5σ发现能力、90%置信水平排除限、参数估计能力，以及通过贝叶斯因子区分不同暗扇区假设（如暗光子与高阶电磁矩相互作用）的统计鉴别力，建立了可直接应用于真实数据的似然框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻暗物质是解释观测到的暗物质丰度的重要候选方案，但尚未被直接探测到。LDMX实验通过高能电子-核碰撞中反冲电子的能量和横动量测量来搜索轻暗物质，亟需评估其发现潜力和模型区分能力。

Method: 基于似然的统计框架，结合信号与背景建模及系统误差，采用频率学派和贝叶斯方法。使用二维反冲电子分布（归一化编码耦合强度，形状编码暗光子质量）进行参数估计，并利用贝叶斯因子进行模型比较。

Result: LDMX在两种背景假设下沿 relic 目标均具有强5σ发现潜力；计算了90%置信水平排除限；在模拟数据中两个参数均能在不确定度内恢复；二维分析相比一维分析具有显著更强的模型鉴别力。

Conclusion: LDMX对轻暗物质具有强大的发现潜力和参数推断能力，其二维分布分析能够有效区分不同暗扇区模型，所建立的统计框架可直接应用于真实实验数据。

Abstract: Light dark matter (DM) is a compelling scenario for the observed relic abundance, with accelerator-based searches as a powerful discovery strategy. The upcoming Light Dark Matter eXperiment (LDMX) is designed to probe light DM by measuring the energy and transverse momentum of recoil electrons in high-intensity electron-nucleus collisions. We evaluate the discovery potential of LDMX to light DM at four benchmark points along the thermal target for complex scalar DM mediated by a dark photon, for different background assumptions, and assess its model selection power at a representative benchmark. We find that LDMX has strong projected 5$σ$ discovery potential along the relic target across both background scenarios for certain benchmarks, and we further compute projected 90% C.L. exclusion limits assuming no measured signal events. The normalization and shape of the two-dimensional recoil electron distribution encodes the coupling and dark photon mass, respectively, enabling parameter inference in the event of a signal excess. We perform parameter estimation on simulated data and find that both parameters are recovered within their uncertainties along the relic target. We assess whether the data can distinguish between competing dark sector hypotheses, in particular, dark photons with additional higher electromagnetic moment interactions. We demonstrate that model comparison using the Bayes factor allows dark sector hypotheses to be statistically distinguished, with the two-dimensional analysis affording substantially greater discriminating power than the one-dimensional analysis. These results are obtained within a likelihood-based statistical framework, incorporating signal and background modelling with their associated systematic uncertainties and employing both frequentist and Bayesian methods. The framework is designed for direct application to real LDMX data.

</details>


### [192] [Decay constants of the two-pole $D_0^*(2300)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24550)
*Qi-Wei Yuan,Jia-Ting Zhang,Ming-Zhu Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过有效拉格朗日方法计算了标量粲介子D_0^*(2300)双极点结构中的衰变常数，发现其值远小于传统c\bar q激发态预言，并基于因子化方法预言了B_s→D_s D_0^*等衰变分支比，为验证该介子的双极点解释提供了关键检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D_0^*(2300)的标量粲介子性质是强子谱学中最引人注目的问题之一，其内部结构存在争议，需通过新的观测量加以区分。

Method: 采用有效拉格朗日方法，在D_0^*(2300)由Dπ、Dη、D_s\bar K和Dη'耦合道产生的双极点图像下，计算两个极点的衰变常数；并利用因子化方法预言相应重子/介子衰变的分支比。

Result: 得到低极点衰变常数f_D_0^*(lower)=64.6^{+0.9}_{-1.0} MeV，高极点f_D_0^*(higher)=80.8^{+9.6}_{-5.3} MeV，均显著小于传统c\bar q激发态的预期值。

Conclusion: 衰变常数可作为区分D_0^*(2300)内部结构的灵敏探针；所预言的分支比为验证双极点解释提供了可检验的唯象学判据。

Abstract: The nature of the scalar charmed meson $D_0^*(2300)$ remains one of the most intriguing states in hadron spectroscopy. A prominent interpretation is a two-pole structure generated by the coupled channels $Dπ$, $Dη$, $D_s\bar K$, and $Dη'$, in which the lower pole couples mainly to $Dπ$ and the higher pole to $D_s\bar K$. Following this picture, we calculate the decay constants of these two poles using the effective Lagrangian approach. The resulting values, $f_{D_0^*}^{(\mathrm{lower})}=64.6^{+0.9}_{-1.0}\,\mathrm{MeV}$ and $f_{D_0^*}^{(\mathrm{higher})}=80.8^{+9.6}_{-5.3}\,\mathrm{MeV}$, are substantially smaller than those predicted by conventional $c\bar q$ excited-state scenarios. This difference suggests that decay constants can serve as a sensitive probe to discriminate between different internal structures of the $D_0^*(2300)$. As a phenomenological application, we further predict the branching fractions for the Cabibbo-favored decays $B_s \to D_s D_0^*$, $Λ_b \to Λ_c D_0^*$, and $Ξ_b \to Ξ_c D_0^*$ within the factorization approach. These predictions provide crucial tests to validate the two-pole interpretation of the $D_0^*(2300)$.

</details>


### [193] [Invisible decay of solar neutrinos at dark matter experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24584)
*Martina Beccaria,Veronica Beligotti,Valentina De Romeri,Giulia Pagliaroli,Dimitrios K. Papoulias,Federica Pompa,Christoph A. Ternes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用XENONnT、PandaX-4T和LUX-ZEPLIN的核反冲数据，首次通过相干弹性中微子-核散射（CEνNS）约束太阳中微子的不可见衰变，得到与SNO实验相当的寿命上限；未来氙基探测器通过核反冲和电子反冲数据可将约束提升1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳中微子的长基线和特征能量为探测中微子不可见衰变提供了理想平台，但此前尚无利用CEνNS过程进行约束的研究。

Method: 对XENONnT、PandaX-4T和LUX-ZEPLIN的核反冲数据进行联合分析，约束ν₂质量本征态的寿命；并扩展到未来氙基探测器的电子反冲数据，分析pp链低能中微子的探测能力。

Result: 当前核反冲数据得到的ν₂寿命约束与SNO相当；未来探测器核反冲数据可改进约一个数量级，电子反冲数据可超越现有太阳中微子约束1-2个数量级。

Conclusion: CEνNS和电子反冲通道为探测太阳中微子不可见衰变提供了互补且更强力的手段，未来氙基暗物质探测器有望大幅提升约束能力。

Abstract: The combination of the long baseline and characteristic energies of solar neutrinos offers an ideal framework to probe invisible neutrino decay. In this work we present the first constraint on invisible solar-neutrino decay using coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, recently observed in dark matter direct detection experiments. Through a combined analysis of nuclear-recoil data from XENONnT, PandaX-4T, and LUX-ZEPLIN, we constrain the lifetime of the neutrino mass state $ν_{2}$, obtaining a bound already comparable in strength to that from the Sudbury Neutrino Observatory. We further evaluate the sensitivity that could be reached by a future xenon-based dark matter detector. For this projection, we extend the analysis to electronic-recoil data, estimating the impact of detecting lower-energy solar neutrinos from the $pp$-chain via elastic scattering off electrons. This channel would allow us to place strong constraints on the lifetimes of both the $ν_{1}$ and $ν_{2}$ mass eigenstates. Our results show that, with nominal future exposures, nuclear-recoil data would improve the current bound by about one order of magnitude, while electronic-recoil data would open a new detection channel for low-energy solar neutrinos, surpassing existing dedicated solar-neutrino bounds by 1 to 2 orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [194] [Global analyses of helicity-dependent parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24613)
*Emanuele R. Nocera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了螺旋度依赖的部分子分布函数（极化PDF）的定义、物理解释、微扰QCD框架、实验约束及全局分析方法，介绍了极化PDF确定的最新进展及未来电子-离子对撞机预期的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解核子自旋如何在其部分子组分（夸克、反夸克、胶子）之间分配是QCD中核子自旋结构的关键问题，极化PDF正是描述这种自旋分布的核心工具。

Method: 基于全局QCD分析，综合包括 inclusive DIS、SIDIS 和极化质子-质子碰撞在内的极化硬散射实验数据，利用微扰QCD框架和统计方法提取极化PDF。

Result: 展示了极化PDF确定的最新进展，呈现了当前极化PDF的确定状态。

Conclusion: 极化PDF是理解核子自旋结构的核心，未来EIC的测量将进一步改进极化PDF的精度。

Abstract: Helicity-dependent, or polarised, parton distribution functions describe the longitudinal polarisation of quarks, antiquarks, and gluons inside a polarised nucleon. They encode how the spin of a fast-moving proton or neutron is shared among its partonic constituents and are therefore central to the modern understanding of nucleon spin structure in Quantum Chromodynamics. Helicity PDFs are extracted from global QCD analyses of polarised hard-scattering data, including inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering, and polarised proton-proton collisions. This article reviews the definition and physical interpretation of polarised PDFs, the perturbative QCD framework used to determine them, the experimental observables that constrain the different parton flavours, and the statistical methodology of global analyses. The state of the art in polarised PDF determination is presented, together with the impact expected from future measurements at the Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [195] [Next-to-leading order FsQED corrections to radiative pion pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24642)
*Carlo M. Carloni Calame,Marco Ghilardi,Andrea Gurgone,Guido Montagna,Mauro Moretti,Oreste Nicrosini,Fulvio Piccinini,Francesco P. Ucci*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在FsQED方法下计算了辐射返回过程e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻γ的次领头阶修正，将π介子形状因子嵌入到圈积分中。通过与标量QED方法、广义矢量介子主导模型的比较，展示了结构依赖性修正对味道工厂辐射返回实验可观测量数值影响。结合文献引入了超越FsQED的领头阶修正并给出初步估计。同时研究了φ介子共振能量附近末态辐射的额外机制（如辐射φ衰变），为KLOE实验提供数值结果。这些新特征已集成到蒙特卡洛事件生成器BabaYaga@NLO中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算辐射返回过程e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻γ的次领头阶修正，以改进对强子截面测量的理论精度，满足味道工厂（如KLOE实验）对低能强子物理的精确计算需求。

Method: 采用铁电QED方法将π介子形状因子嵌入圈积分；与因式化的标量QED方法和广义矢量介子主导模型进行对比；引入超越FsQED的领头阶修正；分析φ介子共振附近的辐射φ衰变机制；在BabaYaga@NLO蒙特卡洛生成器中实现这些新特征。

Result: 得到了辐射返回过程的结构依赖性修正在各种可观测量上的数值影响；提供了超越FsQED项的初步估计；给出了φ介子共振区域辐射φ衰变贡献的数值结果。

Conclusion: BabaYaga@NLO生成器可通过改进的π-光子相互作用模型评估辐射返回测量中的理论不确定性，为味道工厂实验提供更精确的理论工具。

Abstract: We compute the next-to-leading order corrections to the radiative return process $e^+ e^- \to π^+ π^- γ$ within the FsQED approach to embed the pion form factor in the calculation of loop integrals. We compare our results with those of the factorised scalar QED approach, as well as with previous predictions obtained by us in the generalised vector meson dominance model. We show the numerical impact of the structure-dependent corrections on various observables of interest for radiative return experiments at flavour factories. Following recent input from the literature, we include in our calculation also leading corrections beyond FsQED and we provide a first estimate of such contributions. We also investigate additional mechanisms contributing to final-state radiation at center-of-mass energies around the $φ$-meson resonance, such as radiative $φ$ decays, presenting numerical results that are relevant for the KLOE experiment. These novel features are implemented in the Monte Carlo event generator BabaYaga@NLO, which can now be used to evaluate the impact of the modelling of pion-photon interaction in radiative return measurements.

</details>


### [196] [Electromagnetic structure of charged and neutral strange vector mesons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24661)
*Parada T. P. Hutauruk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用协变Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，在Schwinger proper-time正则化方案下，计算了带电K*+(892)和中性K*0(896)奇异矢量介子的电磁形状因子、电荷半径和磁矩，结果与其他理论计算和格点QCD模拟一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奇异矢量介子的电磁结构，特别是K*+和K*0的电磁形状因子，以加深对强子内部结构和非微扰QCD动力学的理解。

Method: 采用协变Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型，配合Schwinger proper-time正则化方案处理紫外发散并纳入QCD层面的夸克禁闭效应，计算了电荷(G_C)、磁(G_M)和四极(G_Q)形状因子。

Result: 带电K*+的电荷半径为0.45 fm²，磁矩为2.67 μ_N；中性K*0的电荷半径为-0.04 fm²，磁矩为0.032 μ_N。

Conclusion: 计算结果与其他理论计算和格点QCD模拟一致，表明该模型能有效描述奇异矢量介子的电磁性质。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic form factors (EMFFs) of the charged $K^{*+}(892)$ ($u\bar{s}$) and neutral $K^{0}(896)$ ($d\bar{s}$) strange vector mesons within the covariant Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, employing the Schwinger proper-time regularization scheme to regularize ultraviolet divergences while incorporating the QCD aspect of quark confinement. To this end, we evaluate the charge (electric) $G_C(Q^2)$, magnetic $G_M(Q^2)$, and quadrupole $G_Q(Q^2)$ form factors, as well as the charge radii, of the charged $K^{+}(892)$ and neutral $K^{0}(896)$ strange vector mesons. We find the charge radii to be $\langle r^2 \rangle_{K^{+}} = 0.45~\mathrm{fm}^2$ and $\langle r^2 \rangle_{K^{0}} = -0.04~\mathrm{fm}^2$, respectively, while the corresponding magnetic moments are $μ_{K^{+}} = 2.67,μ_N$ and $μ_{K^{*0}} = 0.032,μ_N$. These results are consistent with other theoretical calculations and lattice QCD simulations.

</details>


### [197] [The Operational Resistance of Neutrinos: Epistemic Maturity vs. Technological Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24670)
*Neda Razzaghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出，中微子虽然在理论物理学中具有认知成熟性（精确测量和理论整合），但由于弱相互作用截面极小，难以构建可复现的信号恢复链，从而缺乏技术可操作性。作者引入无量纲可操作指数𝒪来量化源功率、探测器规模和信息吞吐量的综合约束，证明中微子系统虽非根本不可能，但处于固有的工程低效区间，其工程价值仅限于战略性的小众应用，而非通用通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示科学现实与技术效用之间的结构性不对称：中微子在物理学中已是认知成熟的理论实体，却难以实现广泛的技术部署。作者试图解释这种不对称性的根本原因，并为之提供形式化的分析框架。

Method: 提出一个无量纲可操作指数𝒪，综合考量源功率、探测器规模和信息吞吐量三个维度的约束，以此形式化评估中微子系统的工程可行性。

Result: 中微子系统并非原则上不可能实现，但被限制在固有工程低效的区间内；其工程价值仅限于战略性小众应用，无法用于通用通信。

Conclusion: 中微子是认知中心但操作抵抗的实体，其认知成熟性（精确测量和理论整合）不会自动转化为技术可操作性；可操作性需要可复现的信号恢复链，而中微子的弱相互作用截面系统性阻碍了这一链条的构建。

Abstract: Neutrinos represent a quintessential dichotomy in contemporary physics because they are epistemically mature components of our physical theories yet remain remarkably resistant to broad technological deployment. This paper examines the underlying structural asymmetry between scientific reality and technological utility. We argue that epistemic maturity, characterized by precise measurement and theoretical integration, does not automatically confer technological operability. Operability requires the reproducible construction of a signal recovery chain, which, in the case of neutrinos, is systematically hindered by their weak interaction cross sections. To formalize this limitation, we introduce a dimensionless operability index denoted as $\mathcal{O}$, which synthesizes the simultaneous constraints of source power, detector scaling, and information throughput. Through this diagnostic lens, we demonstrate that neutrino based systems are not fundamentally impossible but are instead relegated to a regime of intrinsic engineering inefficiency. We conclude that neutrinos should be understood as epistemically central yet operationally resistant entities, whose engineering value remains limited to niche, strategic applications rather than general purpose communication.

</details>


### [198] [Symbolic Extraction of Non-Perturbative Transverse-Momentum-Dependent Distributions from Drell-Yan Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24710)
*Cole Granger,Alessandro Bacchetta,Valerio Bertone,Chiara Bissolotti,Matteo Cerutti,Marco Radici,Simone Rodini,Lorenzo Rossi,Cristiano Fanelli*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用神经网络拟合结合符号回归方法，从Drell-Yan实验数据中提取了非极化夸克的非微扰TMD部分子分布函数的解析参数化形式，最终得到一个包含9个自由数值常数的闭式函数，在482个数据点上达到χ²/ndf=1.040，并发现了非平凡的x-bT交叉关联项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TMD部分子分布函数的非微扰部分缺乏简洁而准确的解析形式，传统参数化方法表达能力有限，而纯机器学习方法缺乏可解释性，需要一种既能灵活拟合实验数据又能产出可解析表达的方法。

Method: 采用分步策略：首先使用因子化神经网络在N³LL精度下直接对固定靶、Tevatron、RHIC和LHC的Drell-Yan截面数据训练；然后对网络各组件应用符号回归；最后在表达式复杂度与实验χ²的Pareto前沿上选择最优闭式函数。

Result: 得到了一个包含9个自由参数的闭式非微扰函数，在482个数据点上χ²/ndf=1.040；即使在稀疏先验偏向零的情况下，仍保留了非平凡的x-bT交叉项，表明纵向动量分数与横向动量之间存在真实的弱关联。

Conclusion: 符号回归是连接灵活机器学习拟合与可解析TMD参数化的有效工具，为数据驱动发现非微扰QCD的具体特征开辟了系统化路径。

Abstract: We present an analytical parametrization of the non-perturbative transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) parton distribution function of unpolarized quarks, extracted from Drell-Yan data using a combination of neural-network fitting and symbolic regression. A factorized neural network is trained directly against experimental cross-section data from fixed-target, Tevatron, RHIC, and LHC experiments at next-to-next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy, and symbolic regression is subsequently applied to each network component to discover compact analytical expressions. The final formula is selected from a Pareto front in the space of expression complexity and experimental $χ^2$, yielding a closed-form non-perturbative function with 9 free numerical constants that achieves $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}=1.040$ over 482 data points. A non-trivial $x$-$b_T$ cross term is retained even under a sparsity prior that biases it toward zero, indicating a mild but genuine correlation between the longitudinal momentum fraction and the transverse momentum. This work demonstrates that symbolic regression is a viable tool for bridging flexible machine-learning fits and interpretable analytical TMD parametrizations, and opens a systematic path toward data-driven discovery of specific features of non-perturbative QCD.

</details>
