### [199] [Flows of geometric structures II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23231)
*Daniel Fadel,Udhav Fowdar,Eric Loubeau,Andrés J. Moreno,Henrique N. Sá Earp*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文发展了张量H-结构流的一般理论，特别关注SU(m)子群情况，研究了固有挠率能量的无限制负梯度流和Ricci-调和H-流两种演化，证明了短时间存在唯一性，并推导了热型演化方程和有限时间延拓准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进H-结构流的一般理论，特别是非等距流情形，将几何流方法应用于具有特殊几何结构（如SU(m)、G₂、Spin(7)）的流形，深化对挠率能量泛函的梯度流理解。

Method: 发展了SU(m)代数，比较了两种自然演化：固有挠率能量的无限制负梯度流与Ricci-调和H-流。通过修正DeTurck论证和主符号计算证明短时间存在唯一性，推导了热型演化方程，并给出了Shi型导数和有限时间延拓准则。

Result: 证明了Ricci-调和H-流对任意闭子群H⊂SO(n)的短时间存在唯一性；对于{1}, SU(2), G₂, Spin(7)群，负梯度流可表示为Ricci-调和形式；SU(m)情况需独立主符号计算；U(m)情况是边界情况，无法通过一阶微分同胚规范实现严格抛物性。

Conclusion: 建立了张量H-结构流的系统理论框架，揭示了不同子群结构在几何流中的行为差异，为六维SU(3)-结构的二阶拟线性流提供了形式化描述。

Abstract: We advance the general theory of flows of tensorial $\mathrm{H}$-structures, focusing on non-isometric flows and on the case $\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{SU}(m)\subset\mathrm{SO}(2m)$. After developing the relevant $\mathrm{SU}(m)$ algebra, we compare two natural evolutions: the unrestricted negative gradient flow of the intrinsic-torsion energy and a Ricci-harmonic flow. We prove short-time existence and uniqueness for the Ricci-harmonic $\mathrm{H}$-flow, with arbitrary lower-order torsion-quadratic terms, for every closed subgroup $\mathrm{H}\subset\mathrm{SO}(n)$. For groups for which the projection to $\mathfrak{h}^\perp$ defines a $4$-form, including $\{1\}$, $\mathrm{SU}(2)$, $\mathrm{G}_2$, and $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$, we express the negative gradient flow in Ricci-harmonic form up to explicit lower-order torsion terms and prove short-time existence and uniqueness by a modified DeTurck argument. We treat the genuinely different $\mathrm{SU}(m)$ case by a separate principal-symbol computation, proving short-time existence and uniqueness for the unrestricted negative gradient flow of $\mathrm{SU}(m)$-structures. The same computation identifies the natural negative gradient flow of $\mathrm{U}(m)$-structures as a borderline case, which cannot be made strictly parabolic by first-order diffeomorphism gauges. For the modified Ricci-harmonic flow, we derive heat-type evolution equations for the intrinsic torsion, a doubling-time estimate and Shi-type derivative estimates for $(|\mathrm{Rm}|^2+|\nabla T|^2+|T|^4)^{1/2}$, and a finite-time continuation criterion. In dimension six, we translate the formalism into the standard torsion forms of an $\mathrm{SU}(3)$-structure and describe, to highest order, the corresponding family of second-order quasilinear $\mathrm{SU}(3)$-flows.

</details>


### [200] [Hermitian manifolds with nonpositive holomorphic sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23246)
*Kai Tang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致Kähler流形上具有非正全纯截面曲率的Hermitian度量，证明了其典范丛是nef的（去掉了Broder-Stanfield结果中的pluriclosed假设）。在复二维情形下，负全纯截面曲率推出典范丛丰富。零全纯截面曲率迫使第一陈类消失。部分逆命题：第一Bott-Chern类为零的紧复流形上，任何具有非负全纯截面曲率的Hermitian度量必然有零全纯截面曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Broder-Stanfield关于非正全纯截面曲率与典范丛nef性的结论，去除pluriclosed这一额外假设；探究复二维情形下负全纯截面曲率与典范丛丰富性的关系；建立全纯截面曲率与陈类之间的对应关系，并给出部分逆命题。

Method: 通过去掉Broder-Stanfield定理中的pluriclosed假设来证明典范丛的nef性；在复二维情形下利用负全纯截面曲率推出典范丛丰富；利用全纯截面曲率消失推出第一陈类为零；在第一Bott-Chern类为零的条件下，从非负全纯截面曲率反推其必须为零。

Result: 1. 紧致Kähler流形上具有非正全纯截面曲率的Hermitian度量，其典范丛是nef的；2. 复二维情形下，负全纯截面曲率推出典范丛丰富；3. 零全纯截面曲率迫使第一陈类为零；4. 第一Bott-Chern类为零时，非负全纯截面曲率必为零。

Conclusion: 本文改进了Broder-Stanfield的结果，揭示了全纯截面曲率与非正曲率情形下典范丛性质之间的深层联系，并在复二维及零曲率情形下给出了更完整的刻画。

Abstract: We study compact Kähler manifolds admitting Hermitian metrics with nonpositive holomorphic sectional curvature. We prove that the canonical bundle of such a manifold is nef, removing the pluriclosed assumption from the corresponding nefness result of Broder-Stanfield \cite{BroderStanfield}. In complex dimension two, we further show that negative holomorphic sectional curvature implies the ampleness of the canonical bundle. We also prove that vanishing holomorphic sectional curvature forces the first Chern class to vanish. As a partial converse, we show that on a compact complex manifold with vanishing first Bott-Chern class, every Hermitian metric with nonnegative holomorphic sectional curvature must have vanishing holomorphic sectional curvature.

</details>


### [201] [Toric geometry of Schouten squares on rotational double extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23306)
*Amine Bahayou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文分析了Medina-Revoy旋转双扩展（其中阿贝尔李代数fb通过标量旋转作用于欧几里得二维平面正交和）上Schouten平方在块秩一轨迹及其非退化开集上的限制，发现Schouten平方在标记切片上正交分解为径向和角向分量，径向分量满足尖锐二次 coercive 估计且具有紧半代数仿射纤维，角分量在联合本征坐标下呈洛朗单项式形式，并通过记号-消去准则确定了符号指数配置及其对微分秩、紧迷向、分量群和复化像Zariski闭包定义的洛朗二项式理想的控制，所有结论对任意维数成立且无需非共振假设，并给出了经典杨-巴克斯特方程的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Medina-Revoy旋转双扩展中Schouten平方在块秩一轨迹上的精细结构，包括其径向与角向分解、轨道几何性质及与经典杨-巴克斯特方程的联系，为双扩展李代数的Poisson几何提供系统分析框架。

Method: 利用标记切片上的正交分解将Schouten平方拆分为径向分量（通过标准toric矩映射因子化）和角分量（联合本征坐标下的洛朗单项式），通过记号-消去准则确定指数配置，运用Smith标准型实现各不变量的算法化。

Result: 径向分量图像为单纯锥，满足具有最优内禀常数的尖锐二次coercive估计，限制Schouten映射为具有紧半代数仿射纤维的固有映射；角分量指数配置控制微分秩、紧迷向及其分量群；建立了块秩一轨迹上经典杨-巴克斯特方程的分类准则。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Medina-Revoy旋转双扩展中块秩一轨迹上Schouten平方的完整结构理论，所有结果适用于任意维数且无需非共振假设，并给出了经典杨-巴克斯特方程在该设置下的具体分类。

Abstract: Let $\g=\fb\oplus V\oplus\fb^{*}$ be a Medina-Revoy rotational double extension in which an abelian Lie algebra $\fb$ acts by scalar rotations on an orthogonal sum of oriented Euclidean two-planes. We analyse the restriction of the Schouten square $r\mapsto[r,r]$ to the block-rank-one locus $\Rone\subset\fb\wedge V$ and to its nondegenerate open part $\Rplus$. On every marked slice $S_{H}\subset\Rplus$ the Schouten square splits orthogonally into a radial and an angular component. The radial component factors through the standard toric moment map: its image is a simplicial cone, it satisfies a sharp quadratic coercive estimate with an intrinsic optimal constant, and the restricted Schouten map is proper with compact semialgebraic affine fibres. The angular component becomes Laurent monomial in joint eigencoordinates; a marker-cancellation criterion determines its signed exponent configuration, whose integral lattice controls the differential rank, the compact isotropy, its component group, and the Laurent binomial ideal defining the Zariski closure of the complexified image. Smith normal form makes each invariant algorithmic. All statements hold for arbitrary $\dim\fb$ and impose no nonresonance assumption. A corollary of the underlying disjoint-support decomposition classifies the classical Yang-Baxter equation on $\Rone$: for any bivector whose $\fb\wedge V$ component lies in $\Rone$, triangularity forces every block detected by its rotation weight to vanish, regardless of the $Λ^{2}\fb$, $Λ^{2}V$, or central-wedge terms.

</details>


### [202] [Pinching rigidity of surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector in spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23428)
*Weiran Ding,Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文受Simon猜想启发，研究了单位球面中具有平行平均曲率向量和正高斯曲率的闭曲面，建立了三个Simons型积分恒等式，获得了前两个尖锐端点间隙以及第三个区间内的刚性与振荡估计，并通过Calabi和Yau分类定理刻画了端点情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受球面中极小曲面的Simon猜想的启发，作者希望将相关间隙现象推广到更一般的平行平均曲率向量曲面情形，从而揭示这类曲面在曲率条件约束下的几何刚性。

Method: 定义修正的第二基本形式$\tilde h = h - \mathbf{H}g$及其模长平方$\tilde S = |\tilde h|^2 = |h|^2 - 2H^2$，建立关于$\tilde S$的三个Simons型积分恒等式（分别对应极小情形下的第一、第二和第三间隙恒等式的推广），并结合Calabi和Yau的分类定理对端点情形进行刻画。

Result: 获得了前两个尖锐端点间隙（sharp endpoint gaps），以及第三个区间内的刚性与振荡估计（rigidity and oscillation estimates），并完整刻画了端点情形。

Conclusion: 成功将极小曲面中的Simon间隙现象推广到平行平均曲率向量曲面情形，得到了一系列最优的间隙结果和刚性分类。

Abstract: Inspired by the Simon conjecture for minimal surfaces in spheres, we study closed surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector and positive Gaussian curvature immersed in unit spheres. Let $h$ be the second fundamental form, let $\mathbf{H}$ be the mean curvature vector field, and set $\tilde h=h-\mathbf{H}g$ and $\tilde S=|\tilde h|^2=|h|^2-2H^2$, where $H=|\mathbf{H}|$ and $g$ is the induced Riemannian metric on the surface $M$. We establish three Simons-type integral identities for $\tilde S$, which extend the first, second and third gap identities in the minimal case. As applications, we obtain the first two sharp endpoint gaps and several rigidity and oscillation estimates in the third interval. We further characterize the endpoint cases by combining these identities with the classification theorems of Calabi and Yau.

</details>


### [203] [A degenerate free boundary minimal annulus and non-uniqueness in spherical caps beyond the hemisphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23534)
*Alexander Pigazzini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了de Oliveira在三维球面测地球盘中构造的嵌入自由边界极小旋转环面族{Σ_a}，证明了半径函数R(a)是实解析的、在端点趋于π/2且存在内部最大值R_*>π/2，因此R不是单射；每个半径ρ∈(π/2, R_*)的球盘至少包含两个互不全等的环面。在R的临界点处，环面退化（零度≥3），且这些退化环面构成非空离散集。通过Robin缺陷恒等式，得到了退化检测的精确恒等式dim K_0^{ev}(Σ_a)=1_{R'=0}(a)，表明Naff-Zhu连续性方法中"所有Jacobi场均由Killing场诱导"的假设在R>π/2时失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究嵌入自由边界极小环面在球面测地球盘中的存在性与唯一性，特别关注半径函数R(a)的解析性质及其对环面退化、Jacobi场结构的决定性影响，进而检验Naff-Zhu连续性方法中关键假设的有效性。

Method: 通过实解析性证明R(a)的折叠行为；利用Robin缺陷恒等式∂_ηφ_a-ct_κ(r(a))φ_a=r'(a)A_a(η,η)建立旋转不变偶Jacob i场与R'的精确对应关系；结合对称性分析和退化环面的离散性刻画零度结构。

Result: R(a)实解析、在端点趋于π/2、存在内部最大值R_*>π/2；R不是单射；每个B(ρ)(π/2<ρ<R_*)至少包含两个互不全等的环面；临界点处环面退化（零度≥3），退化环面构成非空离散集；精确恒等式dim K_0^{ev}(Σ_a)=1_{R'=0}(a)；最大帽半径处的退化环面是严格局部面积最大化者。

Conclusion: 证明了Naff-Zhu连续性方法中"所有Jacobi场均由Killing场诱导"的假设在R>π/2时失效；建立了自由边界极小环面退化的完整刻画，为嵌入自由边界极小环面的分类理论提供了关键反例和精确代数条件。

Abstract: Let $\{Σ_a\}$, $a\in(0,1/2)$, be de Oliveira's family of embedded free boundary minimal annuli of revolution in geodesic balls $B(R(a))\subset\mathbb{S}^3$, $R(a)>π/2$. We prove that $R$ is real-analytic, tends to $π/2$ at both ends, and therefore folds: it has an interior maximum $R_*>π/2$ and is not injective. Hence each $B(ρ)$ with $π/2<ρ<R_*$ contains at least two, and only finitely many, mutually non-congruent annuli of the family. At every critical point of $R$ the annulus is degenerate modulo ball-preserving isometries: its Jacobi--Robin kernel contains a rotationally invariant, reflection-even field not induced by any Killing field of $\mathbb{S}^3$ preserving the ball; its nullity is at least three. These degenerate annuli form a nonempty discrete set; off it, the rotationally invariant even nullity vanishes. Those sitting at a maximizer of $R$, in the largest cap $B(R_*)$, are called degenerate annuli of maximal cap radius; each such annulus is also a strict local area maximizer in the family, since area and $R$ have the same critical points. Thus the hypothesis that all Jacobi fields of an embedded free boundary minimal annulus in a spherical cap are Killing-induced, used in the Naff--Zhu continuity approach to uniqueness, fails for some radius $R>π/2$. The exact identity $\dim K_0^{ev}(Σ_a)=\mathbf{1}_{\{R'=0\}}(a)$ detects the degeneration; it follows from the symmetry-free relation $\partial_η\varphi_a-\operatorname{ct}_κ(r(a))\varphi_a=r'(a)A_a(η,η)$, a Robin defect identity, in all space forms and dimensions.

</details>


### [204] [Infinite Combinatorial Yamabe Flows in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23584)
*Bohao Ji*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧氏和双曲背景几何下局部有限无限三角剖分上的三维组合Yamabe流，在适当的非退化和有界度假设下建立了原始流的短时存在唯一性，并通过引入扩展流证明了在适当初始假设下的长时间存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将组合Yamabe流理论从有限三角剖分扩展到无限三角剖分，并解决三维情形下的存在性问题，为理解非紧流形上的离散几何结构提供理论基础。

Method: 采用三维组合Yamabe流（原始流）及其扩展流（通过立体角的连续延拓），在非退化条件和有界度假设下分析其短时和长时存在性。

Result: 在非退化与有界度假设下，原始流具有短时存在唯一性；扩展流在合适初始条件下具有长时间存在性。

Conclusion: 三维组合Yamabe流可有效推广至无限三角剖分，扩展流克服了原始流可能出现的奇点问题，为离散共形几何提供了更完善的理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study three-dimensional combinatorial Yamabe flows on locally finite infinite triangulations in Euclidean and hyperbolic background geometries. Under suitable non-degeneracy and bounded-degree assumptions, we establish the short-time existence and uniqueness of the original flows. We further introduce the extended flows by using the continuous extension of solid angles, and prove long-time existence for both extended flows under suitable initial assumptions.

</details>


### [205] [Plateau's Problem via covering spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23703)
*James Tissot*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Brakke关于Plateau问题的构造，将最小曲面从有限指数子群推广到所有正规子群（包括无限指数），并证明了存在一个正规子群N₀使得其对应的最小曲面面积达到所有非平凡正规子群中的下确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brakke在1995年提出了一个允许三重结和四面体奇点的Plateau问题公式化构造，但仅限于有限指数子群。本文旨在将该构造推广到所有正规子群，并研究对应的紧致性和最小化性质。

Method: (1) 将Brakke的构造从有限指数子群推广到所有正规子群（包括无限指数）。(2) 通过证明紧致性结果，表明存在一个正规子群N₀使得面积达到下确界。(3) 对Σ_N和Σ_{N₀}的跨越性和最小化性质进行研究。

Result: (1) 成功将Brakke构造推广到所有正规子群N⊲G（不限于有限指数）。(2) 证明了紧致性定理，存在一个真正规子群N₀使得Area(Σ_{N₀}) = inf_{N⊲G, N≠G} Area(Σ_N)。(3) 该结果当Γ有多个连通分量时同样成立。

Conclusion: 本文推进了Brakke关于Plateau问题的理论，不仅将构造推广到更一般的子群类别，还通过紧致性论证确保了面积最小化子群的存在性，为具有奇点的极小曲面理论提供了新的工具。

Abstract: In 1995, Brakke proposed a formulation of the Plateau problem for a given boundary $Γ$ that allows for triple junctions and tetrahedral singularities. Let $Γ$ be a smooth closed curve and let $G=π_1(\mathbb{R}^3\setminus Γ)$. For each proper finite index subgroup $N$ of $G$, Brakke constructs a $(\mathrm{\mathbf{M}}, 0, \infty)$-minimal surface $Σ_N$, which is obtained as the projection of the boundary of a perimeter-minimising fundamental domain in the covering space associated to $N$. We advance the theory in two ways. Firstly, we extend Brakke's construction to include all normal subgroups $N\triangleleft G$, i.e. possibly with infinite index. Secondly, we prove a compactness result which implies that there exists a proper normal subgroup $N_0\triangleleft G$ such that
  \[
  \mathrm{Area}(Σ_{N_0})=\inf_{\{N\triangleleft G, N \neq G\}}\ \mathrm{Area}(Σ_N).
  \]
  A similar result holds when $Γ$ has many connected components. Furthermore, we study the spanning and minimising properties of the $Σ_N$s and $Σ_{N_0}$.

</details>


### [206] [Bochner-Yano Type Theorems for Conformal Killing Vector Fields under Curvature Pinching Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23706)
*Teng Huang,Qiang Tan,Weiwei Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了闭黎曼流形上在曲率夹挤条件下的共形 Killing 向量场。通过建立新的 Bochner 型恒等式并运用 Moser 迭代，得到了尖锐的梯度估计，进而证明在 Ricci 曲率适当有上界时，非平凡共形 Killing 向量场必处处非零。由此推出，在偶数维非零欧拉示性数流形上，共形 Killing 向量场恒为零，其共形变换群有限。该结果将 Yano 和 Bochner 的经典刚性定理从非正 Ricci 曲率推广到小正 Ricci 曲率，并将 Chen 和 Han 的结果从 Killing 向量场推广到共形 Killing 向量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的 Yano-Bochner 刚性定理从非正 Ricci 曲率情形推广到小正 Ricci 曲率情形，并将 Chen 和 Han 关于 Killing 向量场的结果推广到共形 Killing 向量场。

Method: 建立新的 Bochner 型恒等式，结合 Moser 迭代程序推导出尖锐的梯度估计，进而分析共形 Killing 向量场的零点性质。

Result: 在 Ricci 曲率满足适当上界条件下，非平凡共形 Killing 向量场处处非零；在偶数维非零欧拉示性数流形上，所有共形 Killing 向量场恒为零，共形变换群有限。

Conclusion: 将经典刚性定理从非正 Ricci 曲率扩展至小正 Ricci 曲率情形，并将相关结果从 Killing 向量场推广至共形 Killing 向量场。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate conformal Killing vector fields on closed Riemannian manifolds under a curvature pinching condition. By establishing a new Bochner-type identity for the $1$-form dual to a conformal Killing vector field, we derive a sharp gradient estimate via a Moser iteration procedure. Based on this estimate, we prove that, under a suitable upper bound on the Ricci curvature, every nontrivial conformal Killing vector field must be nowhere vanishing. Consequently, on even-dimensional manifolds with non-zero Euler characteristic, every conformal Killing vector field vanishes identically, which in turn implies that the conformal transformation group of such a manifold is finite. Our results extend the classical rigidity theorems of Yano and Bochner from the setting of non-positive Ricci curvature to that of small positive Ricci curvature, and generalize the recent results of Chen and Han from Killing vector fields to conformal Killing vector fields.

</details>


### [207] [The sharp curl-Sobolev inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23827)
*Guofang Wang,Mingwei Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文解决了自Rivière 1998年以来的长期悬而未决的问题，证明了当n≡3(mod 4)时S^n上的尖锐curl-Sobolev不等式，并用Killing形式分类了所有极值函数。进而给出了几个几何和变分应用，解决了多个开放猜想：证明了S^n上标准度规是共形不变量μ([g_st])的唯一优化器；证实了Rivière关于Hopf映射3-能量最小化子的猜想；建立了Faddeev-Skyrme能量全局极小性的完整预测范围；以及证明了S^3上磁场尖下界并回答了Frank-Loss问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自Rivière (1998)以来长期悬而未决的curl-Sobolev不等式问题，该问题在物理最相关的三维情形下也一直是开放的。同时，该不等式将有助于解决几何和数学物理中的多个开放猜想。

Method: 通过证明S^n（n≡3 mod 4）上尖锐的curl-Sobolev不等式，其中共形不变商满足特定下界，并利用Killing形式对所有极值函数进行分类。利用共形不变性将结果推广到R^n。

Result: 证明了S^n上curl-Sobolev不等式的尖锐形式，并分类了极值函数。应用包括：S^n上标准度规是μ([g_st])的唯一优化器；Hopf映射是S^3上3-能量的唯一极小化子（在共形变换下）；Faddeev-Skyrme能量在ρ≤√2时Hopf映射为全局极小；S^3上磁场‖curl A‖_{3/2}≥3ω_3^{2/3}，等号成立当且仅当Killing旋量。

Conclusion: 成功解决了多个长期开放问题，包括Rivière猜想、Ward关于Faddeev-Skyrme模型的预测以及Frank-Loss问题，展示了curl-Sobolev不等式在几何和数学物理中的强大应用价值。

Abstract: We solve a longstanding problem, going back at least to Rivière 1998 and open even in the physically most relevant case $n=3$, by proving a sharp curl-Sobolev inequality on $\mathbb{S}^n$ when $n\equiv 3\pmod 4$: for every $\frac{n-1}{2}$-form $α$, the conformally invariant quotient satisfies (with positive denominator) \[ \frac{\Big(\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}|{\rm curl}α|^{\frac{2n}{n+1}}\,{\rm dV}\Big)^{\frac{n+1}{n}}}{\int_{\mathbb{S}^n}\langle{\rm curl}α,α\rangle\,{\rm dV}}
  \ge \frac{n+1}{2}\,ω_n^{\frac1n}. \] We also classify all extremals in terms of Killing forms. By conformal invariance, the same result holds on $\mathbb{R}^n$.
  We then give geometric and variational applications that settle several open conjectures in geometry and mathematical physics. First, we show that on $\mathbb{S}^n$ the round metric is the unique optimizer for the conformal invariant $μ([g_{\rm st}])$. Second, we prove that the unique minimizers of the $3$-energy $\int_{\mathbb{S}^3}|{\rm d} u|^3$ in the homotopy class of the Hopf map $π:\mathbb{S}^3\to\mathbb{S}^2$ are exactly $π\circΦ$ with $Φ\in{\rm Conf}^+(\mathbb{S}^3)$, confirming a conjecture of Rivière. Third, for the Faddeev-Skyrme energy $\mathcal{FS}_ρ$ on $\mathbb{S}^3$, we establish global minimality of the Hopf map in the full predicted range: for every coupling constant $ρ\le \sqrt{2}$, the unique global minimizers in its homotopy class are precisely $π\circ R$ with $R\in\mathrm{SO}(4)$, as expected since Ward 1999. Fourth, in the presence of Dirac zero modes on $\mathbb{S}^3$, we prove the sharp lower bound $\|{\rm curl} A\|_{3/2} \ge 3ω_3^{\frac 2 3}$ for the magnetic field and characterize equality in terms of Killing spinors; in particular, this yields a sharp criterion for the existence of zero modes and answers a question of Frank-Loss for $n=3$.

</details>


### [208] [A note on the cohomology of pullback Lie algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23859)
*Eckhard Meinrenken*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了Crainic关于Lie algebroid在满射淹没下拉回上同调定理的一个直接微分几何证明：若纤维连通且直到n阶上同调消失，则带任意系数的拉回映射在n阶是同构，在n+1阶是单射。证明归结为叶状上同调的消失性结果，并使用了Buchdahl的穷竭-修补论证，无需纤维的局部平凡性或有限型假设。同时证明了全纯Lie algebroid的类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Crainic关于Lie algebroid拉回上同调的定理提供一个更直接、更简洁的微分几何证明，避免原证明中不必要的技术假设（如纤维的局部平凡性或有限型条件），并将结果推广到全纯情形。

Method: 采用直接微分几何方法，将问题归结为叶状上同调的消失性结果，并运用Buchdahl的穷竭-修补（exhaustion-and-patching）论证技术。该方法不依赖纤维的局部平凡性或有限型假设。

Result: 成功给出了Crainic定理的一个更简洁的直接微分几何证明，弱化了原证明所需的假设条件（无需局部平凡性或有限型假设），并将该结果推广到了全纯Lie algebroid的情形。

Conclusion: 本文提出的微分几何方法比原证明更简洁且适用性更广，不仅统一处理了光滑和全纯两种情形，还去除了不必要的技术假设。

Abstract: We give a direct differential-geometric proof of a result of Crainic on the cohomology of pullbacks of Lie algebroids under surjective submersions: If the fibers are connected and have vanishing cohomology through degree $n$, pullback with coefficients in any representation is an isomorphism through degree $n$ and is injective in degree $n+1$. The proof reduces to a vanishing result for leafwise cohomology and uses an exhaustion-and-patching argument due to Buchdahl. No local triviality or finite-type assumption on the fibers is required. We also prove the analogous result for holomorphic Lie algebroids.

</details>


### [209] [A Partial $ε$-Regularity Theorem for Integral Stationary Geodesic Networks in $\mathbb{R}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23872)
*Henry Bosch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在$\mathbb{R}^n$中整数重数稳态测地线网络序列收敛到重数$k$直线时的部分$ε$-正则性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稳态测地线网络的极限行为，特别是当序列收敛到直线时如何控制正则性。

Method: 采用部分$ε$-正则性方法，分析整数重数稳态测地线网络序列在收敛到重数$k$直线时的结构性质。

Result: 证明了此类序列的部分$ε$-正则性定理，即在适当的小尺度上网络具有良好的正则性。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对稳态测地线网络极限行为的理解，为相关几何测度论问题提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We prove a partial $ε$-regularity theorem for sequences of integer multiplicity stationary geodesic networks in $\mathbb{R}^n$ converging to a multiplicity $k$ line.

</details>


### [210] [Closed mean curvature flows with prescribed tangent flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23879)
*Jingwen Chen,Tang-Kai Lee,Ao Sun,Xinrui Zhao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对嵌入收缩子（shrinker）构造了平均曲率流，使其在首次奇异点处的切流以该收缩子为模型，并确定了切流的一阶渐近行为。该结果是更具一般性定理的推论，该定理允许构造带有附加力的平均曲率流，并预定其在首次奇异点处的切流和一阶渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均曲率流中奇异点的形成是重要研究课题，而切流（tangent flow）描述了奇异点附近的几何结构。本文旨在构造具有指定切流和指定一阶渐近行为（即更高阶信息）的平均曲率流，从而更精细地刻画奇异点的形成机制。

Method: 采用构造性方法，基于一个更一般的定理——该定理允许构造带有附加力（additional force）的平均曲率流，并能预定其在首次奇异点处的切流模型和一阶渐近展开式。具体构造依赖于给定的嵌入收缩子（包括闭的、渐近锥状的或带欧氏空间直积因子的情形），通过适当选择附加力来实现目标流形。

Result: 对于任意满足条件的嵌入收缩子（闭的、渐近锥状的或与ℝᵏ的直积），均存在一个闭嵌入平均曲率流，其首次奇异点处的切流恰好以该收缩子为模型，且切流的一阶渐近行为可被预先指定。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个通用的构造框架，使得平均曲率流在首次奇异点处的切流及其一阶渐近行为可以被精确控制。这不仅推广了先前关于切流模型的结果，还为理解更复杂的奇异行为提供了工具。

Abstract: Given an embedded shrinker $Σ$ in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ that is either closed, asymptotically conical, or a Cartesian product of such a shrinker with $\mathbb{R}^k$, we construct a closed embedded mean curvature flow whose tangent flow at the first singularity is modeled on $Σ$. We also prescribe the first-order asymptotics of the tangent flow. This result is a consequence of a more general theorem that allows us to construct mean curvature flows with an additional force whose tangent flow and first-order asymptotics at the first singularity are prescribed.

</details>


### [211] [Minimal equators and homological systoles in Berger projective spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24001)
*Glen Wheeler*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Berger形变下实射影子流形的极小性及体积最小化问题，通过引入单个斜自伴自同态A_V完全控制了实子空间的线性几何，给出了非圆Berger度量下的完全分类：实射影空间RP(V)是极小的当且仅当V是线性CR子空间，即其多重Kähler角均为0或π/2。这否定了Gil-Medrano关于极小性的一般性猜想，但赤道超曲面仍保持极小。此外，还确定了线性体积泛函的临界点和全局极值，并结合射影积分几何计算了纯拉伸和压缩区域中的模二同调 systole。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Gil-Medrano提出的问题：在大于三维的Berger形变下，实射影子流形是否仍保持极小且体积最小化。

Method: 引入控制实子空间线性几何的单个斜自伴自同态A_V，通过分析多重Kähler角进行分类；结合射影积分几何进行同调计算。

Result: 非圆Berger度量下，RP(V)极小当且仅当V是线性CR子空间（多重Kähler角为0或π/2）；赤道超曲面保持极小；确定了体积泛函的临界点和全局极值；计算了纯拉伸和压缩区域的模二同调 systole。

Conclusion: 对Gil-Medrano问题的极小性部分给出了否定答案，并在体积最小化问题上取得实质性进展，最后提出剩余情况由混合CR赤道构成的猜想。

Abstract: Gil-Medrano asked whether real projective subspaces remain minimal and volume-minimising under Berger deformations in dimensions greater than three. We answer the minimality question and make substantial progress on the corresponding minimisation problem. The fundamental observation is that the full linear geometry of a real subspace $V\subset\mathbb{C}^N$ is controlled by the single skew-adjoint endomorphism $A_V=\operatorname{pr}_VJ|_V$. For every non-round Berger metric, this yields a complete classification: $\mathbb{RP}(V)$ is minimal if and only if $V$ is a linear CR subspace, equivalently if all of its multiple Kähler angles are $0$ or $π/2$. Thus the answer to Gil-Medrano's minimality question is negative in general, although every equatorial hypersurface remains minimal. The same structure determines the critical points and global extrema of the linear volume functional. Combining it with projective integral geometry, we also compute exact mod-two homological systoles in the pure stretched and squashed regimes. Our final contribution is a conjecture that all remaining cases consist of mixed CR equators.

</details>


### [212] [Calabi surgery for Z/2 harmonic 1-forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24281)
*Jiahuang Chen,Siqi He,Dashen Yan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Calabi内在调和性定理的2-值版本，并引入Calabi手术方法——一种针对Z/2调和1-形式的手术理论。通过允许度量变化，将新Z/2调和形式的构造简化为闭2-值1-形式的切割粘贴、局部调和模型匹配以及奇异叶状结构传递性的控制。该方法替代了奇异粘合中的Nash-Moser型解析变形问题，代之以局部模型匹配和叶状结构的全局动力学条件。作为应用，获得了连通和与局部替换定理、按指定欧几里得模型爆破孤立普通零点、分裂光滑k-非退化分支分量，以及在3维和4维中使用适当解消模型解奇异化图形奇点集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Calabi内在调和性定理到2-值设定，并发展一套灵活的手术理论来构造和修改Z/2调和1-形式，以处理更广泛的几何与分析问题。

Method: 证明2-值Calabi内在调和性定理，引入Calabi手术方法，通过切割粘贴闭2-值1-形式、匹配局部调和模型，并控制奇异叶状结构的传递性来构造新形式，用局部模型匹配和全局动力学条件替代Nash-Moser型解析变形问题。

Result: 获得了连通和定理、局部替换定理、按欧几里得模型爆破孤立普通零点、分裂光滑k-非退化分支分量，以及在3维和4维中解奇异化图形奇点集等结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的Calabi手术方法在弱正则性假设下提供了灵活构造和修改Z/2调和1-形式的通用框架，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We prove a 2-valued analogue of Calabi's intrinsic harmonicity theorem and use it to introduce the Calabi surgery method, a surgery theory for $\mathbb{Z}/2$ harmonic $1$-forms. Once the ambient metric is allowed to vary, the construction of new $\mathbb{Z}/2$ harmonic forms can be reduced to cutting and pasting closed 2-valued 1-forms, matching local harmonic models, and controlling the transitivity of the resulting singular foliation. For these constructions, the Nash--Moser-type analytic deformation problem that arises in singular gluing is replaced by local model matching and a global dynamical condition on the foliation. The resulting procedure gives a flexible way to construct and modify $\mathbb{Z}/2$ harmonic 1-forms under weak regularity assumptions. As applications, we obtain connected-sum and local replacement theorems, blow up isolated ordinary zeros by prescribed Euclidean models, split smooth $\vec{k}$-nondegenerate branching components, and desingularize graphic singular sets in dimensions 3 and 4 with suitable resolution models.

</details>


### [213] [On Conformal Flexibility of Completeness and Minimizing Geodesics on Hilbert Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24508)
*Levin Maier*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究无限维黎曼几何中的度量与测地线完备性问题，证明了在可分希尔伯特流形上，任何光滑强黎曼度量的共形类中都存在一个光滑强代表度量，同时满足度量完备、测地线完备且任意两点间存在长度最小化测地线；同时揭示了度量完备性在无限维中独有的局部灵活性：可在任意小区域外保持度量不变、破坏度量完备性而保持测地线完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索有限维黎曼几何的性质（特别是Hopf-Rinow定理）在无限维情形下哪些仍成立。已知Hopf-Rinow定理在希尔伯特流形上失效，需要更深入地理解无限维黎曼几何的基本结构。

Method: 通过共形形变技术，在给定光滑强黎曼度量的共形类中构造满足特定完备性条件的代表度量；利用希尔伯特流形的可分性等结构进行构造性证明。

Result: (1) 任何光滑强黎曼度量的共形类都存在一个度量完备、测地线完备且任意连通分支内两点间有长度最小化测地线的光滑代表。(2) 存在共形形变，在任意给定的希尔伯特范数球外与原度量相等，保持测地线完备性但破坏度量完备性。

Conclusion: 无限维黎曼几何展现出与有限维情形本质不同的性质：一方面可以通过共形形变同时实现多种完备性；另一方面度量完备性具有局部的灵活性（可被局部破坏），这是无限维特有的现象。

Abstract: This article is part of a broader programme investigating which features of finite-dimensional Riemannian geometry persist in infinite dimensions. The Hopf--Rinow theorem fails even for Hilbert manifolds: metric and geodesic completeness need not agree, and neither property guarantees a length-minimizing geodesic between prescribed endpoints.
  Despite this failure, our first main result shows that the conformal class of every smooth strong Riemannian metric on a smooth separable Hilbert manifold contains a smooth strong representative that is metrically and geodesically complete and such that every two points in the same connected component are joined by a length-minimizing geodesic.
  By contrast, our second main result establishes a local flexibility phenomenon for metric completeness that is inherently infinite-dimensional. Given any prescribed Hilbert-norm ball, a metrically complete strong metric admits a conformal deformation which is equal to one outside that ball, preserves geodesic completeness, and destroys metric completeness.

</details>


### [214] [Area-minimizing submanifolds are not generically smooth, except for geodesics, minimal surfaces, and minimal hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24735)
*Zhenhua Liu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了模2同调中面积最小化子流形一般不是光滑的，除非是测地线、极小曲面和极小超曲面情形，解决了White的猜想。同时建立了子流形奇异集Hausdorff维数的下界(d-3)，并证明了Veronese极小嵌入的锥面是模2面积最小化的，解决了另一个长期未解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: White猜想询问模2同调中面积最小化子流形是否一般光滑，本文旨在解决这一猜想，并建立奇异集维数的下界。

Method: 通过证明Veronese极小嵌入的锥面是模2面积最小化的，作为关键步骤，进而证明面积最小化子流形在更高维数下必然出现奇点。

Result: 面积最小化子流形在模2同调中一般非光滑（除测地线、极小曲面和极小超曲面外），奇异集Hausdorff维数下界为(d-3)。

Conclusion: White猜想被解决，且Veronese锥面的面积最小化性质这一长期开放问题也被解决。

Abstract: We prove that area-minimizing submanifolds in mod $2$ homology are not generically smooth, except in the case of geodesics, minimal surfaces and minimal hypersurfaces. This settles a conjecture of White that asks the generic smoothness of area-minimizing submanifolds in mod $2$ homology. We furthermore establish a lower bound on the Hausdorff dimension of the singular sets of area-minimizing submanifolds with respect to open sets of Riemannian metrics. The lower bound is ${(d-3)},$ where $d$ denotes the dimension of the submanifold. As a crucial step, we prove that the cone over the Veronese minimal embedding of $\rpt$ is mod $2$ area-minimizing, settling another long-standing open problem.

</details>


### [215] [Doubling for chronological diamonds in Lorentzian geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21423)
*Mauricio Che,Sebastian Gieger,Clemens Sämann*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在度量洛伦兹长度空间中定义了基于时间直径的加倍条件，证明了该条件可由合适的类时曲率界限推导得出，首次将加倍性与洛伦兹几何中的曲率界限联系起来，并由此获得了关于洛伦兹广义锥的紧致性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洛伦兹几何中建立加倍条件与曲率界限之间的联系，这是此前尚未被探索过的问题。

Method: 在度量洛伦兹长度空间中以时间直径（chronological diamonds）为基础定义加倍条件，并通过类时曲率界限来推导该加倍条件成立。

Result: 证明了类时曲率界限可以推出加倍条件，并由此在Mondino–Sämann引入的洛伦兹Gromov–Hausdorff收敛意义下，获得了洛伦兹广义锥的紧致性结果。

Conclusion: 首次将洛伦兹几何中的加倍性与曲率界限联系起来，为洛伦兹广义锥的紧致性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We define doubling conditions for measured Lorentzian length spaces in terms of chronological diamonds, and prove that such conditions are implied by suitable timelike curvature bounds. In particular, for the first time, we relate doubling to curvature bounds in Lorentzian geometry. As a consequence, we obtain compactness results for Lorentzian generalized cones with respect to the Lorentzian Gromov--Hausdorff convergence introduced by Mondino--Sämann.

</details>
