# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [254] [Rankin-Selberg duality via gluing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22965)
*Bruno da Silveira Dias*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文使用Ginzburg引入的胶合过程描述了超球面哈密顿GL_n × GL_m-变种T^*(Hom(C^m,C^n))的相对Langlands对偶，特别是m=n的Rankin-Selberg情况。证明了对偶同构于Cherkis-Nakajima-Takayama弓形变种的三角形部分，重现了Nakajima的结果。遵循Ginzburg的建议，解释了如何修改胶合过程，使得对于任何复约化群G的有限维表示N，对偶哈密顿变种T^*N自然地配备了一个反辛对合，并在Rankin-Selberg情况下给出了该对合的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超球面哈密顿变种T^*(Hom(C^m,C^n))的相对Langlands对偶结构，特别是Rankin-Selberg情况，并建立与Cherkis-Nakajima-Takayama弓形变种的联系。

Method: 使用Ginzburg引入的胶合过程来描述相对Langlands对偶；通过修改胶合过程使对偶哈密顿变种自然配备反辛对合。

Result: 证明了超球面哈密顿变种的相对Langlands对偶同构于Cherkis-Nakajima-Takayama弓形变种的三角形部分；给出了Rankin-Selberg情况下反辛对合的具体公式。

Conclusion: 成功描述了超球面哈密顿变种的相对Langlands对偶结构，验证了与弓形变种的关系，并给出了反辛对合的构造和显式表达。

Abstract: We use a gluing procedure introduced by Ginzburg to describe the relative Langlands dual of the hyperspherical Hamiltonian $(\mathrm{GL}_n \times \mathrm{GL}_m)$-variety $T^*(\mathrm{Hom}(\mathbb{C}^m,\mathbb{C}^n))$, and in particular the Rankin-Selberg case $m=n$. We show that the dual is isomorphic to the triangle part of Cherkis-Nakajima-Takayama bow varieties, recovering a result of Nakajima. Following a suggestion of Ginzburg, we explain how to modify the gluing so that the dual Hamiltonian variety of $T^*\mathbf{N}$, for any finite-dimensional representation $\mathbf{N}$ of a complex reductive group $G$, is naturally equipped with an anti-symplectic involution, and give an explicit formula for this involution in the Rankin-Selberg case.

</details>


### [255] [A second reduction-type formula for the refined Littlewood--Richardson coefficients in type A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23479)
*Siddheswar Kundu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称群S_n中置换w的 refined Littlewood-Richardson系数c^ν_{λ,μ}(w)，建立了其第二类约化公式，扩展了经典Littlewood-Richardson系数的第二约化公式，证明基于hive模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广经典Littlewood-Richardson系数的第二约化公式到带置换参数的 refined 情形。

Method: 利用hive模型进行证明。

Result: 建立了refined Littlewood-Richardson系数c^ν_{λ,μ}(w)的第二类约化公式。

Conclusion: 成功推广了经典Littlewood-Richardson系数的第二约化公式，并在hive模型框架下给出了严格证明。

Abstract: For a permutation $w$ in the symmetric group $S_n$ and partitions $λ, μ, ν$ with at most $n$ parts, the refined Littlewood--Richardson (LR) coefficients $c^ν_{λ,μ}(w)$ in type $A_n$ count the multiplicity of the irreducible polynomial representation $V(ν)$ of the general linear algebra $\mathfrak{gl}_n(\mathbb{C})$ appearing in the decomposition of the Kostant--Kumar submodule $K(λ,w,μ)$ of the tensor product $V(λ) \otimes V(μ)$ of two irreducible polynomial $\mathfrak{gl}_n(\mathbb{C})$-modules. In this paper, we establish a second reduction-type formula for $c^ν_{λ,μ}(w)$, extending the second reduction formula for the classical Littlewood--Richardson coefficients $c^ν_{λ,μ}$. The proof relies on the hive model.

</details>


### [256] [The loop-nilpotent cohomological Hall algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23544)
*Shivang Jindal,Andrei Neguţ*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为带典型三次势的三折箭图建立了环幂零余环上同调代数的显式洗牌代数模型，并由此推导出多个重要结论，包括与量子库仑分支代数的联系、特殊化后的超交换性、生成元描述、BPS李代数的刻画，以及Kac多项式的新公式。同时证明了一个关于局部化洗牌代数的球面生成猜想，并指出对ADE型箭图，该代数同构于Yangian的Drinfeld-Gavarini对偶的正半部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立环幂零CoHA的显式洗牌代数模型，以统一理解带势能的三折箭图的各种结构（如CoHA、库仑分支代数、BPS李代数）并推导新结果。

Method: 通过构造显式的洗牌代数模型来描述环幂零CoHA，并利用该模型进行代数结构分析、特殊化处理、生成元提取及与已知代数结构的比较。

Result: (1) 建立了环幂零CoHA与量子库仑分支代数的联系；(2) 证明了在ℏ=0特殊化后该代数是超交换的；(3) 给出了环幂零CoHA和预投射CoHA的生成元；(4) 通过度和可整除条件刻画了预投射CoHA的BPS李代数；(5) 给出了Kac多项式的新公式；(6) 证明了局部化洗牌代数的球面生成猜想；(7) 对ADE型箭图，该代数同构于Yangian的Drinfeld-Gavarini对偶的正半部分。

Conclusion: 环幂零CoHA的洗牌代数模型是一个强大的统一框架，不仅建立了该领域多个重要结构之间的联系，还产生了新的组合公式和代数刻画。

Abstract: We give an explicit shuffle algebra model for the loop-nilpotent cohomological Hall algebra (CoHA) of a tripled quiver with canonical cubic potential. As consequences, we (1) relate the loop-nilpotent CoHA to the quantized Coulomb branch algebra of the corresponding quiver gauge theory, (2) show that the loop-nilpotent CoHA is supercommutative after specialization at $\hbar=0$, (3) give generators for both the loop-nilpotent CoHA and the full preprojective CoHA, and (4) obtain an explicit characterization of the BPS Lie algebra of the full preprojective CoHA via certain degree and divisibility conditions. This gives a new formula for the Kac polynomials of the quiver in terms of the dimensions of certain vector spaces of polynomials. We also prove a conjecture on the spherical generation of the localized shuffle algebra and show that for ADE quivers, the loop-nilpotent CoHA is the positive half of Drinfeld-Gavarini dual of the Yangian.

</details>


### [257] [On the natural transformations of extension functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23495)
*Abdolnaser Bahlekeh,Shokrollah Salarian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了正合范畴上扩张函子之间的自然变换。主要结果表明：当范畴有足够多投射对象时，从Ext^{n+1}(N,-)到Ext^{n+1}(M,-)的自然变换群同构于一个商群；利用Auslander-Gruson-Jensen对偶，在有限表现左模范畴中该商群同构于Tor函子之间的自然变换。第二个主要结果将该结论推广到n-弗罗贝尼乌斯范畴。这些结果是对Hilton-Rees定理的深远推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正合范畴上扩张函子之间的自然变换，并建立其与Tor函子自然变换之间的联系，从而推广经典的Hilton-Rees定理。

Method: 利用正合范畴中投射对象的性质，构造自然变换群与商群之间的同构；借助Auslander-Gruson-Jensen对偶建立与Tor函子自然变换的联系；引入n-弗罗贝尼乌斯范畴概念，用n-投射对象替代投射对象实现推广。

Result: 1) 在具有足够多投射对象的正合范畴中，Ext^{n+1}(N,-)到Ext^{n+1}(M,-)的自然变换群同构于Ext^n(M, syz^nN)模掉某子群的商群；2) 在有限表现左模范畴中该商群同构于Tor_{n+1}(-,M)到Tor_{n+1}(-,N)的自然变换群；3) 在n-弗罗贝尼乌斯范畴中，用n-投射对象替代投射对象后结论依然成立。

Conclusion: 本文的结果是Hilton-Rees定理的深远推广（n=0时即恢复该定理），将经典结论从模范畴推广到更一般的正合范畴和n-弗罗贝尼乌斯范畴。

Abstract: Assume that $\C$ is an exact category. This paper is concerned with the natural transformations between extension functors on $\C$. The first main result indicates that if $\C$ has enough projective objects, then for any pair of objects $M, N\in \C$ and any non-negative integer $n$, the group of all natural transformations from $\Ext^{n+1}_{\C}(N, -)$ to $\Ext^{n+1}_{\C}(M, -)$ is isomorphic to the quotient group $\Ext^n_{\C}(M, \syz^nN)/{\p}$, where $\p$ is the subgroup consisting of those extensions of length $n$ arising as a push-out along a morphism $P\rt\syz^nN$, with $P$ projective. This, together with the Auslander-Gruson-Jensen duality yields that if $\C$ is the category of all finitely presented left modules over an associative ring $R$, then the quotient group is isomorphic to the natural transformations from $\Tor_{n+1}^R(-, M)$ to $\Tor_{n+1}^R(-, N)$. The second main result proves that if $\C$ is an $n$-Frobenuis category, then the statement of the first result remains true, whenever projectives are replaced by $n$-projectives. This result is fruitful from the point of view that, $n$-Frobenius categories may not have projective objects. These results provide a far-reaching generalization of the Hilton-Rees theorem, in the sense that the case $n=0$, recover the Hilton-Rees theorem.

</details>


### [258] [Irreducibility of the tensor product of Yangian \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \) evaluation modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24334)
*Vyacheslav Futorny,Zheng Li,Jian Zhang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了超杨图 \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \) 在有限维单 \( \mathrm{U}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \)-模上的评估模的张量积的不可约性条件，在权为协变张量或本质典型的情形下给出了充要条件，并得到了协变评估模任意张量积的不可约性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Molev关于经典杨图 \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_n) \) 的著名结果推广到超杨图 \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \) 情形，研究超李代数表示的张量积的不可约性。

Method: 利用评估同态将超杨图作用诱导到 \( \mathrm{U}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \)-模上，借助Gelfand-Tsetlin基的存在性，对协变张量权和本质典型权两类最高权模进行分析，给出张量积简单的充要条件。

Result: 给出了 \( L_g(λ) \otimes L_h(γ) \) 为单模的充要条件（λ, γ为协变张量或本质典型），并利用协变评估模张量积的二元性质得到任意协变评估模张量积的不可约性判据。

Conclusion: 该结果是经典杨图Molev定理在超李代数情形的类比，为超杨图表示论中张量积不可约性问题提供了完整解答。

Abstract: The evaluation homomorphism from the super Yangian \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \) to \( \mathrm{U}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \) induces a \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \)-module structure on any finite dimensional simple \( \mathrm{U}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \)-module \( L(λ) \). In this paper, we give necessary and sufficient conditions for the tensor product of such evaluation \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \)-modules, \( L_g(λ) \otimes L_h(γ) \), to be simple, provided each of \( λ\) and \( γ\) is either covariant tensor or essentially typical. Our proof is based on the existence of a Gelfand--Tsetlin basis for finite dimensional simple \( \mathrm{U}(\mathfrak{gl}_{m|n}) \)-modules with highest weights that belong to these two families: covariant tensor and essentially typical.
  The obtained result is a super analogue of the Molev's result for the classical Yangian \( \mathrm{Y}(\mathfrak{gl}_n) \). Combining this with the binary property of tensor products of covariant evaluation modules, we obtain an irreducibility criterion for arbitrary tensor products of covariant evaluation modules.

</details>


### [259] [Trace radicals and cocenters of free products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24697)
*Nikita Safonkin*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文定义了迹剩余有限维（trace RFD）代数概念，证明了对RFD代数A和B，其自由积A*B的迹根等于A和B迹根的直和，进而A*B是迹RFD当且仅当A和B都是迹RFD。证明结合了显式余中心分解与有限维表示迹构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将剩余有限维（RFD）性质从一般表示层面推广到迹泛函层面，研究自由积代数在迹意义下的RFD性质保持问题。

Method: 定义迹RFD代数（元素被有限维表示分离，余中心被对应迹泛函分离）；证明自由积的迹根等于各因子迹根直和；通过余中心分解与构造检测长度大于1的词的非零类的有限维表示迹来完成证明。

Result: 对RFD代数A和B，A*B的迹根是A与B迹根的直和；A*B是迹RFD当且仅当A和B都是迹RFD。

Conclusion: 自由积运算在迹RFD性质下是封闭的，即两个迹RFD代数的自由积仍为迹RFD，反之亦然。

Abstract: We call a unital associative algebra $A$ trace residually finite-dimensional if its elements are separated by finite-dimensional representations and its cocenter $A/[A,A]$ is separated by the corresponding trace functionals. For RFD algebras $A$ and $B$, we prove that the trace radical of $A*B$, the common kernel of all finite-dimensional trace functionals, is the direct sum of the trace radicals of $A$ and $B$, which implies that $A*B$ is trace RFD if and only if both $A$ and $B$ are trace RFD. The proof combines an explicit cocenter decomposition with a construction of finite-dimensional representations whose traces detect nonzero classes of words of length greater than one.

</details>


### [260] [Efficiency of mutation reduction for Brauer trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23628)
*Zehavit Zvi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Brauer树代数的突变归约算法，提出了Aihara算法的改进版本，并证明所有版本的Aihara算法在到达Brauer星代数所需步骤数上是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brauer树代数是群模表示论中的重要基本模块，Aihara提出的突变归约算法可以从Brauer树代数得到更简单的Brauer星代数，但不同算法版本会产生边的置换，需要系统分析和优化。

Method: 对Aihara的突变归约算法进行修改，并与原始算法进行计算效率对比测试；通过理论分析证明所有版本的Aihara算法在所有可能的完全突变归约算法中，到达Brauer星代数所需的步骤数最少。

Result: 修改后的算法与原始算法相比有计算效率上的提升；理论上证明了Aihara算法的所有版本在步骤数意义上都是最优的。

Conclusion: Aihara算法及其变体在突变归约的步骤数上已达到理论下界，不可能存在更快的完全突变归约算法。

Abstract: Brauer tree algebras are important and fundamental blocks in the modular representation theory of groups. Aihara develped an algorithm, which we call a mutation reduction, for getting from a Brauer tree algebra to the simpler Brauer star algebra using a sequence of mutations centered on edges. Schaps and Zvi, using the Schaps-Zakay theory of pointing the tree, showed that different algorithms for the sequence of mutations give permutations of the edges.
  We give a modification of Aihara's algorithm and tested it against the original algorithm for computational efficiency. We prove that all versions of Aihara's algorithm are the fastest possible in the sense of requiring the least number steps to reach the Brauer star, when compared to all possible complete mutation reduction algorithms.

</details>


### [261] [Coulomb branches, quantized zastavas, Kac polynomials, and shuffle algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24711)
*Dinakar Muthiah,Alex Weekes*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过zastava空间的量子化封闭嵌入，构造了任意箭图的极限量子化zastava代数A（Borel Yangian）及其正部A⁺（单幂Yangian），证明了它们的Hilbert级数满足Kac多项式的Hua公式，并建立了A⁺与shuffle代数的同构，最终证明了Negut关于局部化shuffle代数球面生成的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前构造的Kac-Moody仿射Grassmannian切片的封闭嵌入在一般情况下不能量子化，因此需要寻找一个变体（zastava空间）使得量子化成为可能，进而构造Borel Yangian和单幂Yangian，并证明相关的组合与代数猜想。

Method: 利用Braverman-Finkelberg-Nakajima（BFN）Coulomb分支构造的变体得到zastava空间；通过取极限构造极限量子化zastava代数A和正部A⁺；使用monopole公式的极限得到Hilbert级数；建立A⁺与shuffle代数的同构；结合第二作者关于Coulomb分支生成元的工作证明Negut猜想。

Result: 构造了任意箭图的极限量子化zastava代数A（Borel Yangian）及其正部A⁺（单幂Yangian）；证明了A和A⁺的Hilbert级数由Kac多项式的Hua公式给出；证明了A⁺与某个shuffle代数同构；证明了Negut关于局部化shuffle代数球面生成的猜想。

Conclusion: zastava空间的量子化封闭嵌入存在，并由此得到了Borel Yangian和单幂Yangian的自然构造，建立了与shuffle代数的联系，解决了Negut猜想，为量子群表示论提供了新的代数框架。

Abstract: We previously constructed closed embeddings of Kac-Moody affine Grassmannian slices using fundamental monopole operators. These spaces are defined via the Braverman-Finkelberg-Nakajima construction of Coulomb branches for quiver gauge theories, and the embeddings do not quantize in general. However, there is variant of the BFN construction that produces zastava spaces, and we show that the closed embeddings do quantize in that case. This allows us to take a limit and construct the limit quantized zastava $\mathcal{A}$ for an arbitrary quiver. This algebra plays the role of the Borel Yangian. We also construct a positive part $\mathcal{A}^+$, which plays the role of the unipotent Yangian. By taking the limit of the monopole formula, we show that both $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathcal{A}^+$ have Hilbert series given by a version for Hua's formula for Kac polynomials. We also show that $\mathcal{A}^+$ is isomorphic to a certain shuffle algebra. Finally, using these results we obtain a proof of Negut's conjecture on the spherical generation of localized shuffle algebras via the second author's work on generators of Coulomb branches.

</details>


### [262] [Notes on Chevalley Groups and Root Category IV: Iwasawa Decomposition and Total Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23697)
*Buyan Li*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文从根范畴角度研究了Chevalley群的Cartan分解与Iwasawa分解，进而探讨了Chevalley群中全正子幺半群的Iwasawa分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Chevalley群的经典分解理论（Cartan分解与Iwasawa分解）置于根范畴的框架下重新审视，并进一步将Iwasawa分解推广到全正子幺半群上，以深化对Chevalley群结构及其正性部分的理解。

Method: 采用根范畴（root category）作为理论工具，对Chevalley群进行Cartan分解与Iwasawa分解的构造与证明；随后将方法应用于全正子幺半群，研究其Iwasawa分解的存在性与具体形式。

Result: 在根范畴视角下成功建立了Chevalley群的Cartan分解与Iwasawa分解；并进一步得到了全正子幺半群上的Iwasawa分解。

Conclusion: 根范畴是研究Chevalley群分解理论的合适框架，且全正子幺半群在该框架下同样具有良好的Iwasawa分解性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Cartan decomposition and Iwasawa decomposition of the Chevalley group from the root category. Then we investigate the Iwasawa decomposition for totally positive submonoid of the Chevalley group.

</details>


### [263] [Counterexamples to Norm Conjectures of Wehlau in Modular Invariant Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23857)
*Muhammad Fazeel Anwar*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在特征2域上构造反例，推翻Wehlau关于范数的两个猜想：给出$C_2^3$和$C_2^4$在$\mathbb F_8$上的忠实四维表示，其中所有非线性轨道范数均可分解；特别地，$C_2^4$例子的不变量环为多项式环，同时否证了Wehlau范数猜想及其无限制推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验并反驳Wehlau关于有限群模表示中轨道范数分解性质的猜想，尤其在特征2情形下寻找反例。

Method: 在特征2域$\mathbb F_8$上构造$C_2^3$和$C_2^4$的忠实四维表示，计算并分析所有非线性轨道范数的可分解性。

Result: 成功构造出两个反例，其中所有非线性轨道范数均可分解；$C_2^4$例子的不变量环为多项式环，直接否证了Wehlau范数猜想及其无限制推广版本。

Conclusion: Wehlau关于范数的两个猜想在特征2下不成立，需对猜想条件作进一步修正。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group and let $V$ be a finite-dimensional $G$-module over a field $k$. We construct explicit counterexamples in characteristic $2$ to conjectures of Wehlau concerning norms. We give faithful four-dimensional representations of $C_2^3$ and $C_2^4$ over $\mathbb F_8$ for which every nonlinear orbit norm is decomposable; in the $C_2^4$ example, the invariant ring is polynomial, thereby disproving both Wehlau's norm conjecture and its unrestricted extension.

</details>


### [264] [The proper stable module category of a group algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24178)
*Georgios Dalezios,Juan Omar Gómez*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过余挠对和阿贝尔模型结构，为任意离散群在任意交换环上引入了真稳定模范畴，该范畴是良生成的张量三角范畴，并在交换环为正则时紧生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稳定模范畴（如Mazza-Symonds稳定模范畴）仅适用于有限群或特定环，缺乏对任意离散群和任意交换环的统一处理框架。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用余挠对（cotorsion pairs）和阿贝尔模型结构（abelian model structures）来构造真稳定模范畴，并将该构造与等变稳定同伦理论和已有的稳定模范畴进行对比验证。

Result: 构建了任意离散群在任意交换环上的真稳定模范畴，证明了它是良生成的张量三角范畴，且在环为正则时紧生成。该范畴与Mazza-Symonds稳定模范畴及分层定义群相关的各种稳定范畴一致。

Conclusion: 引入的同调维数与经典同调维数进行了详细比较，为该理论提供了更深入的结构性理解。

Abstract: We introduce the proper stable module category for an arbitrary discrete group over any commutative ring by means of cotorsion pairs and abelian model structures. This category is a well-generated tensor-triangulated category and is compactly generated in case the commutative ring is regular. Our construction resembles the topological approach via proper equivariant stable homotopy theory. Moreover, it agrees with the Mazza-Symonds stable module category, whenever the latter is defined, and with various other stable categories associated to hierarchically defined groups. Along the way, we introduce certain homological dimensions and study them in detail, comparing them with the classical notions.

</details>


### [265] [Beyond the Adams Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23885)
*Alexander Hazeltine,Aarya Kumar,Andrew Tung*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了p-adic域上辛群和偶正交群的局部Arthur包络计数问题，确定了在上升塔中首次出现时，一个温表示（tempered representation）的局部theta提升所包含的局部Arthur包络的数量。结果表明，局部theta提升可能存在于比Adams猜想预测多得多的局部Arthur包络中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局部theta提升与局部Arthur包络之间的包含关系，检验并扩展Adams猜想的适用范围。

Method: 通过理论分析和计数方法，研究辛群和偶正交群上温表示的局部theta提升在首次出现时所属的局部Arthur包络数量。

Result: 局部theta提升可能存在于比Adams猜想所预测的更多的局部Arthur包络中。

Conclusion: Adams猜想对于局部theta提升所属Arthur包络的预测是不充分的，实际的包含关系更为丰富。

Abstract: For symplectic and even orthogonal groups over a $p$-adic field, we determine the number of local Arthur packets containing the local theta lift of a tempered representation at the first occurrence in the going-up tower. These counts show that the local theta lifts may lie in many more local Arthur packets than those predicted by the Adams conjecture.

</details>


### [266] [On Representations of Weyl Groups attached to Spherical Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24108)
*Guy Kapon,Guy Shtotland*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为任意光滑球面G-簇X构造了Weyl群的两个表示：一个基于Borel-Moore同调，另一个基于Borel轨道的组合结构。通过Fourier-Sato变换证明二者同构，并将相对Langlands纲领中的Finkelberg-Ginzburg-Travkin猜想转化为组合问题，在若干实例中解决了该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立光滑球面G-簇上Weyl群表示的几何与组合两种构造之间的同构关系，从而为相对Langlands纲领中的相关猜想提供新的研究工具。

Method: 利用Borel-Moore同调构造几何表示，利用Borel轨道的组合结构构造组合表示，再通过Fourier-Sato变换证明二者同构，进而将猜想转化为组合问题。

Result: 证明了两种Weyl群表示的同构性，并在余切情形下将Finkelberg-Ginzburg-Travkin猜想转化为一个可解决的组合问题，在若干例子中完成了求解。

Conclusion: 该同构为相对Langlands纲领中相关猜想的解决提供了桥梁，且组合化方法在实例中有效。

Abstract: To any smooth spherical $G$ variety $X$ we attach two representations of the Weyl group of $G$. The first is constructed geometrically using Borel Moore homology. The second is constructed combinatorially using the structure of the Borel orbits on $X$. Using the Fourier Sato transform, we show that both representations are isomorphic. We use this result to translate (in the cotangent case) a conjecture of Finkelberg-Ginzburg-Travkin in the relative Langlands program to a combinatorial problem. We solve this combinatorial problem for several examples.

</details>


### [267] [Simple modules for affine nilCoxeter algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24247)
*David J. Benson,Kay Jin Lim*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了仿射nilCoxeter代数A（型Ã_{n-1}）的表示论，证明了它是Noetherian素PI代数，PI次数为n!，从而所有单A模有限维且最大维数为n!。通过研究交换子代数C及其对称化R，证明了R同构于k[X_1,...,X_{n-1}]^{μ_n}，是Cohen-Macaulay环，Gorenstein当且仅当n为奇数或n=2，且为toric环，除子类群为Z/n，所有投射R模自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射nilCoxeter代数A（型Ã_{n-1}）的表示论，刻画其代数结构和单模性质。

Method: 通过研究与A相关的大型交换子代数C，分析其关联素理想和对称化中心R的结构，利用有限群作用、toric环和Cohen-Macaulay环理论。

Result: A是Noetherian素PI代数，PI次数为n!；所有单A模有限维，最大维数为n!；C有n!个极小素理想且被对称群正则置换；R同构于k[X_1,...,X_{n-1}]^{μ_n}，是Cohen-Macaulay环，Gorenstein当且仅当n为奇数或n=2，是toric环，除子类群Z/n，所有投射R模自由。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了仿射nilCoxeter代数的代数结构和表示论性质，揭示了其与有限群作用、toric几何的深层联系。

Abstract: We study the representation theory of the affine nilCoxeter algebra $A$ of type $\tilde A_{n-1}$, over a field $k$ of any characteristic. Our main theorem states that this is a Noetherian prime affine PI algebra of PI degree $n!$. As a consequence, the simple $A$-modules are all finite dimensional, and the maximum dimension of a simple module is $n!$ over a suitable finite extension of $k$.
  To achieve this, we investigate a large commutative subalgebra $C$ which is finitely generated as an algebra and over which $A$ is finitely generated as a module. We show that the associated primes of $C$ are minimal primes, and there are $n!$ of them, regularly permuted by $\mathfrak{S}_n$. The algebra $R=C^{\mathfrak{S}_n}$ is equal to the centre of $A$, and isomorphic to $C/\mathfrak{p}$ for each of the minimal primes $\mathfrak{p}$.
  We prove that the ring $R$ is isomorphic to $k[X_1,\dots,X_{n-1}]^{μ_n}$, where $μ_n$ is the finite group scheme of $n$th roots of unity, acting so that $X_i$ has degree $i$ modulo $n$. The ring $R$ is Cohen--Macaulay, and is Gorenstein if and only if $n$ is odd or $n=2$. It is a toric ring, with divisor class group $\mathsf{Cl}(R)\cong\mathbb{Z}/n$, and every projective $R$-module is free.

</details>


### [268] [Infinitely Many Components in Auslander--Reiten Quivers of Representation-Infinite Algebras over Perfect Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24466)
*Wen Chang,Quanyu Tang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于完美域上的有限维表示无限型代数，其Auslander-Reiten箭图必然有无穷多个连通分支，从而证实了Auslander、Reiten和Smalø关于Artin代数的猜想在完美域情形成立。证明分两步：代数闭域情形通过局部多项式表示嵌入与域自同构诱导的半线性扭结实现；一般完美域情形通过可分基变换归约到代数闭域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Auslander、Reiten和Smalø提出的猜想：表示无限型Artin代数的Auslander-Reiten箭图具有无穷多个连通分支。该猜想在完美域上的有限维代数情形此前尚未解决。

Method: 1) 代数闭域情形：结合局部多项式表示嵌入与域自同构诱导的半线性扭结；2) 一般完美域情形：利用可分基变换，证明若原代数的AR箭图仅有有限分支，则其代数闭包上的标量扩张亦然，从而归约到代数闭域情形。

Result: 完美域上的表示无限型有限维代数的Auslander-Reiten箭图必有无穷多个连通分支。

Conclusion: 成功证明了Auslander-Reiten-Smalø猜想在完美域上有限维代数情形成立，并将代数闭域上的证明通过可分基变换推广到完美域。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a perfect field and let $A$ be a representation-infinite finite-dimensional $k$-algebra. We prove that the Auslander--Reiten quiver of $A$ has infinitely many connected components. This establishes, for finite-dimensional algebras over perfect fields, a conjecture of Auslander, Reiten, and Smalø concerning Artin algebras. Over an algebraically closed field, the proof combines a localized polynomial representation embedding with semilinear twists induced by field automorphisms. The passage from a perfect field to its algebraic closure is obtained by separable base change: we prove that if the Auslander--Reiten quiver of $A$ has only finitely many components, then the same holds for the scalar extension to the algebraic closure.

</details>
